### 2026-11-09T02:38:47Z [**NekoApocalypse/Vibe99**](https://github.com/NekoApocalypse/Vibe99)

```
Fixes terminal clicks on supported preview file paths (Markdown, PDF and raster images) and HTTP(S) URLs across line breaks:  - Recognizes agy-style word wrapping after hyphens and path separators, preserving the complete target. Supports indented continuation lines and paragraphs containing multiple paths. - Recognizes hard wraps at the terminal margin when a CLI/tmux redraw loses the normal wrap marker. - Clicking either fragment opens the complete file or URL without forwarding mouse reports to the TUI. Shift selection and inactive-pane activation rules remain intact. - Invalidates a cached wrapped link when only one of its rows changes, preventing stale destinations after agent updates.  Verified against the reported agy sample, real pointer tests in both xterm bundles, and the existing terminal-link and pane interaction suites. Reconstruction is bounded and uses margin/separator cues; arbitrary unrelated line breaks are not joined.  Windows packages remain unsigned while code-signing enrollment is pending.  
```

### 2026-11-07T08:56:09Z [**egoist/mygo**](https://github.com/egoist/mygo)

```
## Changes in behavior  No breaking changes to the Go API. Two changes code may notice:  - **Markdown's default parser reads GitHub mentions and references.** `parser.Default` now includes alerts, mentions and issue references, so code that walks a document's inlines gets `Mention` and `Reference` inlines where it used to get `Text`. Their `Text` field holds the text as written, and `parser.PlainText` and the HTML writer handle them. To keep the old output, parse with `parser.Default &^ (parser.ExtMentions | parser.ExtReferences)`. (#1423) - **Generated TypeScript types named `Record` or `Promise` get their package's prefix,** such as `ModelsRecord`, so they no longer hide TypeScript's built-in `Record` and `Promise` and the client compiles. Code importing those types uses the new names after regenerating. JSON fields and method names are unchanged. (#1421, @HiPanJin)  ## Highlights  - **GitHub alerts, mentions and issue references in Markdown.** `> [!NOTE]`, `[!TIP]`, `[!IMPORTANT]`, `[!WARNING]` and `[!CAUTION]` render as GitHub's alerts with their icons and colors, also while a model's answer is still streaming in, so a chat app never shows the marker as text. `@octocat` and `#123`, `GH-123` or `octocat/hello#123` are read as GitHub reads them. With `Options.Resolve`, an issue or PR viewer turns them into links that open the person or the PR in place, and `Options.Preview` shows a hover card on any link, mention or reference. `Theme.Alerts` holds the alert colors. (#1423, @brodienguyen) - **Korean text breaks lines between words.** `Text`, `TextInput` and `TextArea` keep Korean words whole and break at spaces, as CSS's `word-break: keep-all` does, whenever the text's language is Korean (from the app's language or a span's `Lang`). A word longer than the line still breaks. (#1405, requested by @yldst-dev in #1403, who wrote the patch it builds on)  ## New  - `Page.GPUProcessID()` gives the process that does a page's graphics and media, so a music player can capture a web player's sound for a visualizer with a Core Audio process tap on macOS. On Windows it is WebView2's GPU process, and on Linux it is 0. (#1401, requested by @fzn0x in #1397)  ## Fixes  ### UI and apps - Space in an element that takes text itself (`HandleTextInput` or `TextCaret`) inside a scroll view types a space without paging the view. (#1411, reported by @tacshi in #1410) - In a split diff, a hunk's header stays on the old side and ends in an ellipsis before the divider, however far the divider is dragged. (#1415, reported by @yangdev02 in #1413) - `Tester.RightClick` on a list row opens the context menu that the row's content carries. (#1416, reported by @yangdev02 in #1414)  ### Graphics - A clip inside a rounded, clipped view, such as a column filling the bottom of a rounded card, keeps to the card's rounded corners with Metal, Direct3D and OpenGL. (#1420, reported by @Mrhoho in #1418) - A card turned over in perspective far from the window's top left stays visible, and clips inside it keep to its size. (#1409, reported by @fzn0x in #1406) - A rounded view that grows over an animation, such as a card opening out of a cover until it fills the window, keeps one mask instead of allocating a new one each frame, so opening it no longer adds a couple of hundred MB of memory. (#1428) - Idle clip masks keep within their 32 MB memory budget. (#1408, reported by @fzn0x in #1407)  ### macOS - With the Korean 2-Set input source, pressing Return while a syllable is composing commits it and also reaches the app, so a chat box sends on the first press. (#1404, reported by @yldst-dev in #1402)  ### Windows - Animations that change only every other frame free their idle textures. (#1394)  ### Linux - `Window.SetIgnoreMouseEvents(true)` lets clicks through to what is under the window, as on macOS and Windows, for overlays such as desktop lyrics. (#1419, @r0k1s-i)  ### Updates - Apps built with this version or later remove the files that a newer version no longer ships when they update on Linux and Windows. (#1400, reported by @fzn0x in #1396)  ## New Contributors  - @r0k1s-i made their first contribution in #1419 - @brodienguyen made their first contribution in #1423  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/egoist/mygo/compare/v0.8.1...v0.8.3 
```

### 2026-11-11T05:23:50Z [**DIYgod/pinky**](https://github.com/DIYgod/pinky)

```
- On a Mac with Xcode, Pinky can now install its helper on an older iPhone from its window (the phone's page, its checkup, and Add phone › Try an older iPhone), and install it again when its signature is about to run out, showing each step, asking you to unlock the phone when needed, and saying why when it fails. - An iPhone on iOS 27 that Pinky can't show because an app on it is using the camera or microphone (during a call, for example) now says so on its page and in the sidebar, with what to do, and connects by itself once they're free. - Agents can no longer uninstall or stop the Pinky helper on an iPhone, whatever its bundle ID.  ---  ### 中文  - 在装有 Xcode 的 Mac 上，现在可以直接在 Pinky 窗口里给旧款 iPhone 装辅助应用（手机页面、「体检」和「添加手机 › 连接旧款 iPhone」里点「安装辅助应用」），签名快到期时点「重新安装」即可；安装时会显示每一步，需要时提醒你解锁手机，失败时会说明原因。 - iOS 27 的 iPhone 上有 App 正在使用相机或麦克风（比如通话中）、Pinky 无法显示它的屏幕时，手机页面和侧栏现在会说明原因和处理方法，相机和麦克风空出来后 Pinky 会自动连接。 - 智能体不能再卸载或停止 iPhone 上的 Pinky 辅助应用，不管它的 bundle ID 是什么。  ---  **macOS:** `brew install --cask diygod/tap/pinky`, or the `.dmg` for your Mac below (`arm64` for Apple silicon, `amd64` for Intel). **Windows:** `pinky-setup-2026.1111.13-amd64.exe`. **Linux:** `pinky_2026.1111.13_amd64.deb`, or `pinky-2026.1111.13-linux-amd64.tar.gz`. **Claude Desktop:** with Pinky installed, open `pinky-2026.1111.13.mcpb` to add Pinky to Claude Desktop as an extension; Settings › Agents in Pinky connects Claude Code, Codex, Cursor and others with a click. 
```

### 2026-11-10T23:37:39Z [**Anderbone/terminal-spike**](https://github.com/Anderbone/terminal-spike)

```
Customize the input-mode keyboard with a visual editor: add, replace, remove, and drag accessory keys or the input field. The live bar and editor share layout geometry, while the shortcut editor uses the terminal's button icons and colors.  System keyboard input now uses pending one-shot Ctrl, Alt, and Shift modifiers without changing the remainder of an IME text batch. Input layouts persist across app restarts and portable backup/restore; older backups preserve current input-layout preferences.  This release includes all remaining reviewed local keyboard changes. The release APK, stable Latest APK, complete Corresponding Source archive and SHA-256 checksums are attached. Google Play public availability may follow submission after Google review and propagation. 
```

```
OpenIDEA 0.1.40 adds an opt-in built-in MCP server, Ruby debugging, .NET workflows, Docker dashboards in Services, attached projects and a shared Java language server across builds, makes switching editor tabs and long agent conversations faster, and ships Plugin API 1.4.  ### Download  | Architecture | Windows | macOS | Linux | |---|---|---|---| | x86-64 (64-bit) | | | AArch64 (ARM64) | [EXE](https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/releases/download/v0.1.40/OpenIDEA_0.1.40_arm64-setup.exe) &nbsp; Silicon](https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/releases/download/v0.1.40/OpenIDEA_0.1.40_aarch64.dmg) | [AppImage](https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/releases/download/v0.1.40/OpenIDEA_0.1.40_aarch64.AppImage) &nbsp; [DEB](https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/releases/download/v0.1.40/OpenIDEA_0.1.40_arm64.deb) &nbsp; [RPM](https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/releases/download/v0.1.40/OpenIDEA-0.1.40-1.aarch64.rpm) |  **Remote development:** `openidea-server` for [Linux x86-64](https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/releases/download/v0.1.40/openidea-server-linux-x86_64.gz) · [Linux ARM64](https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/releases/download/v0.1.40/openidea-server-linux-aarch64.gz) · [macOS x86-64](https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/releases/download/v0.1.40/openidea-server-macos-x86_64.gz) · [macOS ARM64](https://github.com/l0ng-ai/openidea/releases/download/v0.1.40/openidea-server-macos-aarch64.gz), each with a `.sha256` file.  **Already installed?** OpenIDEA updates itself: use **Check for Updates**.  ## What's new  ### AI and MCP - **Built-in MCP server (opt-in):** agents can read, search and edit files through the IDE's documents (keeping unsaved changes and undo), and run or build after you allow it in the project window; ACP agents get it automatically while it is on. - Agents: the model list and thinking level share one popover, and Claude's models, effort levels and commands are ready before the first task. - Long conversations switch tabs quickly and keep following the newest message until you scroll away. - Codex skills appear in command completion, and composer commands and skills are grouped.  ### Plugins (Preview) - **Plugin API 1.4** (`openidea:plugin@1.4.0`); plugins built for earlier 1.x versions keep loading. - Workbench and editor: status items, Output channels, content tabs, decorations, tree badges, search, Problems, VCS reads and new menu locations. - Runs and tests: plugin-owned run lifecycles, keyed terminals, run targets that also host language servers and debugging, dynamic configurations, test parsers and rerun selection. - Language features: formatters, signature help, lenses, links, symbols and colours, and language servers inside plugin-injected ranges. - Plugins can own database sources and add tools to the built-in MCP server; new permissions cover plain HTTP, local sockets and IDE commands. - Indexer gutter markers carry their plugin's icon, and a plugin's Homepage is a link.  ### Run and debug - **Debug Ruby** programs and tests with rdbg. - Run All, Restart All, Stop All and Open in Browser on a run-configuration type group; a grouped **Add Service** menu and configuration picker. - Before-launch **Build Module** and **Build Artifacts**, shared Java launch preparation with incremental build policies, and Tomcat skips the build when the WAR is up to date. - Import JetBrains **Go Application** and **Go Test** run configurations. - Find in every console, and clickable file and URL links in the debug console. - Hot Swap: Reload Changed Classes can be cancelled, and reloading while a changed method is on the stack is reported as a reload with old frames still running, not as a failure. - Gradle debugs forked JVMs on a dynamic JDWP port; Maven supports a toolchains file and active profiles, and Maven test forks get a fixed JDWP port. - Debug adapters start more reliably (no port races, no launch deadline, shared breakpoint updates).  ### Languages and editor - Java: one JDT LS can serve all builds in a project window; projects with only a JDT `.classpath` list their libraries and JDK; Find Usages and usage counts are faster in large projects. - **.NET:** workspace, NuGet and test workflows. - Missing build tools, SDKs and debuggers now lead somewhere: Maven/Gradle errors open their settings, an incompatible Gradle JVM points to Choose Gradle JVM, and tool installs (Delve, gopls, npm, gem and pip tools) use the project's selected SDK. - Highlighting: tags in doc comments, Kotlin declarations, Go semantic colours, SCSS in Vue `<style lang="scss">`, HTTP request lines with JSON bodies; colour schemes' semantic token styles apply. - Switching tabs keeps recently used editors alive: inlay hints and code lenses no longer pop in after each switch. - Configurable caret mouse gestures, and editor tooltips no longer hide behind panels. - Vim: a Space or comma `mapleader` works, and special-key names are accepted in any case.  ### Database - Procedures and functions get their own folders in the schema tree. - Bundled SQLite updated to 3.54.1 (RUSTSEC-2026-0459).  ### Docker - Services dashboards for Docker with image layers, container processes, view filters and logs in editor tabs.  ### Version control - Multi-repository Push lets you pick each repository's remote and target branch. - Soft-wrap in the diff viewer, remembered diff window size, and ⌘↓ jumps to source on macOS. - Clearer branch sync indicators and line-ending badges in diffs; reorder Git Log columns by dragging. - Edit the whole commit message, delete every selected branch, and remove the worktree holding a branch you delete.  ### Desktop - **Attach projects** to the current project window, and find projects under a chosen folder. - Install the `openidea` command on macOS, Windows and Linux. - The IDE checks for updates while it stays open. - View › Appearance toggles the toolbar, status bar and tool window bars; Increase/Decrease Font Size applies to editors, terminals and web views. - Installed JetBrains Mono and Inter fonts are preferred over the bundled ones. - Terminal: smoother scrolling and pasting, Windows ConPTY compatibility, and Shift+Enter sends Esc+Return. - Windows: choose the installation scope; loose files open in the focused project window.  <details> <summary>All changes (150 pull requests and commits)</summary>  * feat(app): install the openidea command on macOS, Windows and Linux by @l0ng-ai in <!-- msime-download-guide --> ## 下载  大多数人下载第一行的**水杉输入法**（`msime-macos-0.59.3-universal.pkg`）就行：它是完整版，包含全部输入方案。其他版本只带其中一部分方案，可以和完整版同时安装。每个安装包都同时支持 Apple 芯片和 Intel 芯片的 Mac。  | 版本 | 安装包 | Homebrew | | --- | --- | --- | | **水杉输入法**（完整版，推荐） | [msime-macos-0.59.3-universal.pkg](https://github.com/metasequoiaime/msime/releases/download/macos-v0.59.3/msime-macos-0.59.3-universal.pkg) | `brew install --cask metasequoiaime/tap/msime` | | 水杉五笔 | [msime-macos-wubi-0.59.3-universal.pkg](https://github.com/metasequoiaime/msime/releases/download/macos-v0.59.3/msime-macos-wubi-0.59.3-universal.pkg) | `brew install --cask metasequoiaime/tap/msime-wubi` | | 水杉拼音 | [msime-macos-pinyin-0.59.3-universal.pkg](https://github.com/metasequoiaime/msime/releases/download/macos-v0.59.3/msime-macos-pinyin-0.59.3-universal.pkg) | `brew install --cask metasequoiaime/tap/msime-pinyin` | | 水杉日语 | [msime-macos-japanese-0.59.3-universal.pkg](https://github.com/metasequoiaime/msime/releases/download/macos-v0.59.3/msime-macos-japanese-0.59.3-universal.pkg) | `brew install --cask metasequoiaime/tap/msime-japanese` | | 水杉越南语 | [msime-macos-vietnamese-0.59.3-universal.pkg](https://github.com/metasequoiaime/msime/releases/download/macos-v0.59.3/msime-macos-vietnamese-0.59.3-universal.pkg) | `brew install --cask metasequoiaime/tap/msime-vietnamese` | | 水杉藏文 | [msime-macos-tibetan-0.59.3-universal.pkg](https://github.com/metasequoiaime/msime/releases/download/macos-v0.59.3/msime-macos-tibetan-0.59.3-universal.pkg) | `brew install --cask metasequoiaime/tap/msime-tibetan` |  双击下载的安装包，按提示安装（需要管理员密码）。装好后应用会自动打开，点「立即安装」；第一次在这台 Mac 上安装时，请退出登录再重新登录，然后到「系统设置」→「键盘」→「文字输入」→「输入法」中添加。`SHA256SUMS` 是这些安装包的 SHA-256 校验值。 <!-- /msime-download-guide -->  ## v0.59.3 更新内容  ### ✨ 新功能  **输入**  - 粤拼（含粤语双拼）新增简体输出开关。输入菜单和悬浮工具栏里可以直接切换，菜单标题显示「简体输出」。默认关闭，不影响普通方案的简繁设置。(#11219，相关 #10435)  **设置与界面**  - 「我的插件」列表可以直接启用或停用指令表、短语表，也可以拖动卡片调整顺序。启用数量仍遵守原有上限，脚本插件继续在授权页控制。调整后的顺序只影响设置页的展示，不影响插件的加载和运行，并且会通过账号设置同步到其他桌面设备。(#11222, #11238，修复 #11076) - 账号设置同步新增 macOS 候选窗口设置，包括主题、浅色和深色皮肤、布局、字号和字体、缩放、不透明度、圆角、边距、候选项间距、显示开关和候选颜色。同步时会逐项校验，遇到非法值只跳过那一项。候选皮肤包本身仍需在各设备上单独下载。(#11229，修复 #11081)  ### 🐛 问题修复  **输入**  - 修复 Caps Lock 轻点后只切换了大写锁定、没有切换中英文的问题。(#11246，修复 #10845) - 小鹤双拼在英文状态下按 Shift 切回中文后，方案不再被误切成全拼。之后在输入菜单里主动选择「中」，仍然会切到全拼。(#11252，修复 #11245) - 在「中英模式」里把默认模式设为英文后，从别的输入源切回水杉、重装、更新或重启后的第一次使用，不再被改成中文。之前这样还会顶掉该应用已有的英文规则。(#11221) - 长串全拼中间夹着单字母声母缩写时，也能生成整句候选，比如 `mu'qian'yu'dao'de'w't's` 得到「目前遇到的问题是」。(#11215，修复 #11171)  **候选窗口与设置页**  - 竖排候选项之间使用固定的 6pt 间距，并随候选窗口缩放，不再沿用皮肤或用户设置的候选项间距。横排候选项保持原样。(#11211，修复 #11073) - 设置页的主题轮播限制了皮肤预览高度。之前皮肤装饰区较高时，页面会被撑出大块空白，把分页和后面的设置挤出视口。真实候选窗仍按皮肤本身的尺寸绘制。(#11260)  **运行环境**  - **修复输入模式「用着用着变灰」的问题**：输入法进程的所有退出路径现在都以 SIGKILL 结束，系统不再累计异常退出次数，也就不会把水杉封禁。之前被封后，输入菜单里的输入模式会变灰、选不了，修复输入源、重装和升级都不恢复。设置应用在安装、更新和修复时结束输入法进程，也不再计入次数。(#11231)  ### ⚠️ 升级须知  - **之前已经变灰的输入模式，升级后不会自动恢复。** 被系统封禁的状态保存在各个应用进程里，需要注销重新登录或重启电脑一次。之后不会再出现这个问题。  ### 🙏 感谢  - 问题反馈：#10845、#11073、#11076、#11081、#11171、#11245 的各位报告者  <!-- msime-build {"run":42839306614,"source":"65897dd2c5ad6e53f56189ab0237110eefff7bef","channel":"stable"} --> 
```

### 2026-11-08T18:35:37Z [**t8y2/motty**](https://github.com/t8y2/motty)

```
## 新功能  - 终端标签的标题可以选择显示连接名、终端标题或当前路径，手动改过的名字优先。 - 新增内置终端配色方案，深色和浅色外观各自记住所选方案，并支持搜索。 - 代码片段选择器的分组默认折叠，搜索仍能找到折叠分组里的片段；列表下方的命令框可以把一行命令发送到当前终端、所有已连接终端或勾选的终端，可选是否回车。 - AI 助手的 Agent 模式可以选择本地文件上传到 SSH 主机；超过附件读取上限的文件会提示改用上传按钮。 - 主机表单里新增跳板路由入口，跳板路由和端口转发里的主机选择器可以按名称、地址、账号、分组和标签搜索。 - 已保存的主机可以从标签菜单里直接编辑。 - 延迟读数可以停靠到工具栏，位置和顺序会保存。 - 手动输入的会话名可以和其他标签重名，AI 助手的 @ 引用仍然各自指向唯一的终端。 - 串口主机有了自己的图标，切换到串口协议时自动选中。 - SFTP 支持多选后一次删除。  ## 改进  - 分组的竖向标签改用统一的树键盘导航，上下键在两端不再循环。 - 「代理」页改名为「跳板路由」，主机里的「代理转发」改为「出站代理」，不再和跳板路由混淆。 - Windows 上悬停托盘图标会显示产品名和版本。 - 右键复制/粘贴模式下，有选中文字时右键复制，没有选中时粘贴，Shift+右键打开菜单。  ## 修复  - 从文件备份恢复时，自定义快捷键也会一并恢复。 - 远程桌面支持 RealVNC 的 RSA-AES 认证。 - 独立 MCP 服务只在开放传输工具时才在 `sftp_read` 里提示 `sftp_download`，并说明如何开启传输。 - 在已经停止应答的网络上取消上传不再卡住。  ## 安装  | 平台 | 包 | | --- | --- | | macOS（arm64、amd64） | `.dmg`、`.zip` | | Windows（amd64、arm64） | 安装程序、便携版 zip（数据在程序旁的 `motty-data`） | | Linux（amd64、arm64） | `.deb`、`.tar.gz` |   Built against vivaui/viva `50e68bedfb23c59e00725fcb26520158d492e0f7`. 
```

### 2026-11-07T09:47:09Z [**Binaryify/MyGit**](https://github.com/Binaryify/MyGit)

```
 - 升级到 MyGo 0.8.3：并排对比时，每处改动的标题始终留在左边，内容过长就以省略号收尾，分隔线拖到哪里都不会跑到右边（[egoist/mygo#1415](https://github.com/egoist/mygo/pull/1415)，感谢 [@yangdev02](https://github.com/yangdev02) 的报告）；列表里的一行右键时打开的是这一行自己的菜单 - Windows 上动画只隔一帧变化时，用过的贴图也会及时释放，开着动画的程序不再一直涨内存（[egoist/mygo#1394](https://github.com/egoist/mygo/pull/1394)） - 程序自我更新后会删掉新版本不再带的文件，Windows 和 Linux 上不再残留上一版留下的文件（[egoist/mygo#1400](https://github.com/egoist/mygo/pull/1400)） - 圆角卡片里的内容在缩放、裁剪时贴合圆角，Metal、Direct3D 和 OpenGL 下都不会露到角外；从封面展开成整个窗口的动画不再每帧分配新的遮罩，打开时不再多占几百 MB 内存（[egoist/mygo#1420](https://github.com/egoist/mygo/pull/1420)、[egoist/mygo#1428](https://github.com/egoist/mygo/pull/1428)）
```

### 2026-11-09T10:38:59Z [**since2006/shell-rs**](https://github.com/since2006/shell-rs)

```
### Added  - A menu bar on macOS, as in other Mac apps: ⌘H hides ShellRS, ⌥⌘H hides other apps and ⌘M minimizes the window. About ShellRS shows the version and a link to the website. ([#88](https://github.com/since2006/shell-rs/issues/88)) - The compose pane remembers the commands you send. Press ↑ and ↓ to go back through them as in a terminal, up to the last 500. - Snippets are in the right sidebar next to local terminals too. Click one to type it into the local terminal.  ### Improved  - Snippet categories nest like host groups, and you can drag snippets and categories to reorder them. The list shows only names; hover over a snippet to see its command. Order and nesting sync with cloud sync; ShellRS 0.1.17 and earlier show every category at the top level, but don’t change the order or nesting elsewhere. - On Windows and Linux, Close tab now defaults to Ctrl+Shift+W instead of Ctrl+W, as in Windows Terminal, so it works in a terminal too. Ctrl+W still goes to the shell. If you changed this shortcut, yours stays. - The Follow switch in the right sidebar’s Files is remembered per host: Files opened for that host later, also after restarting ShellRS, start the way you last set it. - The transfer queue scrolls to a new batch when it’s added. 
```

```
## Features  - In a project's Skills menu, skills are now grouped by source. Selecting the heading for a group selects or deselects all skills within that group.  ### Install  Download from [usemagpie.ai](https://usemagpie.ai), or `curl -fsSL https://usemagpie.ai/install.sh | sh`. An installed magpie updates itself.  - **macOS (Apple Silicon):** `magpie-darwin-arm64.dmg` - **macOS (Intel):** `magpie-darwin-amd64.dmg` - **Windows:** `magpie-windows-amd64.exe` (ARM: `magpie-windows-arm64.exe`) - **Linux:** `magpie-linux-amd64` / `magpie-linux-arm64` (needs GTK 3 and WebKitGTK 4.1) - **Terminal only:** `magpie-cli-<os>-<arch>`  Thanks to Elorynth, whose reports and code went into this release. Everyone who helped: [contributors](https://usemagpie.ai/docs/contributors).  <!-- lang:zh -->  ## Features  - 在项目的 Skills 菜单中，技能现在按来源分组。选择某个分组的标题会选中或取消选中该分组内的所有技能。  ### 安装  从 [usemagpie.ai](https://usemagpie.ai) 下载，或运行 `curl -fsSL https://usemagpie.ai/install.sh | sh`。已安装的 magpie 会自动更新。  感谢 Elorynth：这次更新里有你的反馈与代码。所有贡献者见[贡献墙](https://usemagpie.ai/docs/zh/contributors)。    --- The same release, and the feed magpie updates from: https://github.com/yetone/magpie-releases/releases/tag/v0.1.3697
```

### 2026-11-09T07:36:12Z [**nyakang/nyaterm**](https://github.com/nyakang/nyaterm)

```
## NyaTerm v2.0.4  本次正式版本重点改进已保存连接的批量管理和 MobaXterm 会话导入，同时修复终端命令补全、Telnet 协商、命令高亮和多窗口解锁中的问题，并优化云同步本地数据库的空间使用。还对主题配色、连接列表与若干设置界面进行了细节调整。  ### ✨ 新增功能  - **连接多选与批量编辑**：在已保存连接列表中，通过复选框、表头全选、Ctrl/Command 增选与 Shift 范围选择批量选中连接，并查看选中数量或清除选择。支持按协议批量修改适用配置；每个字段可选择保持不变、设置或清空，混合值和不适用字段有明确提示。跨协议批量编辑前会显示确认信息，避免误改认证或协议专属设置。（PR #1190，#536） - **更完整的 MobaXterm 会话导入**：除 SSH 外，新增对 RDP、Telnet、VNC 和 Serial 会话的解析；保留书签分组层级、空文件夹与原始顺序。导入窗口可分别选择或拖入会话文件和可选的密码文件，完成后提示跳过的条目及使用默认值的配置。对于无法可靠还原的协议参数，仍采用 NyaTerm 默认值，而非假定完全兼容。（PR #1190）  ### 🐛 问题修复  - **终端命令建议**：恢复完整历史命令与快捷命令的候选、模糊匹配和整条命令填充；填充只更新当前输入，不会自动执行，仍需用户确认后按 Enter。多行候选会进入粘贴预览，避免未经确认直接发送多行内容。 - **Telnet 窗口尺寸协商**：仅在对端接受 NAWS 协商后发送终端尺寸，避免不支持该能力的设备把尺寸字节显示为乱码；同时处理协商前的尺寸变化和拒绝协商的情况。（#1203，PR #1205） - **多行快捷命令登录**：修正 `\r\p` 暂停后的编辑器换行被额外作为回车发送的问题，减少设备登录或多级登录时的意外空输入；保留显式换行指令的行为。（#1203，PR #1205） - **命令输出与关键字高亮**：修复“应用设置”后命令输出高亮未正确保存、恢复设置后已打开终端未同步更新的问题；完整显示内置规则并理顺依赖开关的禁用状态。恢复旧版浅色和深色终端关键字配色，并使设置页色块使用终端实际生效的调色板。 - **Shell 集成历史记录**：修复 zsh 集成脚本可能干扰持久命令历史的问题，使注入过程使用独立的临时历史环境。 - **多窗口解锁**：修复密码验证结果可能返回错误窗口的问题，确保子窗口中的解锁提示和重试结果回到发起操作的窗口。 - **连接批量保存**：完善跨协议字段适用性、RDP 依赖选项与保存结果统计；区分已更新、未变化及跳过字段，连接数据在同一事务内提交，减少部分更新造成的不一致。  ### ⚡ 性能与稳定性优化  - **云同步本地副本空间管理**：本地副本改用压缩存储，并对已安全应用的编辑日志进行清理；在满足空闲空间条件时执行数据库压缩回收。针对共享编辑日志、损坏记录、迁移中断与密钥轮换补充保护，后台维护不会阻塞正常启动。**云端同步数据格式、备份格式与现有凭据加密方式不变，旧本地记录仍可读取**。（PR #1199） - **存储维护调度**：将一次性迁移、日志清理和空间回收安排在启动后的空闲阶段执行，避免与界面初始化和用户操作争用资源。  ### ♻️ 重构与优化  - 整理桌面端连接编辑、会话状态、终端帧处理、文件传输和设置等模块，提取共用逻辑与边界清晰的子模块；属于内部代码维护，不改变对应功能的使用方式。  ### 🎨 界面与交互优化  - 连接列表完善奇偶行底色、多选状态和固定操作列的视觉一致性；默认 RDP 与 VNC 连接图标更加容易区分。 - 统一浅色主题输入框的背景色和深色主题输入控件的配色，调整锁屏卡片和同步分组浮层的主题表现。 - 安全设置增加更明确的功能图标，优化选项卡布局和关闭交互；隧道分组的批量操作收纳到更多操作菜单。 - 移除云同步页面中不再适用的版本切换入口，保留冲突处理中的字段合并预览与确认流程。  ### 📚 文档与国际化  - 更新云同步与备份使用说明、终端命令建议相关文档，并补充连接批量编辑、导入反馈及高亮选项所需的简体中文、繁体中文、英语、法语、日语、韩语界面文案。  ### 👥 贡献者  - @fonaix：贡献连接多选与批量编辑、MobaXterm 导入增强，以及云同步本地副本压缩与空间回收。（PR #1190、PR #1199） - @gzx19990101：修复 Telnet NAWS 协商及多行快捷命令的换行处理。（PR #1205）  ### ⚠️ 升级说明  - 已有云同步数据与备份无需转换格式；本地副本的压缩迁移和空间维护会在启动后择机进行。为避免数据丢失，升级前仍建议备份重要连接与配置。 - MobaXterm 的 VNC、Serial 等导入项中，部分无法可靠映射的参数会采用默认值。导入后请核对端口、串口设置及认证信息。 
```

### 2026-11-05T04:56:54Z [**luoxiaoxin123/bwssh**](https://github.com/luoxiaoxin123/bwssh)

```
## 新功能  - **全新 macOS 原生版**：支持 Apple Silicon 和 Intel Mac，提供菜单栏应用、Bitwarden 密码库同步和 SSH agent。 - **macOS 系统通知审批**：在通知中批准或拒绝 SSH 密钥使用请求，也可按程序限时放行；支持审计日志。 - **macOS 快速解锁与按需启动**：支持 Touch ID 和 PIN，可配置重启后的解锁要求；使用 SSH 时可自动启动 bwssh。 - **Windows ARM64 支持**：新增原生 ARM64 安装包，同时继续提供 x64 安装包。 - **密钥管理**：macOS 支持生成、导入和管理 SSH 密钥，兼容 Ed25519、RSA 和 ECDSA。  ## 修复与改进  - 改进 macOS 登录、锁定和解锁流程，并自动修复部分过期的解锁元数据。 - 修复 macOS API 密钥会话续期，以及重命名 SSH 密钥时附件和元数据的保留问题。 - 改进审批窗口布局和 PIN 解锁体验。 - 发布 Release 时自动构建并上传 Windows 和 macOS 各架构安装包。  ## 安装说明  - **Windows**：选择 `x64` 或 `arm64` 安装包，需要 Windows 10 2004 或更高版本。 - **macOS**：Apple Silicon 选择 `arm64`，Intel 选择 `x86_64`；需要 macOS 13 或更高版本。下载 DMG 后，将 bwssh 拖入“应用程序”文件夹。  macOS 安装包尚未通过 Apple 公证。首次打开如被拦截，可在“系统设置 → 隐私与安全性”中选择“仍要打开”。  ---  ## New  - **Native macOS app**: supports Apple Silicon and Intel Macs, with a menu bar app, Bitwarden vault sync, and an SSH agent. - **Native macOS approval notifications**: approve or deny SSH key requests from notifications, grant time-limited access to individual programs, and review audit logs. - **Quick unlock and on-demand launch on macOS**: unlock with Touch ID or a PIN, configure unlock requirements after restart, and start bwssh automatically when SSH needs it. - **Windows ARM64 support**: adds a native ARM64 installer alongside the existing x64 installer. - **Key management**: generate, import, and manage SSH keys on macOS, with support for Ed25519, RSA, and ECDSA.  ## Fixes and improvements  - Improved macOS sign-in, lock, and unlock flows, including automatic repair of some outdated unlock metadata. - Fixed API-key session renewal and preserved attachments and metadata when renaming SSH keys on macOS. - Improved approval window layout and the PIN unlock experience. - Release publishing now builds and uploads packages for all supported Windows and macOS architectures.  ## Installation  - **Windows**: choose the `x64` or `arm64` installer. Requires Windows 10 version 2004 or later. - **macOS**: choose `arm64` for Apple Silicon or `x86_64` for Intel. Requires macOS 13 or later. Open the DMG and drag bwssh into Applications.  The macOS package is not yet notarized by Apple. If macOS blocks the first launch, select “Open Anyway” in System Settings → Privacy & Security. 
```

### 2026-11-10T08:24:00Z [**Stack-Cairn/K-brain**](https://github.com/Stack-Cairn/K-brain)

```
# K-brain v0.107.30  本版从 `main` 的 `8fc87aa` 构建，在 [v0.107.29](https://github.com/Stack-Cairn/K-brain/releases/tag/v0.107.29) 的基础上，让 kn 终端里运行的会话能及时同步到 LiveAgent，并防止两个进程同时写同一个会话导致丢消息。  ## 会话同步（#192）  - **kn 中运行的会话在回合进行中就能看到**：以前 kn 只在回合结束时保存记录，backend 要靠每 30 秒一次的全量扫描才发现，LiveAgent 往往在回合结束十几秒后才显示这个会话。现在 kn 在回合开始时和每轮模型请求之间都会保存进度（至少间隔 2 秒），backend 每 500ms 检查一次变化标记，几秒内就能在列表里看到会话和工具调用。 - **同一会话同时只能在一个地方运行**：kn、`kn run`、backend 的运行和 `/compact` 开始前都要先取得会话的运行锁（`sessions/.runs/<id>.lock` 上的系统文件锁，进程退出或崩溃时自动释放）。会话正在 kn 里运行时，backend 返回 409 `session is running in kn`；反过来，kn 会提示会话正在 LiveAgent 中运行，并保留输入框里的内容。 - **不再覆盖别处写入的消息**：以前 backend 缓存的会话可能按旧记录保存，把 kn 刚追加的消息覆盖掉。现在记录被其他进程改过时，backend 和 kn 都会先从磁盘重新加载，基于旧记录的保存会被拒绝。 - **会话摘要新增 `external_run`**：说明会话正由哪个进程运行（`client`、`pid`、`started_at`），能力标记为 `session_external_runs`。  ## 已知限制  - 一轮模型请求内部的流式文字不会实时同步，要等下一轮开始或回合结束。 - `kn run` 仍只在结束时保存一次；会话标题在回合结束后生成。  ## 下载与校验  提供 macOS、Linux、Windows 的 x64/ARM64 CLI 与 computer helper；`SHA256SUMS` 用于校验下载文件。  **完整变更**：https://github.com/Stack-Cairn/K-brain/compare/v0.107.29...v0.107.30 
```

```
# Vetta 0.5.74 <!-- release-status: unreleased -->  ## 改进  - 项目根目录的 `.mcp.json`（Claude Code 等工具使用的同一格式）现在会和 `.vetta/mcp.json` 一起生效，同一个仓库不用再把 MCP 配置抄一遍；同名时以 `.vetta/mcp.json` 为准，`.mcp.json` 不会被改写，`${VAR:-默认值}` 写法可用。打开含 `.mcp.json` 的仓库会启动其中的本机命令，请只在信任的仓库使用。 - 模型菜单打开时，如果当前使用的模型已在收藏里，默认停在「收藏」栏而不是它所属的供应商栏，常用模型之间切换少点一步；当前模型没收藏时行为不变。 - 请 Agent 安装或管理 MCP 时可以选「全局」还是「只装当前项目」：没说清时它会先问，确认弹窗会显示配置位置。项目级写入 `.vetta/mcp.json`，会随仓库提交，所以它会要求密钥用环境变量引用而不是明文；来自 `.mcp.json` 的连接只读，Agent 不会去改。能力市场里的 skill 目前仍只能装到全局。 - MCP 现在支持旧式 HTTP+SSE 传输：设置里手动添加 MCP 时可选 SSE，配置文件、项目里的 `.mcp.json`、请 Agent 安装时都可以写 `type: "sse"`，用于仍然只提供 SSE 端点的服务器。支持请求头和浏览器授权；服务器同时支持 Streamable HTTP 时仍建议用 HTTP。连接中断后的恢复没有在真实的不稳定网络下验证过。 - 请 Agent 安装 MCP 时不再去读 `mcp.json` 来查看已装内容（里面可能有明文密钥，读了就进了对话），改用会脱敏的查询；也不再自己启动你的可执行文件做握手探测，能否连上以宿主重连后为准。 - Vetta 命令行打不开时，Agent 不再绕十几步去翻别的安装路径：不用管道吞掉失败信号，连续两次启动失败就停下来如实告诉你，只做不依赖它的部分。 - 点侧边栏左下角的「新版本已就绪」提示，不再直接重启，而是先弹出对话框展示本次更新内容（按钮改为「查看更新」），确认后再重启；对话框加宽，更新内容按标题和列表排版。  ## 修复  - 更新源迁到 GitHub 后，设置 → 检查更新里的更新内容被挤成一团、没有标题和列表；现在会还原成带标题、项目符号和行内代码样式的更新日志，分段清楚。 - 沙箱权限选择移入模型菜单后，输入框左侧工具栏在命令面板收起时还留着一条悬空的竖线；现在只在竖线右侧确实有内容（已激活的计划/目标模式等胶囊、命令面板展开的图片/附件按钮，或团队聊天里仍在工具栏的权限选择）时才显示。  ## 其他  - 开发环境（macOS）下 `bun --compile` 产出的 `vetta` 命令行与启动器签名无效、一运行就被系统杀掉，导致 Agent 调不通桌面端；现在编译后及每次复用缓存产物时都会校验并 ad-hoc 重签。正式安装包不受影响。
```

```
# 中文  本次 Next 更新将上游升级至候选版 DeepSeek Harness v0.2.2-rc.1，同步引入新版 `macOS Computer Use`（`dsh-cua-bundle`）、OAR Subagents、`LiveShare` 与第三方插件一键更新，补齐账户内嵌充值与用量页、右键另存为与图片文件拖出，并修复 Windows 沙箱命令失败与空闲时 Host 退出等问题。  DSH NEXT 是社区维护的开源项目，并非 DeepSeek 官方产品。  **上游版本：** [DeepSeek Harness v0.2.2-rc.1](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/releases/tag/dsh-v0.2.2-rc.1)。  ## 上游内核破坏性更新（`dsh v0.2.2-rc.1`）  本次升级至 `DeepSeek Harness v0.2.2-rc.1` 包含内核接口与运行时破坏性变更，可能影响第三方插件与自部署环境：  - **第三方与自定义插件兼容性:** 上游升级至 Cordis `4.1.0`，重构了工具按步骤激活、原生与 PTC 工具执行拆分、模型路由固定及异步会话投影接口；原独立“Git 工作树”插件已并入“变更请求与工作树”。尚未适配 `0.2.2` 的第三方插件可能导致启动失败，可在恢复助手中通过安全模式或“插件 → 已安装”更新／禁用；旧版本按需安装的官方组件会在启动前自动清理，在“插件”页重新开启即可安装当前版本。 - **自部署 PTC 与远程 SSH 升级要求:** PTC 控制通道改用 `birpc`，SSH 协议升至 v3，取消执行状态码改为 `ABORTED`；自部署 bootstrap 或远程 SSH helper 需与 `0.2.2-rc.1` 同步升级。 - **运行环境与默认策略变更:** 外部运行 DSH 的最低 Node.js 版本提升至 `24.9`（桌面安装包已内置 Node `24.21`）；未声明容量的自定义模型默认上下文／输出调整为 `1M` / `128K`；文件修改前的先读与外部修改检查默认关闭；`web_fetch` 默认允许访问代理与内网地址。  ## 新增与改进  - **新版 `macOS Computer Use`（`dsh-cua-bundle`）与双版本切换:** macOS 下支持按需下载安装 `dsh-cua-bundle`，并在 `Computer Use (Next)` 详情页提供切换开关（macOS 14+ 支持，用于 PTC 模式）；启用后在 PTC 会话中自动接管并隐藏 `Computer Use (Next)` 的命令与工具，切换无需重启。 - **OAR Subagents、`LiveShare` 与插件管理:** 插件页上架 OAR Subagents（可将独立任务委派给本机 Claude Code 或 Codex 并配置默认权限）、`LiveShare`（实验）、`Ralph` 与 `Session Search`；“插件 → 已安装”支持检查第三方市场插件新版本并一键更新。 - **上游 `0.2.2-rc.1` 特性与原生桌面集成:** 支持选中聊天文本引用、复制完整会话为 Markdown、收起步骤保留全部助手文字回复、图片进入输入框即上传、全局统一右键与键盘菜单、文件预览常驻目录树及宿主级“自动化任务”管理；桌面端补齐账户充值与用量的应用内嵌视图、右键原生“另存为”与跨域图片复制，以及会话与预览图片直接作为文件拖出。 - **Linux 更新检查、CLI 与“打开方式”:** Linux 版支持检查更新并打开下载页，支持通过 `<app> dsh <参数>` 直接运行内置 CLI；Windows“打开方式”支持识别 Windows Terminal 及中文路径下的应用，Linux 新增识别 `Ptyxis` 与 `GNOME Console`。  ## 修复  - **Windows 沙箱与编码兼容:** 修复 Windows 受限沙箱下命令无输出退出、临时目录被系统清理后报错，以及托盘终端运行 `dsh` 命令误拉起第二个桌面窗口的问题；禁止将应用安装目录选为工作区或数据目录；修复 `pwsh` 调用 Python 中文乱码及配置文件含 UTF-8 BOM 导致进入恢复助手的问题。 - **Host 稳定性与恢复诊断:** 修复空闲或重连时后台桥接调用超时导致 Plugin Host 退出的问题；恢复助手错误详情顶部直接展示 Host 首条致命错误摘要；修复多次重启后浏览器或局域网访问因 Cookie 累积报 `HTTP 431` 的问题。 - **界面交互与 Linux 打包:** 修复 Office 等应用内插件视图无法复制粘贴、自动化页面顶部无法拖动窗口、“更新公告”按钮默认未指向当前版本，以及 `LiveShare` 读者端在部分插件开启时无法加载页面的问题；补齐 Linux 安装包桌面图标与 Debian 音频／图形依赖。  ## 下载  从本页 **Assets** 下载对应平台的安装包：  | 平台 | 文件 | | --- | --- | | macOS Universal · Apple Silicon / Intel | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.22-next-universal.dmg` | | Windows x64 | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.22-next-x64-Setup.exe` | | Linux x64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.22-next-x86_64.AppImage` | | Linux x64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.22-next-amd64.deb` | | Linux x64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.22-next-linux-x64.tar.gz` | | Linux ARM64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.22-next-arm64.AppImage` | | Linux ARM64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.22-next-arm64.deb` | | Linux ARM64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.22-next-linux-arm64.tar.gz` |  可使用本页 `SHA256SUMS.txt` 核验全部安装包。  ## 使用提示  - 请先退出旧版应用并备份 `~/.dsh`（或在“设置 → 桌面设置”中使用“备份”），安装后验证常用插件和工作流程。 - 如需通过应用内终端管理 `desktop` Profile 的插件，请先启动 Desktop 完成该 Profile 的初始化。 - 从 2.0.17-next 或更早版本升级时，建议手动下载安装；旧版应用内更新可能无法完成下载。 - Office 文档技能首次使用时将随包运行时安装到 `~/.dsh/dsh-runtimes/dsh-next-primary-runtime`，需要额外磁盘空间。 - 默认与旧稳定版共用 `~/.dsh`；Profile 不隔离同一目录中的会话、账号和凭据。 - 可在“设置 → 桌面设置”或恢复助手中切换数据目录，也可通过绝对路径 `DSH_DESKTOP_NEXT_HOME` 隔离 DSH 数据（应用内选择优先于环境变量），但不会因此隔离 AA 的默认共享登录与设备绑定状态。  [完整变更记录](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/compare/v2.0.21-next...v2.0.22-next) · [项目网站](https://dshdesktop.cn)  DSH NEXT 已取代原稳定版通道。  ---  # English  This Next release upgrades upstream to the release candidate DeepSeek Harness v0.2.2-rc.1, bringing on-demand `macOS Computer Use` (`dsh-cua-bundle`), OAR Subagents, `LiveShare`, and one-click third-party plugin updates to Next, adding embedded account top-up and usage views, native Save As, and image file drag-out, and fixing Windows sandbox command failures and idle Host exits.  DSH NEXT is a community-maintained open-source project, not an official DeepSeek product.  **Upstream version:** [DeepSeek Harness v0.2.2-rc.1](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/releases/tag/dsh-v0.2.2-rc.1).  ## Upstream kernel breaking changes (`dsh v0.2.2-rc.1`)  `DeepSeek Harness v0.2.2-rc.1` includes kernel API and runtime breaking changes that may affect third-party plugins and self-deployed environments:  - **Third-party and custom plugin compatibility:** Upstream upgrades to Cordis `4.1.0` and refactors conditional per-step tool activation, native vs. PTC tool execution, fixed per-step model routing, and asynchronous session projections; the standalone Git worktree plugin is now merged into Change requests and worktrees. Third-party plugins not yet updated for `0.2.2` may prevent startup and can be updated or disabled via Safe Mode or **Plugins → Installed** in the recovery assistant; on-demand official bundles from an older release are automatically cleaned up before startup and can be re-enabled in **Plugins**. - **Self-deployed PTC and remote SSH upgrade requirements:** The PTC control channel now uses `birpc`, the SSH protocol is bumped to v3, and cancelled execution status codes now return `ABORTED`; self-deployed bootstraps and remote SSH helpers must be upgraded together with `0.2.2-rc.1`. - **Runtime and default policy changes:** The minimum Node.js version for running DSH externally is raised to `24.9` (packaged desktop builds already bundle Node `24.21`); undeclared custom-model capacities default to `1M` context / `128K` output; read-before-write and external-change checks are disabled by default; `web_fetch` allows proxy and private-network addresses by default.  ## New and improved  - **New `macOS Computer Use` (`dsh-cua-bundle`) and dual-version switching:** Added on-demand installation of `dsh-cua-bundle` on macOS with a toggle on the `Computer Use (Next)` detail page (supported on macOS 14+ for PTC mode); enabling it automatically takes over PTC sessions and hides `Computer Use (Next)`’s command and tools without a restart. - **OAR Subagents, `LiveShare`, and plugin management:** Added OAR Subagents (delegate independent tasks to local Claude Code or Codex CLIs with configurable default permissions), `LiveShare` (Experimental), `Ralph`, and `Session Search` to the Plugins page; added version checks and one-click updates for third-party market plugins under **Plugins → Installed**. - **Upstream `0.2.2-rc.1` highlights and native desktop integration:** Quote chat text into the composer, copy full conversations as Markdown, keep all assistant text replies visible when steps are collapsed, upload images immediately in the composer, use unified context and keyboard menus, browse a persistent directory tree in file previews, and manage scheduled tasks at the Host level; Desktop adds embedded account top-up and usage views, native context-menu "Save as" and cross-origin image copy, and native file drag-out for conversation and preview images. - **Linux update checks, CLI, and "Open In":** Linux builds now check for updates and open the download page, and support running the bundled CLI directly via `<app> dsh <args>`; "Open In" now detects Windows Terminal and apps under non-ASCII paths on Windows, plus `Ptyxis` and `GNOME Console` on Linux.  ## Fixes  - **Windows sandbox and encoding compatibility:** Fixed confined commands exiting without output, failing after temp directory cleanup, or spawning a second GUI window from the tray terminal’s `dsh` CLI; prevented selecting the installation directory as a workspace or data directory; fixed garbled Chinese output from Python in `pwsh` and UTF-8 BOMs in config files triggering recovery. - **Host stability and recovery diagnostics:** Fixed late background bridge call timeouts while idle terminating the Plugin Host; surfaced the Host's first fatal error line at the top of recovery details; fixed accumulated browser cookies causing `HTTP 431` errors after repeated restarts. - **UI interaction and Linux packaging:** Restored clipboard access in app-origin plugin views such as Office, made the top strip of the Automation page draggable on macOS, linked "Release notes" to the installed version by default, and fixed `LiveShare` reader pages failing to load when certain owner plugins are enabled; added multi-size desktop icons and missing Debian audio/graphics dependencies on Linux.  ## Downloads  Choose the matching installer from **Assets** below:  | Platform | File | | --- | --- | | macOS Universal · Apple Silicon / Intel | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.22-next-universal.dmg` | | Windows x64 | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.22-next-x64-Setup.exe` | | Linux x64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.22-next-x86_64.AppImage` | | Linux x64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.22-next-amd64.deb` | | Linux x64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.22-next-linux-x64.tar.gz` | | Linux ARM64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.22-next-arm64.AppImage` | | Linux ARM64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.22-next-arm64.deb` | | Linux ARM64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.22-next-linux-arm64.tar.gz` |  Use `SHA256SUMS.txt` from this release to verify all installers.  ## Usage notes  - Quit the previous version and back up `~/.dsh` (or use **Back up** in **Settings → Desktop settings**) before installing. Verify your usual plugins and workflows afterward. - Before managing plugins for the `desktop` Profile from the bundled terminal, launch Desktop to initialize that Profile. - When upgrading from 2.0.17-next or earlier, download and install manually; in-app updates in those versions may not complete the download. - The Office skills install the bundled runtime under `~/.dsh/dsh-runtimes/dsh-next-primary-runtime` on first use, requiring additional disk space. - Defaults to the shared `~/.dsh` home. Profiles do not isolate sessions, accounts, or credentials within that home. - Change the data directory in **Settings → Desktop settings** or the recovery assistant, or set `DSH_DESKTOP_NEXT_HOME` to an absolute path to isolate DSH data (an in-app choice takes precedence over the environment variable); this does not isolate AA’s default shared sign-in and device-binding state.  [Full changelog](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/compare/v2.0.21-next...v2.0.22-next) · [Project website](https://dshdesktop.cn)  DSH NEXT replaces the former Stable edition channel. 
```

### 2026-11-06T03:32:50Z [**ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI**](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI)

```
自 v0.18.0 以来，dsh-TUI 跟上 DSH `0.2.2-rc.1` 验证线，让一次 `/update` 同时对齐 TUI、全局 dsh 引擎与全局启动器， 并修好会话恢复、btw 输入、子代理失败提示与 Codex 后端命令输出等一批问题。  ## 亮点  - **适配 DSH `0.2.2-rc.1`** — npm 上的 DSH `latest` 已是 `0.2.2-rc.1`，而 v0.18.0 的精确 peer 只到 `0.2.1-rc.5`，升级 DSH 后 TUI 会被判 incompatible 并 skip。本版把 `0.2.2-rc.1` 加入验证线与 peer 范围，`0.2.1-rc.5` 及其之前的验证线继续保留；模型选择改用上游的会话级 `Agent.model` 接缝、工具限制落到每个 Agent 的作用域、适配 cordis 4.1 生命周期与固定动作审批，旧宿主继续走原来的选择引用与请求瀑布。([#1886](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1886) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **一次 `/update` 对齐 TUI、dsh 引擎与全局启动器** — 以前 `/update` 只换 profile 里的 TUI，全局 dsh 与启动器留在原地，可能出现新 TUI 的 peer 范围装不上当前引擎。现在预检为三层解析同一份计划，按 TUI → 引擎 → 启动器串行执行：引擎只选目标 TUI peer 范围内已发布的最高版本（永不降级、不选 alpha 线），装完用 profile 模块解析与 `dsh --version` 验证；启动器只动能证明归属的全局安装，多成员 pnpm 组、项目内依赖与有歧义的别名一律不改。TUI 已是最新时也不再短路，仍会补齐引擎与启动器；引擎安装失败后只有能证明启动的入口才允许重启，跳过原因会留在下一次挂载的屏幕与警告里。([#1847](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1847)、[#1849](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1849) by @Lucas2011wastaken；[#1889](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1889) by @AdamPlatin123) - **子代理通道整体失败会明说** — 同一 provider 的已结算运行至少失败 2 次且失败过半时，子代理列表上方出现通道降级提示，并建议切换 provider；只有可切换的 `claude-code` / `codex` 通道会提示，内建 `subagent`、workflow 与 teammate 行不误报。([#1794](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1794) by @MomentDerek)  ## 需要留意的行为变化  - **权限问卷只提供固定动作** — 在 DSH 0.2.2 上，`approval` 类问题只提供调用方声明的选项，不再有自由文本入口；普通问卷仍可自由输入。([#1886](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1886) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **`/update` 的拒绝语义与逃生开关** — 新 TUI 要升级、而**当前运行**的引擎不在它的 peer 范围内时，即使引擎可以自动升级也会先拒绝，并打印「先升级引擎，再重新 `/update`」的命令（官方加载校验按运行中的 dsh 判定）；引擎装完校验不通过时不重启，但保留已升级的 TUI。`DSH_TUI_UPDATE_ENGINE=0` 钉住引擎，`DSH_TUI_UPDATE_LAUNCHER=0` 跳过启动器，`DSH_TUI_DSH_PM=npm|pnpm` 覆盖包管理器判定。([#1849](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1849) by @Lucas2011wastaken；[#1889](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1889) by @AdamPlatin123) - **`/restart` 带回图片，被后端丢弃的输入不再自动重发** — 交接文件从纯文本改为带图片的输入；被判定「确定没执行」的输入会暂存为未送达，由用户决定是否重发。([#1764](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1764) by @MomentDerek) - **长会话不再重放打字动画，代价是随历史线性增长的内存** — 已完成行的记账不再有 2048 条上限（几万行量级约十几 MB），随会话释放。([#1796](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1796) by @MomentDerek) - **btw 输入框恒为一行** — 长草稿围绕光标开窗、被裁一侧显示 `…`，不再折行；只支持左右查看，不支持多行编辑。([#1866](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1866) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **Linux 进程名显示为 `dsh-tui`** — `ps -o comm`、`top` 与系统监视器不再显示 `MainThread`；`/proc/<pid>/cmdline` 保持不变。([#1841](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1841) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **rio 与 Orca 启用扩展键编码** — 两者加入 `CSI >1u` 白名单，`Shift+Enter` 与修饰键组合可用；名单出问题时可用 `DSH_TUI_FORCE_EXTENDED_KEYS=1` / `DSH_TUI_DISABLE_EXTENDED_KEYS=1` 强制或禁用（disable 优先）。([#1821](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1821) by @Plintash；[#1840](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1840) by @Lucas2011wastaken)  ## 修复  - **会话与恢复**：会话管理页去掉每个 project 下方重复且容易截断的路径、全部来源统一单行列表，并修好快速连点两个 project 时第二击被当成选词的问题（多击判定现在要求同一个 `onClick` 祖先）；从整屏界面返回或 `/resume` 后历史消息不再逐条重新「打字」；会话自身收尾的回合显示「会话已停止」而不是用户中断；Linux 上被其他进程复用的旧线程 ID 不再让空闲会话被误判为被占用。([#1755](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1755) by @xuzijian2019；[#1796](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1796)、[#1764](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1764) by @MomentDerek；[#1769](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1769)、[#1770](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1770) by @FlameTN7) - **输入与面板**：窄侧栏 btw 草稿不再被挤成 0 宽或按未截断的草稿撑高，尾部和硬件光标留在框内；不带 bracketed-paste 标记的整块文本也先压平成单行。([#1866](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1866) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **子代理与后端**：Codex 后端下 `/usage`、`/diff` 查询成功后会显示输出，补全菜单里按 `Enter` 直接执行而不用先补空格。([#1775](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1775) by @MomentDerek) - **终端兼容**：Orca 与 rio 的扩展键、Kitty 小键盘码位 57416–57426 补齐，CSI-u 的非打印键不再被当作文本插入。([#1840](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1840)、[#1841](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1841) by @Lucas2011wastaken；[#1821](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1821) by @Plintash) - **文档**：新增「在 Orca 终端里会话内 git 全部失败」的排查与规避（`env -u GIT_CONFIG_COUNT`）。([#1863](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1863) by @Lucas2011wastaken)  ## 兼容与升级  - **DSH 主适配线**：`0.2.2-rc.1`；`0.2.1-rc.5`、`0.2.1-rc.4`、`0.2.1-rc.3`、`0.2.1-rc.2`、`0.2.0-rc.2`、`0.2.0-rc.1`、`0.1.7-rc.2`、`0.1.7-rc.1` 以及更早的验证线仍在契约内，契约外版本打漂移警告。 - **DSH 0.2.2 要求 Node `>=24.9`**：本包自身仍声明 `^22.19 || >=24`；用更旧的 Node 运行 0.2.2 宿主属于上游未支持组合，升级前请先确认 `node -v`。 - **已经升到 DSH `0.2.2-rc.1`、TUI 打不开的用户**：TUI 起不来时用不了程序内 `/update`，请直接执行下面的 npm 命令升级到 v0.19.0。曾用 `dsh plugin allow-version` 给 v0.18.0 加过豁免的，升级后不再需要。 - **从 v0.18.0 升级**：程序内 `/update`（本版会一并把 dsh 引擎对齐到 `0.2.2-rc.1`、并对齐全局启动器），或   `npm install -g @deepseek-ai/dsh @deepseek-harness-tui/dsh-tui@0.19.0`。 - **`dst` 自动化 CLI 的验证线未同步前移**：仍验证到 `0.2.1-rc.5`；在 0.2.2 宿主上可用 `dst dsh` 原样转交官方参数。 - **嫡系插件清单**：随包的 `tui-profile/feed/first-party.json` 钉到 `0.19.0`。 - **Windows 离线安装器**：引擎验证范围随契约前移，新装或升级到 `0.2.2-rc.1`；已装更高的 RC 会降回验证线。  ## 安装  sh npm install -g @deepseek-ai/dsh @deepseek-harness-tui/dsh-tui@0.19.0   偏好一键安装的用户可下载 Release 中的 `dsh-tui-setup.zip`（解压后 macOS / Linux 运行 `sh install.sh`，Windows 双击 `install.bat`） 或 Windows 离线安装程序 `dsh-tui-setup-win-x64.exe`；便携包解压即用。   <!-- dsh-tui:generated-notes --> <!-- Release notes generated using configuration in .github/release.yml at v0.19.0 -->  ## What's Changed * fix(session): preserve the disposal reason in turn notices by @FlameTN7 in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1769 * fix(session): ignore reused Linux thread ids in mount ownership by @FlameTN7 in surface channel-level failures instead of one opaque sentence by @MomentDerek in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1794 * test(scripts): give composer-mounting scripts a throwaway HOME (#1788) by @MomentDerek in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1792 * fix(ink): allowlist rio for extended keys and add force/disable hatches by @Plintash in process dsh-tui on Linux by @Lucas2011wastaken in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1841 * fix(channel): name disposed turns, hold backend-dropped inputs and carry held images across /restart by @MomentDerek in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1764 * fix(reveal): stop re-typewriting completed history rows on remount by @MomentDerek in ## Release 3.1.0+53  - `040c473` :sparkles: Send a Solian-style User-Agent to Solar Network and MaidCafe  Add a shared user-agent helper that renders `MaidKit/<version>+<build> (<platform>; <model>; <host>)`, the same shape Solian sends, sanitized to printable ASCII and resolved once.  CloudSyncService, MaidCafeService, the Metoer client, the SSE stream and the daemon health/handshake probes now stamp every request through a Dio interceptor, and the Personality/billing HTTP calls add the header directly. Web builds skip it: `User-Agent` is a forbidden header there. (LittleSheep) - `411795d` :sparkles: Show What's New changelog sheet on app upgrade and bump Socommon packages (LittleSheep) - `801f50d` :lipstick: Smooth the @ picker and queued prompts with motion and mono tokens  The mention strip and menu now open and close in an animated slot, chips enter keyed by their resource, and the menu row that the arrow keys lit carries a primary rule and scrolls itself into view. Rows print the token a pick writes, in the mono face the field already uses.  Queued prompts lose the ListTile and divider for compact numbered rows: the first holds the primary tint because it runs next, controls stay quiet until hover, and removal collapses the row before the queue changes so the prompt below rises instead of jumping. (LittleSheep) - `991d36f` :lipstick: Rework the server, proxy, cloud and daemon consoles  Server detail: - left rail keeps identity, connection state with latency and static specs   (cores, RAM, root disk gauge, OS + kernel) plus terminal/files/connect;   the duplicated live metric cards are gone - inspector tabs are left-aligned buttons with a ••• overflow instead of   a thirteen-entry stretched TabBar, and opened tabs stay mounted  Activity: - source badge, uptime and refresh collapse into one header row; the blue   banner and the four summary tiles go, current readings move into each   chart header, and the disk card leaves the time-series stack  Reverse proxy: - detected versions are normalized, so Caddy reads "2.11.7" instead of   a Go module checksum - sites carry upstream and TLS state: rows show → target · HTTPS, open the   host in a browser, and repeat the config path only when it differs - service bar ranks actions: edit/logs/reload visible, stop in error red,   restart and boot state behind one overflow menu  MaidCafe cloud: - daemon cards become a table (identity · status pill · CPU/MEM · last seen   · row menu); the metric plot opens per row on demand, so closed rows keep   equal height - the red disconnect banner becomes an offline pill, full UUIDs become a   short host prefix, and the register action moves from a floating button   to the tab strip, dropping the duplicate account avatar  Daemon detail: - tabs are left-aligned icon tabs - native ops group by Compose/Containers/Services/Other, name each verb in   plain language, mark destructive verbs, and say "Configure & run" when   parameters are needed  Translations updated for en-US, zh-CN and zh-TW. (LittleSheep) - `d59a210` :lipstick: Optimized command (LittleSheep) - `cf3ca3b` :bug: Fix some bugs (LittleSheep) - `87a77e3` :rocket: Launch 3.1.0+53 (LittleSheep)  ---  | Artifact | Platform | Architecture | Size | Download | | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | | app-arm64-v8a-release.apk | android | arm64 | 66.9 MiB | [Download](https://dx.solsynth.dev/artifacts/9a13c329-5c91-45cd-a9aa-1efbcc6c42ea/download) | | app-armeabi-v7a-release.apk | android | armeabi-v7a | 65.2 MiB | [Download](https://dx.solsynth.dev/artifacts/110e11a2-d123-44ed-8bfe-33b7dd802bdd/download) | | app-x86_64-release.apk | android | x86_64 | 70.4 MiB | [Download](https://dx.solsynth.dev/artifacts/ebb7ccee-3aa1-4a69-9968-eb5cb221f5a8/download) | | build-output-linux-appimage.zip | linux | amd64 | 55.1 MiB | [Download](https://dx.solsynth.dev/artifacts/a73f34a3-bb94-451a-b902-7ea1f463f4c7/download) | | build-output-windows-installer.zip | windows | amd64 | 33.5 MiB | [Download](https://dx.solsynth.dev/artifacts/27baa53a-cc62-4e87-b1f4-6feb22eb5726/download) |  ---  Powered by Solsynth Express®
```

```
 ### 新增与优化 - 主机配置支持从已登录的 Tailscale 网络选择设备，支持 Tailscale SSH 身份认证。 - 优化设备加载、连接验证、主机表单及指纹提示。 - 关于页新增反馈与分享入口。 - 更新内置网络组件，加强 TLS 证书校验。  ### 修复与优化 - 修复部分输入法联想输入时文字重复的问题。 - 改善块字符、线画、分隔符、圆头与阴影块的绘制和对齐。 - 修复图标和中文拉伸，改善行距、字距与网格对齐。 - 精简内置字体，中文默认使用系统回退，保留符号、图标及字体下载和导入；修复失效的旧字体选择。 - 优化 tmux 检查进度反馈。 - 修复关闭会话或文件浏览页后，连接验证等待未及时取消的问题。  ### 安装要求 - Android 11+、arm64-v8a，统一提供一个安装包。 - 使用 mosh 或 tmux 时，远端需安装相应程序。
```

```
[中文](#中文) | [English](#english)  ### 中文  OxideTerm 2.3.1 把主机工具带到了本机：查端口、看进程或管理容器，不必先建立 SSH 连接。已有终端可以直接平铺在一起；经常同时使用多条隧道的用户，也可以留在会话管理器里一次启动所选隧道。  #### 🖥️ 本机与远程主机工具  - 在主机选择器中选择本机或 SSH 连接，查看进程、端口、服务、日志、文件系统、容器和软件包，并执行当前平台支持的管理操作。Windows 支持按 winget 包 ID 查看、升级和卸载；即使默认终端是 WSL，Windows 本机操作也留在宿主系统。取消或超时的本机采集会清理其子进程。（PR [#750](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/750)） - 终端复用器面板现在可以切换 tmux、Zellij 和 Herdr。展开会话即可查看标签和窗格；Herdr 还显示工作区。可以在所选主机上附加到会话，并按工具支持的范围聚焦、新建、拆分或关闭窗格。（PR [#750](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/750)） - 主机监控减少了 Docker、系统、日志及部分 AMD、Intel GPU 采样中的无用字段。过大的 Docker 输出会报告采样不完整，不再显示为空列表；旧于 20.10 的 Docker CLI 也能读取容器状态。  #### ⚡ 终端绘制与会话布局  - 持续输出和滚动时，终端会复用未改变的字形与排版结果。在 Windows x64 的绘制基准复测中，纯文本持续输出耗时从 1,108 降至 773 微秒，混合 Unicode 从 1,133 降至 824 微秒。这里测的是绘制工作负载，不含 SSH 网络传输。[基准条件与完整结果](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/blob/v2.3.1/docs/development/performance-and-benchmarking.md#incremental-terminal-drawing-2026-11-03) - 标题和超链接等 OSC 序列的载荷改为批量扫描。修复了 UTF-8 编码的 C1 控制字符跨输入块时可能被当作文字显示的问题，以及自定义背景上的透明 Powerline 分隔符。 - 可将 2 到 16 个已打开的终端平铺到一个标签页，无需重新连接。双击窗格标题可单独放大，再还原原布局；也可以将窗格移出为独立标签页。 - 浏览 Shell 历史后可恢复命令建议。替换或执行前会重新核对当前输入，迟到的终端回显不会覆盖新输入；上一个和下一个候选的快捷键可在设置中修改，默认按键不变。  #### 🔗 SSH、隧道与文件传输  - SSH 终端工具栏可显示当前连接的请求往返延迟。同一连接共享采样，默认每 10 秒探测一次；可调整间隔或关闭显示，超时状态与有效读数分开呈现。 - 在会话管理器里启动隧道不再切换标签页。多选启动时，同一已保存主机共用一次认证并逐条启动；失败提示给出隧道名，转发表也显示自定义名称。 - 命令面板可以搜索并打开各类已保存连接。SFTP 列表和按钮随界面字号缩放；悬停在修改日期上可查看精确到秒的本地时间。 - Linux 重新连接已保存的 BLE 设备时，不再把 BlueZ 缓存中的离线设备误判为当前可连接设备。（PR [#749](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/749)）  #### 🎛️ 外观与快捷键  - 外观设置可在普通圆角与连续曲线之间切换。macOS 的隐藏应用和最小化窗口快捷键也可在快捷键设置中修改。  #### 👥 贡献者  - @idxdy：将主机工具扩展到本机，加入 winget 操作并改进 Windows 采样。（PR [#750](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/750)） - @allegrox：修复 Linux BLE 重连时对离线设备的误匹配。（PR [#749](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/749)）  ### English  OxideTerm 2.3.1 brought Host Tools to the local machine: inspect ports, processes and containers without opening an SSH connection. You can also tile terminals that are already running and start several saved tunnels without leaving Session Manager.  #### 🖥️ Local and remote Host Tools  - Select the local machine or an SSH connection to inspect processes, ports, services, logs, filesystems, containers and packages, and run the actions available on that platform. Windows gained winget inspection, upgrade and uninstall by package ID. Windows host actions stay on Windows even when the preferred terminal is WSL. Cancelled or timed-out local captures clean up their process trees. (PR [#750](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/750)) - The terminal multiplexer panel now supports tmux, Zellij and Herdr. Expand a session to inspect tabs and panes, plus workspaces in Herdr. Attach on the selected host, or focus, create, split and close panes where the multiplexer supports it. (PR [#750](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/750)) - Monitoring requests fewer unused Docker, system, log and AMD/Intel GPU fields. Oversized Docker samples are reported as incomplete rather than shown as empty lists. Container state is also available with Docker CLI versions before 20.10.  #### ⚡ Terminal rendering and layout  - Streaming output and scrolling reuse unchanged glyphs and text layouts. In a Windows x64 drawing benchmark rerun, plain streaming text fell from 1,108 to 773 microseconds and mixed Unicode from 1,133 to 824 microseconds. These timings cover the drawing workload, excluding SSH transport. [Benchmark setup and results](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/blob/v2.3.1/docs/development/performance-and-benchmarking.md#incremental-terminal-drawing-2026-11-03) - OSC payloads such as titles and hyperlinks are scanned in batches. UTF-8-encoded C1 controls now work across input chunk boundaries, and transparent Powerline separators render correctly over custom backgrounds. - Tile 2 to 16 open terminals in one tab without reconnecting. Double-click a pane header to enlarge it and restore the original layout, or extract a pane into its own tab. - Command suggestions recover after shell history navigation. Replacement and execution recheck the current input, so delayed echo cannot overwrite a newer command. Previous and next suggestion shortcuts are configurable and retain their existing defaults.  #### 🔗 SSH, tunnels and file transfers  - The SSH terminal toolbar can show request round-trip time for the active connection. Tabs sharing a connection share its probe. The default interval is 10 seconds; you can change it or disable the display. Timeouts are shown separately from valid readings. - Starting a tunnel no longer takes you out of Session Manager. Batch starts authenticate once per saved host and start its rules in sequence. Errors identify affected tunnels by name, and the forwarding table shows their saved names. - The command palette can find and open all saved connection types. SFTP rows and buttons follow the UI font size; hover over a modification date to see local time down to the second. - Reconnecting a saved BLE device on Linux no longer matches offline devices from the BlueZ cache. (PR [#749](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/749))  #### 🎛️ Appearance and shortcuts  - Choose standard rounded corners or continuous curves in Appearance. On macOS, you can also rebind the shortcuts for hiding the app and minimizing the window.  #### 👥 Contributors  - @idxdy brought Host Tools to the local machine, added winget actions and improved Windows sampling. (PR [#750](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/750)) - @allegrox fixed offline device matching during Linux BLE reconnects. ([#4697](https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk/issues/4697)) * **cicd:** build the deb, rpm and aarch64 Linux binaries as static musl ([#5017](https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk/issues/5017)) * **dotnet:** preserve discovery and classic TRX reports ([#4628](https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk/issues/4628)) * **gain:** distinguish filtered timings from passthrough lifetimes ([#4677](https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk/issues/4677)) * **git:** announce the cap on the filtered `git log` path ([#4152](https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk/issues/4152)) * **hermes:** close the rewrite's ([3467fb2](https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk/commit/3467fb24c155aa1b4c6aca71e76b3d37798be9c3)) * **hook:** preserve fish-wrap decisions and reuse permission scans ([#4785](https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk/issues/4785)) * **hook:** rewrite commands without rewriting the text between them ([#4132](https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk/issues/4132)) * **hook:** skip proxy autostart on occupied loopback ports ([#4778](https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk/issues/4778)) * **hooks:** honour exclude_commands for a swapped program called by path ([#5019](https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk/issues/5019)) * **hooks:** preserve host checks when settings cannot be loaded ([#4680](https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk/issues/4680)) * **init:** install the ### 2026-11-10T15:50:10Z [**zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui**](https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui)
```

```
English:  ✨ Features - **Add Fable 5.2 to the Claude model list**: Fable 5.2 is now the top built-in Claude model, with its 200K and `[1m]` context windows, pricing, effort levels up to `max` and model mapping. Fable 5.1 moves to the previous-generation slot. A saved Fable 5 selection is migrated to Fable 5.2 instead of falling back to Sonnet, while a custom model that uses the `claude-fable-5` id keeps working and is not labelled as retired (by @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Run Codex in projects opened from WSL** (#2013, #2050): when the project is opened through a `\\wsl.localhost\<distro>\...` path and node runs inside WSL, Codex now receives a Linux working directory for sends and for the plan, review, compact, preconnect and release controls, so it no longer fails with `spawn ... ENOENT`. The CLI settings page also detects a Codex installed inside WSL instead of reporting it as not installed (by @Faq, @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Replay more of native Codex exec history** (#2016): replay reads literal arguments, object spreads, string concatenation and const or destructured tool aliases without running the script, and splits `Promise.all`/`allSettled` map callbacks and awaited `for-of` loops into one result per command, matched by their native receipts. Yielded cells stay open until the host reports a final response, recorded dynamic tool calls are shown, and successful automatic approvals no longer add noise. Calls that may never run keep their original wrapper, and const analysis runs once per script, which takes one large record from over 80 seconds to under a second (by @gadfly3173, @saligia3173, @zhukunpenglinyutong)  🔧 Improvements - **Drop the Beta labels from the CLI providers**: Grok, Kimi, OpenCode, PI, OMP, DeepSeek Harness, MiniMax Code and ZCode no longer carry a Beta badge in the provider switcher or in Settings. Picking one now switches immediately instead of opening a one-time Beta notice (by @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Keep Codex turn footers and track MCP file edits**: a turn that used tools shows its time, token and cost footer again. File edits made through MCP tools, including the JetBrains `replace_text_in_file`, `create_new_file` and `apply_patch` tools, now appear in the edit list. Only edits that fully state their effect can be undone: overwrites, case-insensitive replacements, edits from other servers and relative paths are listed with a read-only diff. Undo and diff Reject follow the same rule, which now also applies to MCP edits in Claude sessions (by @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Tell users once when their Codex CLI is outdated**: a CLI older than 0.160.0 now gets one notice per chat window instead of failing silently, and the `unknown variant auto_review` rejection from 0.123.x shows a localized diagnostic that explains how to update the CLI or change the permission mode. The fallback model metadata warning, which the CLI repeats on every turn for models outside its catalog, no longer pops up as a toast (by @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Explain why Claude Code cannot start on older Windows** (#1928, #2052): exit code 0xC0000139 (also reported as `-1073741511`) now opens a localized diagnostic card. It explains that Claude Code 2.1.118 and later need Windows 10 1809 or Windows Server 2019, and offers two ways out: update Windows, or switch the SDK to v0.2.112 or earlier in Dependency Management (by @Faq, @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Make code blocks easier to read** (#1908, #2039, #2047, #2064): Dart and Flutter fences are now syntax-highlighted. The light theme gets its own highlight palette, with keywords, built-ins, comments and tags darkened to reach 4.5:1 contrast on the code background, and the palette follows IDE theme changes at runtime (by @hebulin, @EeeasyCode) - **Open project files through symlinked paths** (#2030): file links under symlinked project directories now open, navigate and show tooltips correctly. `#L12-L20` line ranges work, including on paths with spaces, non-ASCII names and file URIs. Diff writes and deletes still go through the canonical project boundary check, so a link through a symlink or Windows junction cannot grant write access outside the project (by @kochpy, @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Give chat-tab activity its own status colors**: the tab strip marks each turn by color. A running turn shows a blue dot, a finished turn shows a green dot (kept while its reply is unread), and a turn that failed shows a red dot until the tab is opened or a new turn starts. The tab title is no longer rewritten to `AI1...` or suffixed with a localized "Completed", so names stay stable (by @zhukunpenglinyutong)  🐛 Fixes - **Restore the OMP icon's brand colors**: the OMP CLI π now uses omp.sh's pink → violet → cyan gradient in the provider switcher, Settings and history instead of a flat theme tint, while mono contexts keep the currentColor mark (by @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Stop a stalled Claude stream from blocking the window** (#2001, #2051): when the model API or a relay stops sending mid-stream, the turn now fails after 10 minutes without activity with a clear "Claude stopped responding" error, and the next message starts a fresh runtime instead of waiting behind it forever. The timer pauses while a main-conversation tool call is running, so long commands, subagents and approval dialogs are not treated as a stall. `CC_GUI_CLAUDE_STALL_TIMEOUT_MS` changes the limit and `0` disables it (by @Faq) - **Unstick the message queue when a send never starts streaming**: a send that ended or failed before streaming began, for example with streaming disabled or an error reported only as a status, could leave the chat loading forever, so every later message waited in the queue. The webview now asks the backend for the session's real loading state once the wait window closes (by @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Stop denying commands that only mention `bin`, `usr` or `etc` inside a longer path** (#2054): system directories now count only when they start a path, so commands such as `~/.claude/bin/...`, `./bin/rails` and `/opt/homebrew/bin/gh` run again. The check also catches the `/private/etc` and `/proc/<pid>/root/etc` aliases, which previously let a read-only tool read `/private/etc/hosts` on macOS without a dialog, as well as quoted and combined short options, brace lists and parameter expansions, and it stays fast on very long input (by @Faq, @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Review edits in attached content roots** (#2048): in PyCharm and other JetBrains IDEs, Edit/Write on a file in a content root outside the project base directory now opens the review diff instead of a plain permission dialog, and diff actions and undo work for these files. The project boundary is the base directory plus every loaded content root, and multi-project routing uses the same boundary (by @yuansezx, @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Convert SDK sessions on Windows** (#1745, #2053): conversion read the session file through a second handle while holding an exclusive lock on it, which Windows rejects, so every conversion failed. The backup and rewrite now read through the locked file itself, and a failed conversion only restores a fully written backup, so it can no longer replace the session with an empty file (by @Faq) - **Clear stale Claude plan usage** (#1737, #2045): for Claude CLI login, the plan-usage bar stops showing a figure once its window has reset and clears when the active Claude provider changes, instead of keeping the old number or the previous account's usage until the CLI sends a new event (by @EeeasyCode) - **Read version info from npm 12** (#2003, #2037): Dependency Management now understands the array-wrapped output of `npm view` in npm 12, so SDK version information is read correctly again (by @EeeasyCode) - **Open the tool window on IJent file systems without file locking** (#2060, #2061): when a remote file system cannot lock files, the failed lock is now treated as an ordinary I/O error, so the chat window opens instead of crashing at startup. Settings and MCP changes that need the lock still fail without writing anything (by @nikitasnv) - **Apply a custom chat background to the whole page** (#1832, #2065): the band around the status panel used to keep the startup theme color after a custom chat background was chosen; the page root now follows the chosen background too (by @hebulin) - **Keep collapsed Settings sidebar tooltips readable on the light theme**: tooltip text used to fall back to white on a light background and now uses the theme's primary text color (by @zhukunpenglinyutong)  中文：  ✨ 新功能 - **Claude 模型列表新增 Fable 5.2**：Fable 5.2 成为内置 Claude 模型中的首选，并配置了 200K 与 `[1m]` 上下文窗口、计费、最高到 `max` 的推理强度以及模型映射；Fable 5.1 移到上一代位置。之前保存的 Fable 5 选择会迁移到 Fable 5.2，不会再回退到 Sonnet；以 `claude-fable-5` 为 ID 的自定义模型仍可正常使用，也不会被标记为已退役（by @zhukunpenglinyutong） - **支持在 WSL 路径打开的项目中运行 Codex**（#2013、#2050）：项目通过 `\\wsl.localhost\<distro>\...` 路径打开且 node 运行在 WSL 中时，发送消息以及计划、审查、压缩、预连接与释放等控制请求都会向 Codex 传入 Linux 工作目录，不再报 `spawn ... ENOENT`。CLI 设置页也能识别安装在 WSL 内的 Codex，不再显示“未安装”（by @Faq、@zhukunpenglinyutong） - **回放更多原生 Codex exec 历史**（#2016）：回放在不执行脚本的前提下读取字面量参数、对象展开、字符串拼接以及 const 或解构出的工具别名，并把 `Promise.all`/`allSettled` 的 map 回调和带 await 的 `for-of` 循环拆分为每条命令一个结果，按原生回执逐一对应。yield 出的单元会保持打开，直到宿主报告最终响应；录制的动态工具调用会显示出来；成功的自动审批不再产生多余内容。可能不会执行的调用保留原有包装，const 分析改为每个脚本只做一次，单条大型记录的耗时从 80 多秒降到 1 秒以内（by @gadfly3173、@saligia3173、@zhukunpenglinyutong）  🔧 优化 - **CLI Provider 去掉 Beta 标记**：Grok、Kimi、OpenCode、PI、OMP、DeepSeek Harness、MiniMax Code 与 ZCode 在 Provider 选择器和设置页中不再显示 Beta 标签；选择后立即切换，不再弹出一次性的 Beta 提示（by @zhukunpenglinyutong） - **Codex 回合底栏恢复显示，并记录 MCP 文件编辑**：用过工具的回合重新显示耗时、Token 与费用底栏。通过 MCP 工具（包括 JetBrains 的 `replace_text_in_file`、`create_new_file` 与 `apply_patch`）做的文件编辑现在会出现在编辑列表中。只有完整描述了改动效果的编辑才能撤销；覆盖写入、忽略大小写的替换、其他服务器的编辑以及相对路径会列出，但 diff 只读。撤销与 diff 拒绝遵循同一规则，Claude 会话中的 MCP 编辑也按此处理（by @zhukunpenglinyutong） - **Codex CLI 版本过旧时只提示一次**：低于 0.160.0 的 CLI 现在会在每个聊天窗口提示一次，不再默默失败；0.123.x 报出的 `unknown variant auto_review` 错误会显示本地化诊断，说明如何升级 CLI 或切换权限模式。CLI 对目录外模型每个回合都会重复发出的“回退模型元数据”警告，不再弹出提示（by @zhukunpenglinyutong） - **说明旧版 Windows 上 Claude Code 无法启动的原因**（#1928、#2052）：退出码 0xC0000139（也可能显示为 `-1073741511`）现在会显示本地化诊断卡片，说明 Claude Code 2.1.118 及以后版本需要 Windows 10 1809 或 Windows Server 2019，并给出两种解决办法：升级 Windows，或在依赖管理中把 SDK 切换到 v0.2.112 或更早版本（by @Faq、@zhukunpenglinyutong） - **代码块更易阅读**（#1908、#2039、#2047、#2064）：Dart 与 Flutter 代码块现在支持语法高亮。浅色主题使用独立的高亮配色，关键字、内置名、注释和标签的颜色加深到在代码背景上达到 4.5:1 对比度，IDE 主题在运行时切换时配色也会跟随变化（by @hebulin、@EeeasyCode） - **可通过符号链接路径打开项目文件**（#2030）：位于符号链接目录下的文件链接现在可以正常打开、跳转并显示悬浮提示；支持 `#L12-L20` 行号范围，包括含空格、非 ASCII 字符的路径和 file URI。diff 的写入与删除仍经过规范化后的项目边界检查，经由符号链接或 Windows junction 的链接无法获得项目外的写权限（by @kochpy、@zhukunpenglinyutong） - **聊天标签页按状态区分圆点颜色**：回答中显示蓝色圆点，回复完成显示绿色圆点（回复未读时保持显示），回合失败显示红色圆点，直到打开该标签页或开始新的回合。标签页标题不再被改写成 `AI1...`，也不再追加本地化的“已完成”，名称保持稳定（by @zhukunpenglinyutong）  🐛 修复 - **恢复 OMP 图标的品牌配色**：OMP CLI 的 π 标记在 Provider 选择器、设置页和历史记录中改为 omp.sh 的粉 → 紫 → 青渐变，不再显示为单色主题色；单色场景仍保留 currentColor 版本（by @zhukunpenglinyutong） - **Claude 流卡住时不再阻塞整个窗口**（#2001、#2051）：模型 API 或中转服务在流式输出中途停止发送时，回合会在 10 分钟无任何数据后失败，并给出明确的“Claude 已停止响应”错误，下一条消息会启动新的运行时，不再一直排在后面等待。主对话的工具调用执行期间计时会暂停，长命令、子代理和审批对话框都不会被误判为卡住。可通过 `CC_GUI_CLAUDE_STALL_TIMEOUT_MS` 调整时限，设为 `0` 则关闭（by @Faq） - **发送后迟迟未开始流式输出时，消息队列不再卡死**：如果一次发送在开始流式输出前就已结束或失败（例如关闭了流式输出，或错误只以状态提示的形式出现），聊天可能一直处于加载中，之后的所有消息都只能排队。现在等待窗口结束后，webview 会向后端查询会话真实的加载状态（by @zhukunpenglinyutong） - **命令中只是在较长路径里出现 `bin`、`usr` 或 `etc` 时不再被拒绝**（#2054）：系统目录只有出现在路径开头时才会被识别，`~/.claude/bin/...`、`./bin/rails`、`/opt/homebrew/bin/gh` 等命令可以重新运行。检查同时覆盖 `/private/etc` 与 `/proc/<pid>/root/etc` 别名（此前在 macOS 上只读工具可以不经对话框读取 `/private/etc/hosts`），以及带引号或组合的短选项、花括号列表和参数展开，并且在超长输入下依然很快（by @Faq、@zhukunpenglinyutong） - **附加内容根中的文件也能进入编辑审查**（#2048）：在 PyCharm 等 JetBrains IDE 中，对项目根目录之外的内容根里的文件执行 Edit/Write 时，现在会打开审查 diff 而不是普通权限对话框，diff 操作与撤销也能正常使用。项目边界改为项目根目录加上所有已加载的内容根，多项目路由也使用同一边界（by @yuansezx、@zhukunpenglinyutong） - **Windows 上可以正常转换 SDK 会话**（#1745、#2053）：转换时在持有会话文件独占锁的同时又用第二个句柄读取该文件，Windows 会拒绝这种访问，导致每次转换都失败。现在备份与重写都通过已加锁的文件本身读取；转换失败时只会用完整写入的备份恢复，不会再把会话替换成空文件（by @Faq） - **清除过期的 Claude 套餐用量**（#1737、#2045）：使用 Claude CLI 登录时，套餐用量条会在窗口重置后停止显示旧数值，切换 Claude Provider 时也会清空，不再一直显示旧数字或上一个账号的用量，直到 CLI 发出新事件（by @EeeasyCode） - **兼容 npm 12 的版本信息**（#2003、#2037）：依赖管理现在能识别 npm 12 中 `npm view` 以数组包裹的输出，SDK 版本信息可以重新正确读取（by @EeeasyCode） - **在不支持文件锁的 IJent 文件系统上也能打开工具窗口**（#2060、#2061）：远程文件系统无法加锁时，加锁失败会按普通 I/O 错误处理，聊天窗口可以正常打开，不再在启动时崩溃。需要加锁的设置与 MCP 修改仍会失败，但不会写入任何内容（by @nikitasnv） - **自定义聊天背景覆盖整个页面**（#1832、#2065）：选择自定义聊天背景后，状态面板周围的边带此前仍保留启动时的主题色，现在页面根元素也会跟随所选背景（by @hebulin） - **浅色主题下折叠的设置侧边栏提示文字清晰可读**：提示文字此前会回退为白色，在浅色背景上几乎看不见，现在使用主题的主文本颜色（by @zhukunpenglinyutong）  ## What's Changed * fix(dependencies): read npm 12's array-wrapped version info by @EeeasyCode in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2037 * fix: preserve project symlink paths for file navigation only by @kochpy in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2030 * fix(webview): syntax-highlight Dart and Flutter code fences by @hebulin in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2039 * fix(usage): drop the Claude plan-usage snapshot once its window resets by @EeeasyCode in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2045 * feat(codex): replay mapped batches, yielded cells and dynamic tools by @saligia3173 in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2016 * fix(claude): explain exit code 0xC0000139 when Claude Code cannot start on Windows by @Faq in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2052 * fix(codex): support Codex in WSL when the project is opened from a WSL path by @Faq in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2050 * fix(webview): give highlighted code a readable palette on the light theme by @EeeasyCode in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2064 * Fix: prevent tool window startup failure on unsupported IJent file locking by @nikitasnv in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2061 * fix(webview): let a custom chat background repaint the page root by @hebulin in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2065 * fix(history): convert SDK sessions on Windows without a second handle on the locked file by @Faq in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2053 * fix(claude): fail a turn when the upstream stream stalls by @Faq in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2051 * fix(permission): stop denying commands that merely contain /bin/, /usr/ or /etc/ in a longer path by @Faq in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2054 * fix(permission): fix code review not triggering in multi-content-root… by @yuansezx in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2048 * Feature/v0.6.5 by @zhukunpenglinyutong in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2071  ## New Contributors * @Faq made their first contribution in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2052 * @nikitasnv made their first contribution in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2061 * @yuansezx made their first contribution in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/2048  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/compare/v0.6.4...v0.6.5
```
