### 2026-11-10T20:05:23Z [**t8y2/dbx**](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx)

```
### 新功能  - 查询结果新增可自定义的「复制当前结果集」快捷键，按默认复制格式复制已加载的可见行列，保持编辑器焦点不变 (contributed by @imcman) (PR [#14545](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14545)) - 数据网格复制菜单新增一键「复制列名（逗号分隔）」和「复制列名（单引号 + 逗号）」，可直接粘贴为 IN 列表 (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#14501](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14501)) - 列格式化新增「数字」类型，可将科学计数法显示的数值改为普通数字表示，支持千分位分隔符和固定小数位 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14551](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14551)) - SQL 编辑器工具栏新增「切换行注释」按钮，右键菜单的次要操作收进「更多」子菜单，常用操作保持直达 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14557](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14557)) - 存储过程和函数编辑新增「保存到数据库」入口，正确处理 DELIMITER 和过程体内的分号，保存后自动刷新对象列表 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#14556](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14556)) - 侧边栏本地表搜索支持 schema.table 形式，可直接定位其他 schema 下的同名表 (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#14499](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14499)) - 对象浏览器「配置显示列」支持拖拽调整列显示顺序并按连接范围持久化，也支持键盘移动 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14676](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14676)) - 数据传输将每张表的条件设置、查看和清除入口移到已选对象列表中对应表的右侧，表多时不再需要在两个列表间来回定位 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14673](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14673)) - Elasticsearch / Easysearch 连接新增独立的索引局部搜索框，按名称和别名筛选，不影响全局搜索 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14553](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14553)) - 连接深链支持插件提供的连接类型（如 type=ssh），可直接预填打开新建连接弹窗 (contributed by @Evvvvvvvan) (PR [#14678](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14678))  ### 改进  - 更新 HuaLongAI 入口链接 - 执行历史的耗时改用与查询摘要一致的执行耗时口径，多语句累加各结果耗时 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14683](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14683)) - MySQL 视图在编辑器源码标签页中按 SQL 格式化偏好排版查询部分，保留 ALGORITHM、DEFINER 等头部 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14549](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14549)) - MySQL 查询编辑器补全新增 FORCE INDEX 建议，表引用后按子句优先级排序展示 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14431](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14431)) - MongoDB 的 find / sort 输入框支持自动补齐括号和引号，字段补全优先显示当前页已有字段，不必等待远端采样 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14547](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14547)) - MySQL 对象浏览的元数据加载预算跟随连接的查询超时设置，不再固定为 5 秒 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#14488](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14488)) - Redis 大型 Hash 键的行预览改为按边界解码，打开键时不再整体格式化超大字段，查看、复制和编辑仍使用完整值 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14438](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14438)) - 表结构编辑页统一字段名、类型下拉选项和默认值的字体、字号与字重显示 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#14560](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14560)) - 悬停 SQL 编辑器书签时不再绘制额外背景，书签槽与行号槽的当前行高亮保持一致 (contributed by @heeeex100) (PR [#14493](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14493)) - 备份进度百分比四舍五入为整数显示，不再出现过长小数 (contributed by @chukui2005) (PR [#14609](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14609))  ### 修复  - 达梦 FLOAT 列按双精度读取，修复大数值只保留约 7 位有效数字、显示为近似值的问题 (contributed by @seaways1015-afk) (PR [#14684](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14684)) - MySQL 表传输携带零值日期（0000-00-00）到严格模式目标库时不再报 ERROR 1292 (contributed by @jeff377) (PR [#14681](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14681)) - PostgreSQL 系引擎标识符折叠规则不同（如 TDSQL-PG / OpenTenBase 折叠为大写）导致编辑数据提交报 42P01 的问题，改为按目录中存储的真实表名回填写目标 (contributed by @hongshuo-wang) (PR [#14607](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14607)) - 无主键 PostgreSQL 表的重复行编辑和删除改用隐藏元组定位（tableoid/ctid/xmin），不再生成可能命中多行的全列条件 (contributed by @thailoc-dev) (PR [#14596](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14596)) - PostgreSQL 类型下拉对保留字类型名（如 order）保留引号，修复由此生成非法 CREATE TABLE 的问题 (contributed by @dienaso) (PR [#14561](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14561)) - PostgreSQL 语句出错时展示底层服务器错误信息，不再只显示通用的「解析服务器响应失败」 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#13426](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13426)) - Oracle 对象树触发器列表改按基表 owner 过滤，修复跨 schema 触发器不显示的问题 (contributed by @yiqiui) (PR [#13537](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13537)) - IoTDB 查询超时设为「不限制」时显式传超大预算，长导出不再被服务端默认 60 秒终止 (contributed by @dienaso) (PR [#13993](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13993)) - MCP 只读范围策略下超过 2048 token 的 PostgreSQL 长查询不再被误判为超出数据库范围而拒绝执行 (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#14597](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14597)) - 重新执行 SQL 前确认未保存的结果修改，不再静默清空待提交的表格编辑 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#14619](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14619)) - 启用点击置顶后在结果标签页之间切换不再误取消已有的置顶状态 (contributed by @x-labb) (PR [#14631](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14631)) - 新建 schema 后查询编辑器的 schema 选择列表即时刷新，不再显示过期缓存 (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#14594](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14594)) 
```

### 2026-11-08T04:17:28Z [**JuliusBrussee/caveman**](https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman)

```
Caveman 4.1.0 is a maintenance release: bug fixes across compress, OpenCode, Hermes, Pi, the proxy and the hooks, a recovery store that no longer fills up and stops compressing, and a few small switches people asked for. caveman-compress now refuses a file with a key or token in it, and installs from skill hubs again.  ### Action needed  - **`forward_headers` under a `providers.<name>` block now stops the proxy from starting.** Before, it was silently ignored: no headers were forwarded and the only symptom was an upstream 401. Configure that upstream as a compat mount instead: `compat.<name>` with `base_url` and `forward_headers`, plus `wire_dialect: anthropic` if it speaks the Anthropic protocol (see `docs/technical/configuration.md`) (#1458). - **caveman-compress refuses a file whose text holds a credential**, such as a private key block or a cloud, Git host or model API token. Compression sends the file to your model provider, so move the secret into an env var or secret manager first. The error names the line, never the secret. Placeholders like `sk-...` or `$OPENAI_API_KEY` still compress (#1471).  ### Changed  - **Old recovery handles can now expire.** The recovery store used to keep every compressed original forever, so it filled up and compression stopped (#1370). When a write would exceed the store's cap, it now frees room: typed objects of sessions idle 14 days (archived) or 30 days (any) are expired, oldest first, until the store is at 80%. Retrieving an expired handle answers `cave_expired_recovery`, and each expiry is logged in `proxy.log`. Blob recoveries never expire (#1481).  ### New  - **`caveman-proxy recovery-store status|check|repair`** for a damaged `ccr.db`. A damaged store now refuses writes instead of staying corrupt across upgrades; reads keep working. Repair copies only rows whose bytes still match their hash, and moves the damaged files aside untouched. `caveman status` and `caveman doctor` point at it (#1369, #1481).  - **Turn off the per-prompt reminder** with `CAVEMAN_REINFORCE=0`, or `"reinforce": false` in `~/.config/caveman/config.json` or a repo's `.caveman.json`. The full ruleset is still sent at session start (#1468). - **`--hook-node node`** makes installed hooks find Node through `PATH` at run time instead of pinning the install-time path, for people who switch Node versions (#1452). The default is unchanged, because apps started outside a terminal don't see the shell's `PATH`. - **The installer falls back to Bun when Node is absent.** Node stays first (#570, #1344 by @cyrus-za). - **`caveman learn` scans Pi sessions**, and honors `PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR` / `PI_CODING_AGENT_SESSION_DIR` (#1317 by @tranducquy0). - **`caveman mem`** can revert a memory to an earlier value, and `supersede --stdin` takes replacements too large for a command line (#1386 by @PARTHDESHMUKH2005). The client checks that the installed binary supports `--stdin` first, so an older binary can no longer store the literal text `--stdin` as the fact.  ### Fixed  **caveman-compress** - Hermes' skill hub blocked the install with a DANGEROUS verdict that `--force` can't override. Every finding was a false match, and the matched text is respelled with identical behavior (#1464). - Backups are keyed on the file's full path, so two projects' `CLAUDE.md` no longer share one backup slot. A run that dies before writing removes its backup and staging file, so the next run doesn't abort. - Validation now refuses commentary appended after the body (#1433). The prompt keeps the verb that carries an instruction (#1434). Frontmatter stays local on both passes (#1359 by @leninworld). Trailing punctuation on URLs and paths no longer fails validation (#1356 by @Pharex38). Credential dotfiles and bare dotfiles like `.env` are refused by name (#1357 by @Pharex38, #1363).  **OpenCode** - OpenCode 2: the shrink plugin is generated for the API the installed major actually loads (#1445). `OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR` is honored everywhere (#1441). Deleted sessions are acted on (#1444), and the host's project directory is passed through (#1443). - The installer's already-installed probe reads JSONC `settings.json` correctly, and the plugin honors a repo `defaultMode: "off"` (#1430, #1431 by @Quinnod345).  **Hermes** - `caveman enable hermes` writes YAML that still parses (#1405). It reads ownership by value, so a reformatted config stays owned (#1410). It honors `plugins.enabled` / `plugins.disabled` membership (#1442, #1446 by @Ha1baraA11).  **Pi** (`@caveman-ai/pi` 0.3.2) - Per-prompt context is sent as a message, so the provider's prompt cache survives each prompt (#1364, #1376 by @worldofgeese). - Tool outputs under 4 KB are no longer replaced by a retrieval handle (#1365, #1378 by @danieldutu). `caveman_retrieve` with a query now says it returns an excerpt (#1368, #1377 by @danieldutu). - Pi's virtual `router/auto` model no longer warns that it is unroutable (#1449). The compat route is verified for an OpenAI base URL without `/v1` (#1325 by @szsunyuan).  **Proxy, stats and cost** - Claude Code requests that declare the advisor tool are priced again, instead of every request showing "Unknown" cost (#1465). Streaming Anthropic responses read their outcome qualifiers from the right place (#1453). - A completed Codex stream closed by the client keeps its token counts (#1397, #1398 by @Ha1baraA11). - `caveman start` reads the proxy's top-level `mode`, not a nested `middleware.mode` (#1457). - A compat upstream blocked by the SSRF guard now answers `cave_upstream_blocked` and logs the host plus the `CAVE_SSRF_ALLOWLIST` fix, instead of a generic 502 (#1440). - `caveman stats` no longer times out on large stores: per-request cache digests moved to their own table, migrated in the background (#1371, #1481). On a timeout it names the database size and suggests `--days 7`. - Native runtime: CCR writes no longer re-scan the whole store, which in large homes made native requests run out their 250 ms budget and close empty (#1279). Overlong `native.sock` paths are reported (#1399, #1402 by @Ha1baraA11). - Recovery bytes that don't match their handle are refused (#1373 by @zsltg). The recovery store's page cap applies on every connection, and its writes are serialized (#1374, #1375 by @zsltg). Stats windows are indexed (#1372 by @zsltg). Bedrock ARN model ids keep their encoded slash (#1345 by @AlSh007). A middleware response is not marked over the limit at a quarter of it (#1367 by @AlSh007). Kimi K3 effort is mapped (#1306 by @ysntony). The wrap plugin's `caveman_retrieve` results are no longer recompressed (#1432). - Claude OAuth token refresh is relayed through the proxy. Routed Gemini pins API-key auth instead of exiting 41. - The pricing catalog was re-verified against vendor pages (no price changed). Vertex `gemini-2.5-pro` and `gemini-2.5-flash` were removed: Google retired both on 2026-10-20, and those requests now show as unpriced rather than mispriced (#1473).  **Hooks and installer** - Statusline setup keeps literal `$` in `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` paths (#1400, #1401 by @Ha1baraA11). Managed hook entries are compared ignoring key order, so `doctor --fix` stops fighting a reordered entry (#1437, #1420 by @jamestrandung). Uninstall no longer deletes a `settings.json.bak` it didn't create (#1330 by @AlSh007). - Codex PostCompact hook output is valid (#1467 by @mauhpr). Cursor stops always injecting skill files from `AGENTS.md` (#1411 by @minhhung2556). Hook downloads are bounded and pinned to HTTPS (#1269 by @tejgokani). - `ENABLE_TOOL_SEARCH` defaults to `"true"` to match Claude Code (#1362 by @AliReza99).  **Engine, shrink and learn** - Escaped pipes stay inside Markdown table cells (#1456 by @kevin9327). Integers above 2^53 stay off the numeric range track (#1332 by @AlSh007). Elision notes are placed stably (#1343 by @CHLIN0). CR-separated terminal output is no longer truncated. - `caveman-shrink` skips null list entries instead of exiting (#1435, #1436 by @shaikn6), and reads Anthropic `input_schema` tools (#1331 by @AlSh007). - `caveman learn` stops reporting caveman's own injected context as text you pasted (#1459), and separates cold resumes from cache churn (#1419 by @jamestrandung).  **Agent versions** - Tested agent versions move up: Codex 0.166.0, Claude Code 2.1.318, Gemini CLI 0.67.0, OpenCode 1.19.5, Qwen Code 0.26.8, Kilo 7.8.27, OpenClaw 2026.11.1 and Hermes 0.23.0, each passing the repo's conformance run (#1474, with #1416 by @szsunyuan and #1451 by @anirudhqwerty). Pi stays at 1.0.4 for now; Pi 1.5.0 needs compatibility work (#1225).  ### Maintenance  - Security: `klauspost/compress` v1.20.3 (GO-2026-6865), `golang.org/x/net` v0.62.0 (GO-2026-6860, GO-2026-6866), and a patched Go toolchain. `@caveman-ai/middleware`'s MCP adapter now requires `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` 1.32.0 or newer (GHSA-6prh-2h8m-c8cw), and says so when it finds an older one (#1466 by @sudip28122812-creator). `filelock` is patched, and the broken `litellm` releases are excluded (#1379 by @szsunyuan). - Dependency bumps: npm dev group (#1479), golang image, pip group. - Binaries: `bin-v4.1.0`. CLI: `@caveman-ai/cli` 4.1.0. Pi: `@caveman-ai/pi` 0.3.2.  ### Packages  - [Signed binaries, module index and container: `bin-v4.1.0`](https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman/releases/tag/bin-v4.1.0) - [npm CLI: `@caveman-ai/cli@4.1.0`](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@caveman-ai/cli/v/4.1.0) - [npm Pi extension: `@caveman-ai/pi@0.3.2`](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@caveman-ai/pi/v/0.3.2) - [npm middleware: `@caveman-ai/middleware@1.1.0`](https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman/releases/tag/middleware-ts-v1.1.0) - [PyPI middleware: `caveman-middleware==1.0.1`](https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman/releases/tag/middleware-python-v1.0.1) 
```

### 2026-11-06T11:40:09Z [**tw93/Kaku**](https://github.com/tw93/Kaku)

```
<div align="center">   <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tw93/Kaku/main/assets/logo.png" alt="Kaku Logo" width="120" height="120" />   <h1 style="margin: 12px 0 6px;">Kaku V0.29.0</h1>   <p><em>A fast, out-of-the-box terminal built for AI coding.</em></p> </div>  ### Changelog 1. **Tabs Needing You**: Cmd+J jumps to the next tab with its dot lit, so an agent waiting on you is one key away. 2. **Phone Remote**: On a phone the remote page lists tabs in a side drawer with each agent's topic and status, keeps the key row at hand with a keyboard key, and no longer raises the keyboard while you scroll. 3. **Agent Status in the CLI**: `kaku cli wait` and `kaku cli events` follow the status Claude Code and Codex report, so a script can wait until an agent finishes, needs you or fails.  ### 更新日志 1. **待处理标签**：按 Cmd+J 直接跳到下一个亮着圆点的标签，正在等你回复的 Agent 一键就能找到。 2. **手机远程**：手机上用侧边抽屉切换标签，并显示每个 Agent 的话题和状态，快捷键栏常驻并带键盘按钮，滑动终端也不会再弹出键盘。 3. **命令行状态**：`kaku cli wait` 和 `kaku cli events` 会读取 Claude Code 与 Codex 上报的状态，脚本可以等到 Agent 完成、需要处理或出错。  Special thanks to @sunbird89629 for the contributions to this release.  > https://github.com/tw93/Kaku

-   **[Quota Attribution Governance: Root-Fixing 503 Misattribution & Quota False-Death; Refactoring Bucket Scheduling and Priority Competition] (Fixes #3838, #3841, #3821, PR #3845, Ref #3827, #3840)**:     -   **Eradicate 503 Attribution Pollution (92.6% of all 5xx)**: Unified all internal gateway throttling predicates into the single source of truth `policy::is_internal_gateway_exhaustion`, covering the previously mis-detected `All accounts rate-limited` (the old list only matched `all accounts limited`), so local pool throttling is no longer reported as an upstream 503; converged the two other drifting implementations in `rate_limit.rs` and `token_manager.rs`. (Fixes #3838)     -   **Dedicated 403 Ban Attribution**: Added the `account_forbidden` error code with 12-language copy for `TOS_VIOLATION` / `PERMISSION_DENIED`, so a banned account is no longer conflated with capacity exhaustion after the 403→503 mapping. (Fixes #3838)     -   **Decouple Quota Exhaustion from the Adaptive Circuit Breaker**: Established the invariant that an upstream-declared quota exhaustion is an objective physical fact and must never be gated by the breaker switch. Fixed the defect where `if !config.enabled { return; }` short-circuited before the quota predicate, silently dropping `Individual quota reached` so the account was neither zeroed nor locked; added `is_quota_exhausted_or_explicit_limited` so `QuotaExhausted` accounts are intercepted even with the breaker disabled. (PR #3845, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Account Exclusion in Retry Loops**: Each protocol retry loop now maintains `attempted_accounts` and passes it into `get_token_filtered`, eliminating the P2C behavior that kept re-selecting an account that had just returned 429 based on historical quota weights, and failing fast once the pool is exhausted. (PR #3845, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Eliminate Zero-Quota High-Priority Pool Starvation (Priority Inversion Deadlock)**: Moved the target-model positive-quota filter ahead of priority convergence in `select_with_p2c`, establishing that “priority only competes among healthy accounts holding a valid positive quota for that model”; fixed the severe defect where a drained account boosted to `priority=1` still evicted every healthy full-quota account and returned “no available accounts”. (Ref #3840)     -   **Authoritative Quota Diagnostics from Upstream**: Added L1 `quotaResetTimeStamp` priority locking so all four bucket classes (Argon standalone / Ultra shared / standard Gemini / 3P) adopt the official recovery instant when exhausted; the lock source now prefers hard quotas, the false-full blind spot caused by an omitted `remainingFraction` is fixed, and the all-pool-zero-quota pass-through hole is closed. (Fixes #3841)     -   **Frontend Circuit Breaker Default Corrected**: `circuit_breaker` now initializes with `enabled: true`, preventing an accidental breaker shutdown when saving the settings page. (PR #3845) -   **[Pedal Mode Rebuilt as Top Scheduling Priority] (PR #3840, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)**:     -   **From Hard Disabling to Priority Competition**: Activating pedal mode no longer suspends other accounts via `proxy_disabled = true`; expiring accounts are instead promoted to priority `1` (default 50). This eliminates three pathologies at once: Prompt Cache invalidation from forced failover, 429s from squeezing all concurrency onto a single account, and cross-model collateral damage from account-level disabling. Existing sessions stay bound to their account to preserve cache, new sessions consume the expiring quota first, and concurrent traffic spills over automatically. (PR #3840, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)     -   **Smooth Automatic Recovery**: Once the pedal account's weekly quota is fully drained (0%) or its reset time arrives, original priorities are restored precisely from `originalPriorities`, with tolerant cleanup retained for stale suspended state left by older versions. (PR #3840, Thanks to @zepeng-jin) -   **[Timeout Contract and Stream Lifecycle Unification: Hard Truncation Removed, Four Protocols Share One Pipeline] (Fixes #3816, Fixes #3821, Ref #3763, PR #3817)**:     -   **Hardcoded Truncation Abolished**: Retired the 135s/160s hard truncation (below the measured medium p90 of 130.9s, which reversed legitimately slow requests into 504s and triggered retry storms); the first-byte baseline is restored to 180s, multimodal ceiling to 270s, and the 270s global request budget is pushed down into the pipeline, short-circuiting immediately when remaining budget is insufficient. (Fixes #3821)     -   **Codex / Legacy Converged onto the Shared Lifecycle**: `create_codex_sse_stream` and `create_legacy_sse_stream` moved from hand-rolled `select!` drivers to `ProtocolStreamHandler` adapters under `run_stream_lifecycle`, unifying 3s heartbeats, initial-TTFT grace, thinking/content phase promotion, steady-state sliding timeouts, and abnormal finalization; Gemini gained `is_thinking_active` so the thinking phase is no longer misjudged as a transition cliff. (Ref #3816, PR #3817, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Transition-Cliff Adaptive Promotion**: Fixed the failure where a deep-thinking model emits thought blocks and then goes silent while upstream buffers a monolithic `functionCall`, causing the 120s steady-state timeout to truncate the stream into a false HTTP 200 with no output; the silent window now promotes to the `Transition` phase with a 180s grace window and `: ping` keep-alives. (Fixes #3816, Thanks to @mclinyeshi)     -   **Sticky Unbinding Across All Terminal States**: `TerminalDisposition` now folds `FirstByte` / `Upload` / `GlobalBudget` into `abandon_sticky_account`, eliminating the deadlock where clients waited out multiple three-minute attempts on the same slow account. (Fixes #3821)     -   **Upload and Replay Guards**: Upload stall timeouts are decoupled from the adaptive upload budget and floored at the connect timeout; the cross-account replay threshold for large bodies is widened to 4MB with a cap of 3 attempts to suppress upstream amplification. (Fixes #3821) -   **[Model Routing and Gates: Custom Mapping Takes Top Precedence, Legacy Models Hard-Retired] (Fixes #2065, Ref #3764, #3769, #3815, PR #3787)**:     -   **Four-Stage Inbound Model Gate**: Missing model → 404 `model not exist`; custom mappings first (exact / case-insensitive / normalized / wildcard); the resolved target must exist in “downstream exposure ∪ agent tool list ∪ official catalog ∪ mapping values”; all-pool zero quota → 502 `no quota accounts`; fail-open on cold start without metadata, and gateway self-answered 404/502 go through the single-source `is_gateway_self_answer` exemption so accounts are never locked or unbound. (Ref #3764, #3769)     -   **Custom Mappings Elevated to the Very Front**: Ahead of official hot-update deprecation redirects, fixing the post-hoc `resolve_bare_tiered_model_route` rewrite into `tiered`/`medium`; the default tier selector flipped to prefer High, with in-place promotion to High on upstream 503 `MODEL_CAPACITY_EXHAUSTED`.     -   **Legacy Model Retirement with Map-Before-Judge Ordering**: Hard gates for Claude 3.x / < 4.6 and Gemini 2.x (image-generation families exempted independently), with the “map first, then judge” order locked so legacy names remain valid inbound aliases. (Ref #3815)     -   **Pool-Adaptive Dark Routing**: Removed the static `CLAUDE_TO_GEMINI` hardcoded map and added `auto:gemini-flash-low` / `auto:claude-opus` / `auto:claude-opus-4-7` adaptive targets that pick and downgrade among Opus 5.5 / 4.6 / 4.7 by live positive quota; `custom_mapping` is decoupled from `custom_mapping_expose` with a `builtin_mapping_version` seamless migration. (Fixes #2065, PR #3787, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Upstream Physical Model Convergence**: Removed the `modelVersion` override of `upstream_model` (internal build names polluted the already-arbitrated routing result); `X-Upstream-Model` is now the single authoritative source across protocols. (Ref #3658, #3654)     -   **Official Thinking Migration Alignment**: `thinkingLevel` first with `thinkingBudget` fallback; outbound fingerprint calibrated to 2.28.0 / CL 1008300195; model lists and one-click presets expose only models an account can actually use. (Ref #3751) -   **[Multimodal Self-Healing and Freshness Sliding Window: In-Place Bad-Image Repair, Responses Protocol Parity] (Fixes #3758, #3824, #3756, PR #3759, PR #3830)**:     -   **In-Place Bad-Image 400 Self-Healing**: On `400 Unable to process input image` the pipeline no longer locks the account or unbinds stickiness (preserving Prefix Cache), replacing the corrupted image with a structured placeholder and retrying in place; single-image cases record a SHA-256 fingerprint into `ImageBlacklist` (FIFO, cap 2000) for persistent immunity, while multi-image cases are replaced for the current turn only to avoid false positives. (Fixes #3758, PR #3759, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Multimodal Freshness Sliding Window Enabled by Default**: `enable_sliding_window = true` keeping the latest 5 images by default; with the window enabled the gateway entry hard-limit on accumulated images is bypassed so the pipeline can prune, preventing long Codex conversations from being killed. (Fixes #3824)     -   **Responses Protocol Parity**: Simple-log mode now recursively redacts Base64 in `input_image`, inline Data URLs, and `function_call_output`; the token estimator gained a Responses branch with vision-token accounting. (PR #3830, Thanks to @mclinyeshi)     -   **Image-Generation Watchdog Widened with Empty-Image Diagnostics**: Primary endpoint 90s, fallback 45s, total budget 135s; a protocol-agnostic empty-image diagnostic extractor means an upstream 200 without an image is no longer treated as a false success. (Fixes #3756)     -   **Cowork Auto-Compact Alignment**: Removed the blanket 1M-model exemption and the mid-tool-call block, triggering compression precisely at `effective_threshold`, backed by a 10-case dual-track control matrix unit test. (Fixes #3821, PR #3835, PR #3836, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel) -   **[Prompt Sanitization Track Separation and Inbound Security Hardening] (Fixes #3764, #3781, Ref #425, #146, PR #3746)**:     -   **Sanitization Tracks Converged to System / Content / Anchored**: The current turn only sanitizes inside `<system*>` / `<identity*>` tags while user text outside them is preserved verbatim, so single-turn translation, proofreading, and code-audit requests are no longer rewritten into an official identity; the injection-zone boundary is determined by “the last text part of the last user message”. (Fixes #3764)     -   **Dual Identity-Regex Protection and Multi-Pass Idempotence**: Bridge segments no longer span lines, with interrogative and cross-clause exemptions added, protecting `compute_prefix_hash` prefix stability and Prompt Caching. (Fixes #3764)     -   **Adaptive CTF / Security-Policy Stripping**: Injected CTF and defensive-disclaimer text is stripped on both the System and full-text tracks, code blocks are deeply isolated, and empty parts are physically removed (an empty `systemInstruction` is unregistered). (Fixes #3781)     -   **Inbound Gzip Decompression**: Mounted `RequestDecompressionLayer` after `auth` (zip-bomb defense) and before `monitor` (so auditing sees the real decoded text). (Ref #425, #146, PR #3746, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel) -   **[Account System and Enterprise Access: GCP Vertex AI, IDE 2.6.0 Switching, Multi-Link Consistency] (Fixes #3823, #3829, #3806, #3802, #3791, #3754)**:     -   **GCP / Vertex AI Enterprise Accounts**: Aligned with the official enterprise client credential system, enabling standalone `antigravity_gcp` registration and dynamic authentication; the account detail dialog gained Project ID viewing/editing and one-click auto-fetch (`loadCodeAssist` sniffing `cloudaicompanionProject`, timeout 3s→12s, wired into the global proxy pool), and the misclassification of ordinary accounts as `ENTERPRISE` is fixed. (Fixes #3823)     -   **Antigravity IDE 2.6.0+ Account Switching**: Triple-write across SQLite `state.vscdb`, the system Keyring, and file fallback, with `expires_in` / `id_token_email` field alignment; process identity collapsed into the single `classify_process` entry point that trusts only executable names/paths, preventing `taskkill /F /T` from killing VS Code; orphan `agy` language servers are swept per target. (Fixes #3762, #3770, #3778, #3829, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9, @fuyangtian2003-beep)     -   **Classic Edition Switching Completed**: The local fallback credential `jetski-standalone-oauth-token` is now written uniformly, with an atomic backup plus dual-write of `state.vscdb`. (Fixes #3829)     -   **Account Deletion Consistency**: The dispatch pool treats `accounts.json` as the sole admission list, logging orphan credential files instead of deleting them; the delete flow moves failure points to the front (commit the index before removing credential files). (Fixes #3806, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel, @dnmdngcsb)     -   **Web / Docker Links**: OAuth fixes (no duplicate Prepare, forced loopback fallback for the built-in client, dirty-state cleanup on exchange failure, resilient manual-input parsing); an opt-in “remember login credential” toggle; the update-settings endpoint now merges partial patches, fixing 400 responses and channel resets. (Fixes #3802, #3791, #3754, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel, @dandelion-promise520)     -   **DeepSeek Harness One-Click Sync**: Covers macOS / Windows / Linux, writing `cordis.patch.yml` and `.credentials.yaml`, with `antigravity-tools sync` added to the CLI; lookahead regex was replaced by a plain-text chunking state machine, eradicating Regex panics. (PR #3761, Thanks to @wakeupnotfound) -   **[Platform Stability: Linux Remote-Desktop Keep-Alive, macOS Mini HUD, UI Zoom] (Fixes #3752, #3757, #3744, Ref #3748, PR #3766)**:     -   **Linux X11/xrdp Keep-Alive**: Hooked `XSetIOErrorHandler` and rebuilt `_exit` into thread-level isolation so a remote-desktop disconnect no longer kills the 8045 proxy; GTK tray menus are dispatched to the main thread; xrdp / VNC sessions automatically enable WebKit software rendering. (Fixes #3752, #3757, Thanks to @hazerwin)     -   **macOS Mini HUD**: `switch_to_main_window` now returns to the native main thread and Mini activation uses the `Accessory` policy, removing foreground cascade jumps and cross-thread deadlocks; the bottom CSS shadow was replaced with native rounded corners. (Fixes #3744, Ref #3748)     -   **UI and Font Zoom**: Native `webview.setZoom()` on desktop with a CSS zoom fallback for the web, `Cmd/Ctrl + = / - / 0` shortcuts, 85%~150% presets, and a double guard that isolates Mini from zoom changes. (PR #3766, Thanks to @nicolastinkl)     -   **Tray and Interaction**: New `tray_click_action` preference, 5px ultra-thin scrollbars that fade in on hover, and a temporary APIKEY.FUN Double 11 badge (with a `bump-version` expiry reminder). (Ref #3748) -   **[Performance and Observability: Tool Signature Cache Rebuild, Token Dual-Perspective Stats, QoS Deadlock Fix] (PR #3765, PR #3774, Fixes #3810, #3749, #3783)**:     -   **Tool Signature Cache Rebuild**: L1 moved from `Mutex<HashMap>` to sharded `DashMap` with capacity raised 500→10000; a new `ag-tool-sig-writer` background thread (bounded queue, up to 512 rows per transaction) plus `PERSIST_SEQ` two-level watermarks prevents dirty-signature resurrection; the read path batches `IN` queries with a 4-connection read-only pool; a new `CacheProfile` adapts to CPU, memory, and deployment shape. (PR #3765, PR #3774, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)     -   **Token Dual-Perspective Stats + TTFT**: `token_usage` gained `requested_model` / `ttft_ms`, separating the “actual routed model” from the “requested model” with automatic historical migration; first-token latency is integrated across the overview, deep-insight, and classic dashboards, and the control bar was redesigned for minimalism. (Fixes #3810)     -   **QoS Fixes and Switch**: Fixed the DashMap same-thread read/write self-deadlock in the expiry sweeper (two-phase collect-then-swap); added `qos_auto_jail` (default off) which clears the whole pool's jails and skips strike evaluation when disabled. (Fixes #3749, #3783, Thanks to @TakimotoUki)     -   **True TTFT Closure**: `StreamTtftCapture` backfills the pure first-chunk latency of the effective endpoint after the stream completes, with frontend guards rejecting `<= 0`; Gemini stream interruptions now relay the real `finishMessage` and `gatewayError`. (Fixes #3813) -   **[Scheduling Stickiness and 3P Quota Governance] (Fixes #3789, #3809, #3795, #3750, #3797)**:     -   **Multi-Agent Root Session Normalization**: `extract_root_session_id` trims derived suffixes such as `:step:N` / `:agent:...` / `--step-N` so the affinity key collapses onto the root session; added `AffinityScope::ApiKey` for credential-level strong stickiness (`ANTIGRAVITY_SCHEDULING_AFFINITY_SCOPE`). (Fixes #3789)     -   **3P Phantom Quota Governance**: The generic 3P quota group is stripped when an account has no Claude/GPT entitlement, rendering `-` placeholders instead of a false 100%; the default quota refresh interval moved from 15 to 10 minutes. (Fixes #3809)     -   **Argon / Ultra Convergence**: Unified `hasAccountArgonCapability` and `checkArgonEligibility` criteria; Ultra legally inherits the shared Gemini quota pool, fixing scheduling 503s and hidden badges. (Fixes #3750)     -   **Hub Fingerprint and Gate Diagnostics**: The official Hub fingerprint was upgraded to 2.28.0 / CL 1008300195 with asynchronous Headless detection; 12-language gateway error diagnostics and a settings-save fix were added. (Fixes #3797, Ref #3750) -   **[Release Notes] (Ref #3845)**: This is the stable promotion of the 5.0.3 pre-release line (`beta.0` ~ `beta.5`) and is functionally identical to the latest beta; `v5.0.3-beta.4` never produced a Release because of a transient GitHub API 500 in the publish step, and its fix shipped with the following versions. Thanks to every contributor in this cycle: @cubelikeplayDaniel, @mclinyeshi, @modded-soldier-9, @zepeng-jin, @fuyangtian2003-beep, @wakeupnotfound, @wthsama, @nicolastinkl, @hazerwin, @ddmixi, @dandelion-promise520, @dnmdngcsb, @EricZhou05, @Ran-Xing, @TakimotoUki, @W2514, @lbjlaq.  ---  -   **[配额归因治理：根治 503 污染与配额假死，重构大桶调度与优先级竞争] (Fixes #3838, #3841, #3821, PR #3845, Ref #3827, #3840)**:     -   **根除 503 归因污染（占全量 5xx 的 92.6%）**: 网关内部限流判据统一收敛至 `policy::is_internal_gateway_exhaustion` 单一权威源，补全此前漏判的 `All accounts rate-limited`（旧列表只写 `all accounts limited`），本地号池限流不再被误报为上游 503；同步收敛 `rate_limit.rs` 与 `token_manager.rs` 中另两处漂移实现。(Fixes #3838)     -   **403 封禁专用归因**: 为 `TOS_VIOLATION` / `PERMISSION_DENIED` 新增 `account_forbidden` 错误码与 12 语言文案，账号封禁不再被 403→503 映射后与容量枯竭混淆。(Fixes #3838)     -   **配额耗尽与自适应熔断彻底解耦**: 确立不变式「上游告知的配额耗尽是客观物理事实，绝不受熔断开关影响」。修复熔断关闭时 `if !config.enabled { return; }` 抢先拦截导致 `Individual quota reached` 被丢弃、账号既不置 0% 也不锁定的缺陷；新增 `is_quota_exhausted_or_explicit_limited`，熔断关闭时同样拦截 `QuotaExhausted` 账号。(PR #3845, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **重试循环引入账号排除机制**: 各协议重试循环维护 `attempted_accounts` 并传入 `get_token_filtered`，根治 P2C 依据历史配额权重反复锤击刚 429 账号的问题，全池遍历后快速失败。(PR #3845, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **消除 0 配额高优账号饥饿全池（优先级倒置死锁）**: 将 `select_with_p2c` 的配额过滤前置于优先级收敛，确立「优先级只在具备该模型有效正配额的健康账号之间竞争」；修复临期账号被置 `priority=1` 后配额归零仍剔除全部满额账号并报「无可用账号」的严重缺陷。(Ref #3840)     -   **配额诊断采信上游权威信号**: 新增 L1 上游 `quotaResetTimeStamp` 优先锁定，四类配额桶（Argon 独立桶 / Ultra 共享大桶 / 普通 Gemini 大桶 / 3P 桶）耗尽时直接采信官方恢复时刻；锁定源按硬配额优先排序，修复上游省略 `remainingFraction` 时被读成满额的假满额盲区，并封堵全池 0 配额裸奔放行漏洞。(Fixes #3841)     -   **前端熔断默认值修正**: `circuit_breaker` 初始默认改为 `enabled: true`，避免在设置页点击保存时意外关闭熔断器。(PR #3845) -   **[蹬额度模式重构为最高调度优先级] (PR #3840, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)**:     -   **解除暴力禁用，改为优先级竞争**: 激活时不再通过 `proxy_disabled = true` 挂起其他账号，改为将临期账号优先级提升至 `1`（默认 50），彻底消除 Prompt Caching 击穿、并发挤压唯一账号触发 429、以及账号实体级禁用造成跨模型误伤三重顽疾；老会话留在原账号保住缓存，新会话优先消耗临期额度，并发自动外溢。(PR #3840, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)     -   **平滑自动恢复**: 待蹬账号周配额耗尽（0%）或到达重置时间后，按 `originalPriorities` 精准恢复初始优先级，并保留旧版本残留挂起状态的容错清理。(PR #3840, Thanks to @zepeng-jin) -   **[超时契约与流式生命周期统一：解除硬截断、四协议共用一条流水线] (Fixes #3816, Fixes #3821, Ref #3763, PR #3817)**:     -   **废除硬编码截断**: 下线 135s/160s 硬截断（低于 medium 实测 p90 130.9s，会把本可成功的慢长请求反向判为 504 并引发重试风暴）；首字基准恢复 180s、多模态上限 270s、全局请求预算 270s 统一下沉至 pipeline，剩余预算不足时直接短路。(Fixes #3821)     -   **Codex / Legacy 收敛至统一生命周期**: `create_codex_sse_stream` 与 `create_legacy_sse_stream` 由手写 `select!` 改为 `ProtocolStreamHandler` 适配器，统一交由 `run_stream_lifecycle` 接管 3s 心跳、首字宽限、思考/正文阶段跃迁、稳态滑动超时与异常收尾；Gemini 补齐 `is_thinking_active`，思考期不再被误判为过渡断崖。(Ref #3816, PR #3817, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **过渡断崖自适应跃迁**: 修复深思考模型输出思考块后上游整包缓冲巨型 `functionCall` 引发长静默、被 120s 稳态超时截断导致 HTTP 200 无输出的硬伤，静默期自动跃迁至 `Transition` 阶段并给予 180s 宽限与 `: ping` 保活。(Fixes #3816, Thanks to @mclinyeshi)     -   **终态全量解绑会话粘性**: `TerminalDisposition` 将 `FirstByte` / `Upload` / `GlobalBudget` 全部纳入 `abandon_sticky_account`，消除卡在同一慢账号反复等满多轮 3 分钟的死锁。(Fixes #3821)     -   **上传与重放护栏**: 上传停滞超时与自适应上传预算解耦并保底不低于连接超时；大请求体跨账号重放门限放宽至 4MB、上限 3 次，抑制上行放大。(Fixes #3821) -   **[模型路由与门禁：自定义映射最高优先级、旧版模型硬下线] (Fixes #2065, Ref #3764, #3769, #3815, PR #3787)**:     -   **入站模型门禁四道判定**: 缺失 model → 404 `model not exist`；自定义映射优先（精确 / 大小写 / 规范化 / 通配符）；映射结果须存在于「下游开放列表 ∪ agent 工具列表 ∪ 官方目录 ∪ 映射值」；全池无正配额 → 502 `no quota accounts`；冷启动无元数据时 fail-open，网关代答的 404/502 走 `is_gateway_self_answer` 单源豁免，不再锁号或解绑粘性。(Ref #3764, #3769)     -   **自定义映射升为物理最前位**: 高于官方热更新淘汰重定向，修复映射命中后被 `resolve_bare_tiered_model_route` 二次篡改为 `tiered`/`medium` 的缺陷；默认档位翻转为 High 优先，上游 503 `MODEL_CAPACITY_EXHAUSTED` 时原地升档自愈。     -   **旧版模型硬下线与先映射后判定**: Claude 3.x / < 4.6 与 Gemini 2.x 硬门禁（生图族独立豁免），并锁定「先映射后判定」顺序，允许旧名作为入站别名指向合规模型。(Ref #3815)     -   **号池自适应暗路由**: 删除 `CLAUDE_TO_GEMINI` 静态硬编码，新增 `auto:gemini-flash-low` / `auto:claude-opus` / `auto:claude-opus-4-7` 自适应目标，依据实时正配额在 Opus 5.5 / 4.6 / 4.7 间择优降级；`custom_mapping` 与 `custom_mapping_expose` 解耦并引入 `builtin_mapping_version` 无感迁移。(Fixes #2065, PR #3787, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **上游物理模型收敛**: 移除 `modelVersion` 覆写 `upstream_model` 的逻辑（上游内部构件名会污染已仲裁好的路由结果），统一以各协议 `X-Upstream-Model` 为唯一权威源。(Ref #3658, #3654)     -   **官方思考迁移对齐**: `thinkingLevel` 优先、`thinkingBudget` 兜底，出站指纹校准至 2.28.0 / CL 1008300195，列表与一键配置仅暴露账号真实可用模型。(Ref #3751) -   **[多模态自愈与保鲜滑窗：坏图就地修复、Responses 协议补齐] (Fixes #3758, #3824, #3756, PR #3759, PR #3830)**:     -   **坏图 400 就地自愈**: 识别 `400 Unable to process input image` 后不锁号、不解绑粘性以保护 Prefix Cache，原地替换结构化占位符并即时重试；单图场景写入 SHA-256 至 `ImageBlacklist`（FIFO 上限 2000）持久免疫，多图场景仅做当轮替换防误杀。(Fixes #3758, PR #3759, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **多模态保鲜滑窗默认开启**: `enable_sliding_window = true`、默认保留最新 5 张图；开启滑窗时豁免网关入口多图硬拦截，交由管线裁剪，避免 Codex 长对话误杀。(Fixes #3824)     -   **Responses 协议补齐**: 简要日志对 `input_image` / Data URL / `function_call_output` 递归 Base64 脱敏，Token 估算器新增 Responses 分支与视觉 Token 折算。(PR #3830, Thanks to @mclinyeshi)     -   **生图看门狗放宽与空图诊断**: 首选端点 90s、备选 45s、总预算 135s；新增协议无关的空图诊断提取器，上游 200 但无图不再视为假成功。(Fixes #3756)     -   **Cowork 自动压缩对齐**: 移除 1M 模型一刀切豁免与工具轮次阻断，按 `effective_threshold` 精准触发，并补齐 10 次双轨对照实验矩阵单测。(Fixes #3821, PR #3835, PR #3836, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel) -   **[提示词清洗分轨与入站安全加固] (Fixes #3764, #3781, Ref #425, #146, PR #3746)**:     -   **清洗轨道收敛为 System / Content / Anchored**: 当前轮只清洗 `<system*>` / `<identity*>` 标签内部，标签外用户正文逐字保留，单轮翻译、校对、代码审计不再被改写为官方身份；注入区边界按「最后一条 user 消息的最后一个 text part」裁定。(Fixes #3764)     -   **身份正则双重防护与多轮幂等**: 桥接段不跨行，新增疑问句与跨子句连词豁免，保护 `compute_prefix_hash` 的前缀稳定性与 Prompt Caching。(Fixes #3764)     -   **CTF / 安全策略自适应剥离**: 在 System 轨与全文本轨正则剥离注入的 CTF/安全免责声明，代码块深度隔离，空 Part 自动剔除、空 `systemInstruction` 物理注销。(Fixes #3781)     -   **Gzip 入站解压**: 挂载 `RequestDecompressionLayer`，置于 `auth` 之后（防解压炸弹）、`monitor` 之前（保证审计拿到真实明文）。(Ref #425, #146, PR #3746, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel) -   **[账号体系与企业接入：GCP Vertex AI、IDE 2.6.0 切号、多条链路一致性] (Fixes #3823, #3829, #3806, #3802, #3791, #3754)**:     -   **GCP / Vertex AI 企业账号支持**: 对齐官方企业客户端凭据体系，支持 `antigravity_gcp` 独立注册与动态鉴权；账号详情新增 Project ID 查看/修改与「自动获取」（`loadCodeAssist` 嗅探 `cloudaicompanionProject`，超时 3s→12s 并接入全局代理池），修复普通账号被误标 `ENTERPRISE` 的判据。(Fixes #3823)     -   **Antigravity IDE 2.6.0+ 切号**: SQLite `state.vscdb` + 系统 Keyring + 文件三级双写，字段对齐 `expires_in` / `id_token_email`；进程身份判定收敛为 `classify_process` 单一入口，只认可执行文件名/路径，杜绝 `taskkill /F /T` 误杀 VS Code；孤儿 `agy` 语言服务按目标隔离清理。(Fixes #3762, #3770, #3778, #3829, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9, @fuyangtian2003-beep)     -   **经典版切号补全**: 统一写入 `jetski-standalone-oauth-token` 本地降级凭据，并原子备份 + 双写 `state.vscdb`。(Fixes #3829)     -   **账号删除一致性**: 调度池以 `accounts.json` 索引为唯一准入清单，孤儿凭据文件只告警不删；删除流程失败点前置（索引先落盘再删文件）。(Fixes #3806, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel, @dnmdngcsb)     -   **Web / Docker 链路**: OAuth 修复（禁止重复 Prepare、内置客户端强制 loopback 回退、换码失败主动清理脏状态、手动输入弹性解析）；新增「记住登录凭据」；更新设置接口改为局部补丁合并，修复返回 400 与通道被重置。(Fixes #3802, #3791, #3754, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel, @dandelion-promise520)     -   **DeepSeek Harness 一键同步**: 覆盖 macOS / Windows / Linux，写入 `cordis.patch.yml` 与 `.credentials.yaml`，CLI 新增 `antigravity-tools sync`；移除 lookahead 正则改用纯文本分块状态机，根治 Regex panic。(PR #3761, Thanks to @wakeupnotfound) -   **[平台稳定性：Linux 远程桌面保活、macOS Mini 视窗、界面缩放] (Fixes #3752, #3757, #3744, Ref #3748, PR #3766)**:     -   **Linux X11/xrdp 保活**: 挂载 `XSetIOErrorHandler` 并重构 `_exit` 线程级隔离，远程桌面断开不再秒杀 8045 反代；GTK 托盘菜单调度至主线程；xrdp / VNC 环境自动启用 WebKit 软件渲染。(Fixes #3752, #3757, Thanks to @hazerwin)     -   **macOS Mini 视窗**: `switch_to_main_window` 统一回到原生主线程，Mini 唤起统一 `Accessory` 策略，消除前台级联跳出与跨线程死锁；底部 CSS 阴影改为原生圆角。(Fixes #3744, Ref #3748)     -   **界面与字体缩放**: 桌面端 `webview.setZoom()`、Web/CSS zoom 回退，支持 `Cmd/Ctrl + = / - / 0` 与 85%~150% 档位，Mini 双重守卫隔离缩放。(PR #3766, Thanks to @nicolastinkl)     -   **托盘与交互**: 托盘左键行为偏好 `tray_click_action`、5px 超细滚动条移入渐显、APIKEY.FUN 双 11 临时活动标签（含 `bump-version` 到期自动提醒）。(Ref #3748) -   **[性能与可观测性：工具签名缓存重构、Token 双视角统计、QoS 死锁修复] (PR #3765, PR #3774, Fixes #3810, #3749, #3783)**:     -   **工具签名缓存重构**: L1 由 `Mutex<HashMap>` 改 `DashMap` 分片、容量 500→10000；新增 `ag-tool-sig-writer` 后台线程（有界队列 + 单事务 512 条）与 `PERSIST_SEQ` 双层水位防脏签名复活；读路径批量 `IN` 查询 + 4 条只读连接池；新增 `CacheProfile` 按 CPU/内存/部署形态自适应。(PR #3765, PR #3774, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)     -   **Token 统计双视角 + TTFT**: `token_usage` 新增 `requested_model` / `ttft_ms`，区分「实际路由模型」与「请求模型」并自动迁移历史数据；总览、深度洞察与经典看板全量集成首字延迟，控制栏极简重构。(Fixes #3810)     -   **QoS 修复与开关**: 修复到期巡检 DashMap 同线程读写自死锁（两阶段读取 + 置换）；新增 `qos_auto_jail`（默认关闭），关闭时清零全池小黑屋并跳过 Strike 判定。(Fixes #3749, #3783, Thanks to @TakimotoUki)     -   **真实 TTFT 闭环**: `StreamTtftCapture` 在流结束后回填生效端点的纯净首包耗时，前端拦截 `<= 0` 防负数展示；Gemini 流中断透传 `finishMessage` 与 `gatewayError` 真实原因。(Fixes #3813) -   **[调度粘性与 3P 配额治理] (Fixes #3789, #3809, #3795, #3750, #3797)**:     -   **多智能体根会话归一化**: `extract_root_session_id` 裁剪 `:step:N` / `:agent:...` / `--step-N` 等派生后缀，粘性键归拢到根会话；新增 `AffinityScope::ApiKey` 凭据级强粘性（环境变量 `ANTIGRAVITY_SCHEDULING_AFFINITY_SCOPE`）。(Fixes #3789)     -   **3P 幽灵配额治理**: 无 Claude/GPT 授权时主动剥离通用 3P 配额组，前端渲染 `-` 占位，消除虚假 100% 满额；配额刷新默认间隔 15→10 分钟。(Fixes #3809)     -   **Argon / Ultra 收敛**: 统一 `hasAccountArgonCapability` 与 `checkArgonEligibility` 判据，Ultra 合法继承 Gemini 通用配额池，修复调度 503 与徽章隐藏。(Fixes #3750)     -   **Hub 指纹与门禁诊断**: 官方 Hub 指纹升级至 2.28.0 / CL 1008300195 并支持 Headless 异步探测；补齐 12 语种网关错误诊断与设置保存修复。(Fixes #3797, Ref #3750) -   **[发布说明] (Ref #3845)**: 本版为 5.0.3 预发布线（`beta.0` ~ `beta.5`）的正式转正，功能与此前 beta 完全一致；`v5.0.3-beta.4` 因发布流水线 GitHub API 瞬时 500 未产出 Release，其修复已随后续版本交付。感谢本轮全部贡献者：@cubelikeplayDaniel、@mclinyeshi、@modded-soldier-9、@zepeng-jin、@fuyangtian2003-beep、@wakeupnotfound、@wthsama、@nicolastinkl、@hazerwin、@ddmixi、@dandelion-promise520、@dnmdngcsb、@EricZhou05、@Ran-Xing、@TakimotoUki、@W2514、@lbjlaq。 ### 2026-11-04T06:54:44Z [**kunkundi/crossdesk**](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk)

```
**新增功能与改进：**  1. 新增终端 CLI 客户端，支持 ANSI、Kitty 和 Sixel 画面、显示器选择、局部缩放与平移、键鼠控制及 Pi 扩展，可通过 npm 安装。 2. 优化 CLI 渲染性能与自适应预览帧率，优先保持画面清晰度；新增实时 FPS 显示、鼠标锚点缩放和持续本地命令模式。 3. 桌面端新增可信设备配对与管理，支持复制、粘贴配对信息、查看设备指纹及移除信任，可按需限制 ID 连接和 IP 直连的访问设备。 4. 桌面端服务器登录与控制端会话新增设备密钥认证，完善本机身份存储、权限保护及迁移机制。  **问题修复：**  1. 修复 macOS VideoToolbox 回调死锁导致的连接卡住，完善失败会话恢复，并修正接收数据码率重复统计。 2. 修正证书错误提示，恢复证书错误后的自动重连。 3. 修复 Linux 在 NVIDIA 主显示设备上错误启用 PRIME offload 导致的窗口空白问题。 4. 修复 iOS、Android 远程画面的安全区域与缩放复位问题，完善 iOS 文件传输错误处理及多语言键盘按钮布局。 5. 修复 CLI 画面比例、鼠标报告和点击定位问题，改善终端操作体验。 6. 自动更新失败后提供重试更新与手动下载入口，完善错误状态提示。 7. 适配 Xcode 27.1，完善 iOS 签名及 Xcode Cloud 依赖构建，修复 OpenSSL 资源符号与归档问题。  **升级说明：**  1. 新桌面端需要支持设备密钥登录的信令服务，自托管部署请先升级服务端。 2. 可信设备限制默认关闭；开启后，不支持该认证的旧客户端、CLI 和移动端无法连接。 3. 迁移已绑定的设备身份时，请同时备份 ID/密码缓存与 `identity.json` 私钥文件。  ---  **New Features and Improvements:**  1. Added a terminal CLI client with ANSI, Kitty and Sixel rendering, display selection, zoom and pan, keyboard and mouse control, and a Pi extension, available through npm. 2. Improved CLI rendering performance and adaptive preview frame rates while prioritizing image clarity; added live FPS, mouse-anchored zoom and persistent local command mode. 3. Added trusted-device pairing and management on desktop, including pairing-data import and export, fingerprint inspection and trust removal, with optional access restrictions for ID and direct IP connections. 4. Added device-key authentication for desktop server login and controller sessions, with improved identity storage, permission protection and migration.  **Bug Fixes:**  1. Fixed macOS connection hangs caused by VideoToolbox callback deadlocks, improved failed-session recovery, and corrected double-counted inbound data bitrate. 2. Corrected certificate error messages and restored automatic reconnection after certificate failures. 3. Fixed blank Linux windows caused by incorrectly enabling PRIME offload when NVIDIA is the primary display provider. 4. Fixed remote viewport safe areas and zoom reset on iOS and Android, and improved iOS file-transfer error handling and localized keyboard-button layouts. 5. Fixed CLI aspect ratios, mouse reporting and click positioning for a better terminal experience. 6. Added retry and manual-download actions after automatic update failures, with clearer error status messages. 7. Updated iOS build settings for Xcode 27.1, improved signing and Xcode Cloud dependency builds, and fixed OpenSSL resource symbols and archive issues.  **Upgrade Notes:**  1. New desktop clients require a signaling server that supports device-key login. Upgrade self-hosted servers first. 2. Trusted-device restrictions are disabled by default. When enabled, older clients, CLI and mobile clients without this authentication cannot connect. 3. Back up both the ID/password cache and the `identity.json` private-key file when migrating a bound device identity.  **完整变更 / Full Changelog：** [[v1.7.4-20261030…v1.7.5-20261104](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk/compare/v1.7.4-20261030...v1.7.5-20261104)](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk/compare/v1.7.4-20261030...v1.7.5-20261104) 
```

### 2026-11-09T23:46:31Z [**Fission-AI/OpenSpec**](https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec)

```
## What's New in v1.17.0  `openspec update` now upgrades a project from any older release in one run, and announces that command files go away in OpenSpec 2.0.  > **Heads-up: command files (`/opsx:*`) go away in OpenSpec 2.0.** Your OpenSpec skills stay and do the same work. To switch now, run `openspec config set delivery skills && openspec update`. The plan is in [#2022](https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/issues/2022).  ### New  - **2.0 notice** - While a configured tool still gets command files, `openspec update` ends with a short notice that they go away in 2.0, plus the one command to switch early. It stays silent once you use skills only, and for tools without command files, such as Codex.  ### Improved  - **Upgrades from any release** - `openspec update` now upgrades a project made by any release since 0.12 in one run, without stopping to ask for `--force`. It removes old OpenSpec files nobody edited, including leftover copies that made some tools show every skill twice, and keeps any file you changed. - **Safer cleanup** - Cleanup never touches files outside your project, even when a folder inside it links somewhere else, and never removes anything when a tool fails to set up.  ### Fixed  - **Switching to skills only** - `openspec config set delivery skills` followed by `openspec update` no longer deletes command files you edited. Only files OpenSpec generated are removed; edited ones are kept and listed. - **Windows installer** - `irm https://openspec.dev/cli/install.ps1 | iex` no longer fails with `unsupported CPU` on Windows PowerShell 5.1 machines that report no CPU architecture.  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/compare/v1.16.1...v1.17.0 
```

### 2026-11-09T08:51:00Z [**longbridge/gpui-kit**](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit)

```
# GPUI Kit v0.9.1  GPUI Kit 0.9.1 is a backward-compatible patch release. It adds a draggable GridLayout, right-pinned table columns, Markdown task checkboxes, link tooltips and motion transitions that outlive their element, along with a large batch of input, text, slider, accessibility and gpui-shell hardening fixes. The default backend moves to gpui-pre 0.3.12 and the optional GPUI Fast backend to 0.1.14.  ## New APIs  - **GridLayout**: a grid whose items the user drags and resizes, also registered in the gpui-shell component catalog. ([#3770](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3770), [#3789](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3789)) - **Table**: `ColumnFixed::Right` pins columns to the right edge. ([#3833](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3833)) - **Text**: `on_task_toggle` makes Markdown task checkboxes clickable; block surfaces in `TextView`; pointer-following link tooltips. ([#3811](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3811), [#3830](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3830), [#3793](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3793)) - **Motion**: `TransitionState` for transitions that outlive their element. ([#3841](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3841)) - **Components**: `Button::selected_accent`, `Slider::track_focus`, `Dialog::accessibility_label`, `TimeField::separator`, `Toolbar::axis` for vertical toolbars, and a ratio-sized PieChart hole. ([#3775](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3775), [#3824](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3824), [#3826](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3826), [#3829](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3829), [#3821](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3821), [#3784](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3784)) - **WindowBorder**: the frame colour comes from the `window.border` theme token, and window corners can be rounded on request. ([#3849](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3849), [#3848](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3848))  ## Editor and text input  - Keep the first typed character in the editor search panel, compose decoration styles deterministically, draw a placeholder for a folded line, and show the arrow over the gutter. ([#3772](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3772), [#3777](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3777), [#3791](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3791), [#3806](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3806)) - Tell the platform when the caret moves, publish layout at the end of prepaint, notify a moved input only while focused, and wait for the first layout before shaping an early value. ([#3827](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3827), [#3831](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3831), [#3828](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3828), [#3864](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3864)) - Keep the caret's visual line at soft wraps. ([#3842](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3842))  ## Markdown and text rendering  - Supply editor foreground ranges through the render context and give span-less HTML paragraphs unique ids. ([#3754](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3754), [#3771](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3771)) - Tables: highlight the hovered body row, select aligned cell text where it is drawn, and size columns to their images. ([#3812](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3812), [#3813](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3813), [#3787](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3787)) - Limit selection highlights to selected glyphs, draw unsized inline images at their own size, and avoid quadratic paragraph highlight merging. ([#3799](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3799), [#3786](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3786), [#3846](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3846))  ## Components  - **Table / DataTable**: name rows after their cells, centre default header names, Tab and Shift+Tab leave a focused DataTable, and a selected cell gets a border distinct from hover. ([#3773](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3773), [#3731](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3731), [#3800](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3800), [#3866](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3866)) - **Slider**: allow `min` above the default `max` while building, snap dragged values to steps from `min`, and step one thumb from assistive technology. ([#3823](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3823), [#3822](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3822), [#3840](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3840)) - **Menu**: size items and default max width in rems, and open a scrollable menu on its checked item. ([#3809](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3809), [#3814](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3814)) - **Settings**: follow locale changes in the search placeholder, clear search on Escape, show an empty state for unmatched searches. ([#3815](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3815), [#3816](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3816), [#3692](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3692)) - **Motion**: reverse an interrupted transition as CSS does, and keep value-transition timing under reduced motion. ([#3820](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3820), [#3839](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3839)) - **Scrolling**: stop smooth-scrolling wheels at the edge on macOS, let the parent scroll a swipe over a fitting horizontal area, and keep the overscroll stream alive through sideways packets. ([#3781](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3781), [#3779](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3779), [#3790](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3790)) - **List, Tab, WindowBorder**: accent rows use `accent_foreground`, icon-only tabs keep their width, the element id path stays stable across decoration changes. ([#3774](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3774), [#3792](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3792), [#3783](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3783)) - **Diff**: reject invalid hunk prefixes before slicing UTF-8. ([#3473](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3473)) - **WebView**: fix an X11 abort when a WebView is dropped on Linux. ([#3862](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3862))  ## Accessibility  - Report a disabled `SliderThumb` to assistive technology. ([#3825](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3825))  ## gpui-shell security and robustness  - Require a network grant for `WebViewState` pages, accept only `"*"` as the unrestricted `execute` grant, and check `Link.href` against the URL scheme rule. ([#3782](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3782), [#3802](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3802), [#3804](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3804)) - Refuse bundle values that would break generated scripts, bound script-supplied counts, nesting depth and self-nesting views, and leave proxy URLs out of the default `process.env`. ([#3803](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3803), [#3805](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3805), [#3808](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3808), [#3817](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3817), [#3810](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3810)) - Stop a Git dependency selector being read as a `git fetch` option, honor the manifest entry when bundling, expose group hover to scripts, and shrink `materialize_node` frames. ([#3778](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3778), [#3682](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3682), [#3796](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3796), [#3807](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3807))  ## Dependencies  - Update `rustls-webpki` and `webpki-root-certs` for CVE-2026-111943. ([#3801](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3801)) - gpui-pre 0.3.12 and gpui-fast 0.1.14. Under GPUI Fast 0.1.14, views that were not notified reuse the previous frame instead of rebuilding. ### 2026-11-05T06:24:39Z [**Captain-AI-Hub/IDA-MCP**](https://github.com/Captain-AI-Hub/IDA-MCP)

```
## What's Changed  ### Fixed **Two real bugs behind "search lags and clicking a function shows nothing":**  - **Clicks were dead**: the app handled `on_list_item_selected`, but textual's ListView posts **`ListView.Selected`** — so clicking or ENTER on a function *never invoked the handler at all*. Renamed to `on_list_view_selected`; clicking/entering now actually opens the function. - **Every keystroke froze the UI**: each filter character ran a synchronous ~1s bridge round-trip on the UI thread (on a 220k-function firmware), and every function open ran disasm/decompile synchronously. Search is now **debounced (250 ms) and executed on a worker thread** with results applied back via `call_later`; function opens load their listing off-thread with a "loading" status; typing stays responsive while queries run. - The sidebar renders at most 200 rows per rebuild instead of 1000 ListItems; the count hint ("showing 200 of 220802 — keep typing") explains the rest. - The initial sidebar load also moved off the UI thread — startup no longer blocks.  Verified with real keystrokes in a headless pilot against a live 220k-function worker: filter → ENTER opens the function, `p` toggles pseudocode, `x`/`b` (xrefs/CFG) all fire.  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Captain-AI-Hub/IDA-MCP/compare/v1.1.3...v1.1.4 
```

### 2026-11-09T17:54:12Z [**AChep/keyguard-app**](https://github.com/AChep/keyguard-app)

```
This release adds experimental P2P sync for app settings and using passkeys on another device on Android.  ### New - Sync: Added experimental P2P sync for app settings. - Android: Added using passkeys on another device.  ### Improvements - Attachments: Dropping more than one file at once into the attachments box is now supported. - Passkeys: Importing p256 passkeys from Proton Pass exports is now supported. - SSH agent: Request logs older than a configured limit can now be removed automatically. - SSH agent: Clicking request log items now opens a details dialog. - Vault: Invalid TOTP raw values are now handled gracefully.  ### Security - Backups: Vault exports and backups now support the more secure .zip.age format.  ### Fixes - Android: Fixed "Unknown username" when logging in by passkey (#1669). - Android: Fixed the Termux GPG agent failing to connect (#1664). - iOS: Fixed the navigation bar covering the toolbar on iPad. - iOS: Fixed the Type picker not opening in the Login editor's generator. - iOS, macOS: Fixed a crash when opening Bitwarden 2FA or SSO screens. - iOS, macOS: Fixed a crash when leaving GPG tools or the Generator with a screen open. - iOS, macOS: Fixed picking a client certificate doing nothing. - iOS, macOS: Fixed an account with a revoked session so it shows the Sign in button. - iOS, macOS: Fixed nested folders so opening them opens the folder instead of their items. - iOS, macOS: Fixed pasting a .env file into the import field so it keeps line breaks. - iOS, macOS: Fixed the GPG generator's Custom date expiry row so it shows the chosen date. - iOS, macOS: Fixed tag suggestions that differ only in case overlapping each other. - iOS, macOS: Fixed the Lock after reboot option so it is hidden. - Desktop: Fixed the request approval window so it shows toast messages. - Backups: Fixed Bitwarden attachments so the correct plainSize is written. - Generator: Fixed generating email aliases and GPG keys so it stops for inputs that show an error. - Generator: Fixed wordlist inputs so HTML pages are refused and HTTP failures fail. - KDBX: Fixed removing a group so it keeps previously deleted objects. - SSH agent: Fixed the SSH Agent Request log badge overlapping with text (#1665). - Vault: Fixed the Trash screen so it shows trashed archived items. - Vault: Fixed importing so missing SSH key fingerprints are recovered from the source data. 
```

### 2026-11-09T16:50:17Z [**xming521/WeClone**](https://github.com/xming521/WeClone)

```
## 🎉 What's Changed  WeClone 1.0.4 tells you what a build will cost and how long it will take, before you start!  **Cost and time estimate** - The confirm step of the Build portrait page estimates a build's cost, time and number of requests. A trial sends about 10 real requests to the model service to measure them, and the build reuses their answers. - Known providers such as DeepSeek are priced at their official rates, other services at the common price across providers. For a service with off-peak prices, the estimate follows the hours a build started now would run in. - With Codex, the estimate shows tokens and time, since Codex uses your subscription quota. - A build starts after a trial of the saved model settings. The page tells you what is still missing before you can start. - While a build runs, the page shows what it has spent and the time and cost still ahead, and warns you when spending runs far past the estimate. The finished page shows what the build spent.  **Build portrait page** - Every new build starts from its own upload. Opening the page clears chats uploaded earlier, and a successful build clears the chats it used. - When Codex reaches its usage limit, the build stops and keeps the answers it already got. Build again once the quota is back.  **Personal assistants** - The Assistants and Portrait summary pages explain how cloud assistants such as dots, Muse and Grok Bot can use your portrait: import the exported summary into their memory or custom instructions, or send it and ask them to remember it.  **Logs** - In encrypted mode, build logs now show the numbers of chats, requests and tokens, still without any chat text.  ## ⚠️ Upgrade Notes - A build started from the web UI reads only the chats uploaded on the Build portrait page. The `csv_folder` in `settings.jsonc` is still read by `build-profile` on the command line.  ## 🐛 Bug fix - With `deepseek-v4-pro`, about a quarter of extraction requests were rejected and sent again because the model wrote sample IDs as `#0`. These answers are accepted now (#262).  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/xming521/WeClone/compare/v1.0.3...v1.0.4  ## 😊 更新内容  WeClone 1.0.4 在开始构建前就能告诉你大概要花多少钱、多长时间！  **费用和用时估算** - 「构建画像」页的确认步骤会估算这次构建的费用、用时和请求次数。试算会向模型服务发送约 10 次真实请求来测量，这些回答构建时直接沿用。 - DeepSeek 等已知服务商按官方价格计算，其他服务按各家服务商的常见价格估算。有错峰价格的服务，按现在开始构建会经过的时段计算。 - 使用 Codex 时显示 token 用量和用时，因为 Codex 用的是订阅额度。 - 开始构建前需要先用保存的模型设置试算一次，页面会提示还差哪些步骤。 - 构建过程中会显示已经花了多少、还需要多长时间和多少钱，实际花费远超估算时会提醒。构建完成后显示这次一共花了多少。  **构建画像页** - 每次新建构建都从重新上传开始。打开页面时会清掉之前上传的聊天记录，构建成功后也会清掉这次用过的。 - Codex 额度用完时构建会停下，已经拿到的回答都会保留，额度恢复后重新构建即可。  **个人助手** - 「助手接入」和「画像总结」页说明了 dots、Muse、Grok Bot 等云端助手怎么使用画像：把导出的画像总结导入助手的记忆或自定义指令，或者直接发给它，请它记住。  **日志** - 加密模式下，构建日志也会记录聊天、请求和 token 的数量，仍然不含任何聊天内容。  **升级说明** - 从网页开始的构建只读取在「构建画像」页上传的聊天记录。`settings.jsonc` 里的 `csv_folder` 仍供命令行 `build-profile` 读取。  **问题修复** - 使用 `deepseek-v4-pro` 时，模型把样本编号写成 `#0` 这样的形式，约四分之一的抽取请求因此被判为失败并重发。现在这类回答也能正常识别（#262）。 
```

### 2026-11-05T00:34:13Z [**jj-vcs/jj**](https://github.com/jj-vcs/jj)

```
This release fixes a dependency error that broke Linux builds in the 0.47.0 release.  ### About  jj is a Git-compatible version control system that is both simple and powerful. See the [installation instructions](https://docs.jj-vcs.dev/v0.47.1/install-and-setup/) to get started.  ### Deprecations  * The `git.sign-on-push` boolean option is deprecated in favor of the   `revsets.git-sign-on-push` revset. The default value is `none()`. If   `git.sign-on-push` was previously set to `true`, it will be migrated to   `revsets.git-sign-on-push = "~signed() & mine()"`.  ### New features  * New `revsets.git-sign-on-push` config option containing the revset of   commits to be signed when `jj git push` is run. It replaces the   `git.sign-on-push` boolean option.  ### Contributors  Thanks to the people who made this release happen!  * Philippe Daouadi (@blastrock) 
```

### 2026-11-10T21:10:07Z [**gildas-lormeau/SingleFile**](https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/SingleFile)

```
### Changes  - Added the `%switch<value|case1|result1|case2|result2|...|default>` function in the filename template: it compares `value` with each case and returns the matching result, or `default` when no case matches, e.g. `%switch<{url-hostname-domain}|bbc|BBC|imdb|IMDb|%capitalize<{url-hostname-domain}>>`; the help page documents it (#2052) - The script embedded in annotated pages to display, move, resize and collapse the notes is now built from single-file-core (`single-file-notes.js`) and minified, instead of being generated from the source of the editor - Pages using many web fonts are processed faster when the option "remove alternative fonts to the ones displayed" is enabled: the parsed `src` lists and the font details are cached (gildas-lormeau/single-file-core#12)  ### Fixes  - A page auto-saved on unload from a tab being closed is saved again: the content was received without the tab of the sender, the save failed and its entry stayed in the pending saves with the "processing" status (#2053) - The first auto-save of a browser session is removed from the pending saves when the page has been saved (the task id `0` was read as "no task"), and the auto-save entries of pages that went away without sending their content are removed when the tab is closed, displays another page, or does not answer (#2053) - The unload listener of the auto-save is registered as soon as the options are received instead of on `DOMContentLoaded` - With "auto-save after page load or on page unload" and "auto-save on tab removed" both enabled, a page left before being saved is no longer lost; with "auto-save on page unload" and "auto-save on tab removed" both enabled, closing the tab no longer saves the page twice - With "auto-save on tab discard" enabled, hiding a tab no longer leaves the `data-single-file-*` marker attributes of the capture in the page, the capture kept for a hidden tab is released when the tab is closed, and a tab discarded before its capture is received is saved - In pages saved with notes, releasing the mouse after resizing or moving a note no longer leaves the note following the pointer: the embedded script referenced a function that only exists in the editor (#2054, regression from 2023; the pages already saved must be opened in the editor and saved again to get the fixed script) - In the editor, removing the selected note no longer keeps it as the selected note, adding a note selects it, and the anchor of a note is no longer looked for in the notes or in the masks - With "Deferred content > keep the zoom level", the sizes and positions reported to the page are scaled like the page, so pages that lay out their content from them are saved whole (#1919) - The option "skip the save if the file exists" only applies to the files saved in the download folder: saves to the annotation editor, the clipboard and the companion are no longer cancelled by a file of the same name in the download history, and a skipped save no longer opens the saved page nor requests a proof of existence - Updated single-file-core to 1.12.0 
```

## Release Notes  Released on 2026-11-05.  ### Preview features  - \[`flake8-logging-format`\] Avoid behavior changes and syntax errors in fixes (`G004`) ([#27419](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/27419)) - \[`security`\] Implement `tarfile-ignore-zeros-true` ([#28680](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28680))  ### Bug fixes  - Accept a bare raise before a semicolon ([#29761](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29761)) - Allow match identifiers after a semicolon ([#29760](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29760)) - Fix panic on BOM-prefixed files in diagnostic renderer ([#30017](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30017)) - \[`flake8-simplify`\] Report collapsible `elif` clauses after a multi-statement `if` branch (`SIM102`) ([#29814](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29814))  ### Rule changes  - Show more nonprinting characters in diagnostic fix diffs ([#30062](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30062))  ### Performance  - Coalesce source slices when formatting attributes ([#29841](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29841)) - Collect return statements with a statement-only visitor ([#29911](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29911)) - \[`pyflakes`\] Defer value comparisons for repeated dictionary keys (`F601`, `F602`) ([#29739](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29739)) - Format node fields directly when comment handling is unnecessary ([#29853](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29853)) - Reduce formatter source-slice overhead ([#29855](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29855)) - Reuse a callee's qualified name across lint rules ([#29916](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29916)) - Skip invalid string character checks when the source contains none ([#29883](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29883)) - Use a Bloom filter for formatter comment lookups ([#29852](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29852)) - Use a concrete buffer for ordinary writes ([#29819](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29819))  ### CLI  - Give `NO_COLOR` precedence in Ruff and ty diagnostics ([#29898](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29898)) - Honor `CLICOLOR=0` in Ruff and ty diagnostics ([#29910](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29910))  ### Documentation  - Correct the documented `version` and `src` defaults for `ruff-action` ([#29880](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29880)) - \[`flake8-bugbear`\] Make `B014` example error out-of-the-box ([#29941](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29941)) - \[`flake8-no-pep420`\] Document preview behavior (`INP001`) ([#29764](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29764)) - \[`isort`\] Expand `I001` documentation ([#29843](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29843))  ### Other changes  - [syntax-errors] Extend `__debug__` write check to attribute targets ([#27908](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/27908))  ### Contributors  - [@fixfon](https://github.com/fixfon) - [@hirehamir](https://github.com/hirehamir) -
