### 2026-11-04T07:36:16Z [**langgenius/dify**](https://github.com/langgenius/dify)

```
**Dify 1.19.0 brings nested workflow tracing to your observability tools.** Follow a workflow into the Workflow Tools it calls, inspect the steps inside each invocation, and keep the LLM context alongside the execution tree.  This release also makes Workflow Tools portable with App packages, adds Skill Hub automation, and makes Agent files easier to upload and preview. The bundled plugin daemon is upgraded to **0.6.14-local**.  [Release highlights](#user-content-release-highlights) · [Upgrade guide](#user-content-upgrade-guide) · [Detailed changes](#user-content-detailed-changes)  <a id="release-highlights"></a>  ## Release highlights  ### Nested workflow tracing  **See what happens inside a Workflow Tool, all the way down to its LLM calls.**  Building on the Console drilldown introduced in 1.18.0, new external traces now include the persisted execution steps inside called Workflow Tools. Child steps appear beneath the Tool span that invoked them, preserving the hierarchy across nested and repeated calls. (#44673)  ![Langfuse trace tree showing a workflow, nested Middle and Child Workflow Tools, and the selected LLM call with formatted prompts and responses](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/langgenius/dify/dcfb52e62726c91b43db20f3c087acf285428df1/docs/assets/pr-44673-langfuse-nested-prompts.jpg)  *From the outer workflow to a nested LLM call: the execution tree and formatted messages, together in Langfuse. Screenshot from [#44673](https://github.com/langgenius/dify/pull/44673).*  - **Follow the hierarchy.** Expand called Workflow Tools to see their internal execution steps under the invoking Tool span. - **Understand each invocation.** Repeated calls and failures retain their place in the trace. - **Keep the LLM context.** Inspect formatted prompts and responses, token usage, and project, trace, and observation links.  > [!NOTE] > **Your saved provider settings continue to work.** The richer hierarchy applies to new executions; historical traces are not backfilled. Independently nested workflow runs remain separate traces linked by parent identifiers. For rolling deployments, follow the [tracing queue transition](#user-content-upgrade-guide) below.  ### Take Workflow Tools with your apps  Export an app and bring the workflows behind its Workflow Tools with it. `.ifpkg` packages include those workflows at their bound versions, including transitive dependencies. Import reuses tools already in the destination workspace and recreates, publishes, and registers missing ones, remapping their references. (#44592)  All Dify app exports now use **package format v2**, including apps without Workflow Tools. Existing v1 imports and explicit YAML export remain supported. See the [upgrade guide](#user-content-upgrade-guide) before importing new packages into older installations. (#45353)  ### Upload together. Preview in place.  Select or drop multiple Agent configuration files and upload them in parallel. Successful uploads are bound in one atomic batch, concurrent Composer saves preserve the latest files, and Prompt uploads keep the cursor position while inserting multiple references. (#44947)  Read PDFs inline, inspect source files with syntax highlighting, and see when text has been truncated. Previews cover configured files, Skill members, and working-directory files, with a download action when a PDF cannot be loaded. (#45032, #45045)  <details> <summary><strong>See Agent file uploads and previews</strong></summary>  ![Inline PDF preview in Agent files, with the research brief and file list](https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/22bafc75-c8f5-46fd-b7ba-c5097f9cffb6)  *Read a PDF directly in the Agent file preview and switch between project files.*  ![Agent batch upload dialog with a PDF, Python source file, and Markdown guidelines selected](https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/5eafd0c1-f46d-4966-bbc0-e6cd5eacff02)  *Select several files and upload them together.*  ![Syntax-highlighted Python source in the Agent file preview](https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/1435fd23-7489-450a-a200-dbd81c1b588d)  *Inspect source files with syntax highlighting and line numbers.*  </details>  ## Developer spotlight  **Manage workspace Skills through OpenAPI and difyctl.** Find and inspect Skills, export a draft or published version, import local files, and publish a new version from the command line. (#44941)  <details> <summary><strong>The six Skill commands</strong></summary>  | Command | Capability | | --- | --- | | `difyctl get skill` | List Skills, with keyword and tag filters. | | `difyctl describe skill` | Read details, the draft hash, and latest published version. | | `difyctl get skill version` | List published versions. | | `difyctl export skill` | Export the draft or a selected published version as ZIP. | | `difyctl import skill --files <dir>` | Create a Skill or replace an existing Skill's draft from local files. | | `difyctl publish skill` | Publish the draft as a new version. |  </details>  These operations require an account caller, the corresponding workspace permissions, and Skills to be enabled. **Import does not publish.** Importing over an existing Skill is classified as destructive; the operation catalog and CLI help distinguish read, write, and destructive operations. (#44941, #45314, #44587)  ---  <a id="upgrade-guide"></a>  ## Upgrade guide  > [!IMPORTANT] > **Read the upgrade guide before deploying.** Docker Compose now requires **v2.35.0 or later**. Preserve existing environment files, upgrade the Dify components together, and use the new tracing queue during rolling deployments. New App packages use format v2 and require a compatible destination importer. The bundled plugin daemon moves to **0.6.14-local**.  **Compatibility and rollout**  1. **Back up first.** Retain the database, vector-store data, uploaded files, Agent snapshot/workspace storage, deployment secrets, and environment files. Use a maintenance window for a single-stack upgrade. 2. **Upgrade the components together.** Use matching `1.19.0` API, worker, web, Agent Backend, and Local sandbox images, with plugin daemon `0.6.14-local`. Keep the existing Local sandbox authentication token and any configured `PASSWORD_PEPPER` unchanged. If upgrading from before 1.18.0, also follow the [1.18.0 upgrade guide](https://github.com/langgenius/dify/releases/tag/1.18.0). 3. **Use Docker Compose 2.35.0 or later.** This is the minimum supported version for root `.env` overrides to take precedence when Compose resolves service defaults. Check your installed version with `docker compose version`. (#45359) 4. **Keep tracked defaults separate from local overrides.** Bundled `docker/envs/**/*.defaults` files load automatically. Customize a service through its matching local `.env` file, using the override path documented in the defaults file; root `docker/.env` holds deployment inputs and explicit overrides. Preserve existing `.env` files and edit them in place. Review accidentally empty assignments and values pinned by older template copies before upgrading. (#45359, #45376) 5. **Existing `shared.env` files remain supported.** Common API, worker, and Beat customizations now belong in `docker/envs/core-services/api-worker.env`. Compose still reads legacy `docker/envs/core-services/shared.env` first, then `api-worker.env`, so `api-worker.env` wins when both set conflicting values. Root `docker/.env` takes precedence over both. An unchanged copy of `api-worker.defaults` preserves existing `shared.env` customizations. See the [Docker configuration guide](https://github.com/langgenius/dify/blob/laipz8200/release-1.19.0/docker/README.md#review-configuration-after-an-upgrade). (#45376) 6. **Separate tracing queues during a rolling upgrade.** New tasks default to `OPS_TRACE_QUEUE=ops_trace_v2`; API, workers, and Beat must agree. Custom `CELERY_QUEUES`, `CELERY_WORKER_QUEUES`, or `--queues` lists must include the new queue. Keep old tracing workers on `ops_trace` until old producers and all their queued, active, reserved, scheduled, and retry work have finished. Do not purge the old queue or ask the new retired handler to export legacy messages. See the [tracing rollout guide](https://github.com/langgenius/dify/blob/laipz8200/release-1.19.0/api/providers/trace/README.md#rolling-upgrade-from-the-previous-pipeline). (#44673) 7. **Upgrade package destinations before importing new App packages.** New app packages always use format v2. Older importers, including builds with the earlier strict v2 rule, may reject them. Version 1 imports remain supported on 1.19.0. Explicit YAML export remains available, but does not carry bundled Workflow Tool dependencies. (#44592, #45353)  <details> <summary><strong>Deployment instructions · Docker Compose, source, and difyctl</strong></summary>  **Docker Compose**  Once the 1.19.0 release tag and images are published, stop a single-stack deployment gracefully, switch to the release, retain and review configuration, then pull and start the new images:  bash cd dify/docker docker compose stop -t -1 git fetch --tags git checkout 1.19.0 # Merge required configuration changes into existing env files here. docker compose pull docker compose up -d   Keep API startup migrations enabled (`MIGRATION_ENABLED=true`), or apply the full migration chain through your managed migration process before starting new workers. For rolling deployments, follow the separate tracing-queue sequence instead of mixing old and new consumers.  Configure shared plugin credentials, `PLUGIN_DAEMON_KEY` and `PLUGIN_DIFY_INNER_API_KEY`, in root `docker/.env` so the daemon and its clients receive matching values. Ports, volume paths, profiles, and health-check scheduling also remain root configuration inputs. See the [Docker configuration guide](https://github.com/langgenius/dify/blob/laipz8200/release-1.19.0/docker/README.md#configure-dify). (#45359)  **Source deployments**  Use Python **3.13**, Node.js **24.22.1 or later in the 24.x line**, and pnpm **12.16.1**. pnpm no longer provisions Node.js for the workspace, so install the required Node.js runtime separately. (#44976, #45094)  From the repository root:  bash uv sync --project api --frozen uv run --directory api flask db upgrade pnpm install --frozen-lockfile   Rebuild the web application and restart API, worker, scheduler, and Agent services from the same release. Refresh the standalone Agent Backend's locked `server` dependencies when deploying it separately.  **difyctl**  Install the matching 1.19.0 client and use `difyctl help` to discover the current server catalog. Refresh installed agent skills with `difyctl install skills <dir>`. Workflow and Chatflow automation should use the revised `stop.run` operation; message-based apps use `stop.task`.  </details>  <details> <summary><strong>Database migrations and existing data</strong></summary>  Upgrading from 1.18.0 applies **six migration revisions**, ending at **`53c10126d551`**. Apply the complete chain through the normal migration runner.  Operational changes to plan for include:  - **Storage-cleanup indexes (`d7e9a1c3f502`).** Add reference indexes on `message_files.upload_file_id`, `workflow_node_execution_offload.file_id`, and `workflow_draft_variable_files.upload_file_id`. PostgreSQL creates them concurrently, but index construction still scans data and consumes I/O. Online upgrades reuse matching healthy indexes and reject conflicting or invalid definitions. Investigate an interrupted invalid build before retrying; do not drop healthy indexes as routine recovery. (#44982) - **Tracing configuration uniqueness (`53c10126d551`).** Keep one configuration per app/provider and add a unique index. When duplicates exist, the most recently updated row wins, with the ID breaking timestamp ties. Superseded rows are deleted; downgrading the index does not restore them. Retain a database backup. (#44673) - **Extraction changes affect newly processed content.** Re-import or re-index documents whose stored content was affected by the EPUB, EML, DOCX, CSV, Markdown, or Notion fixes. The repeated-PDF-preview fix prevents new duplicate assets for source uploads; it does not remove duplicates already stored. (#44836, #44953, #44905, #43505, #43434, #45053, #44684) - **Tracing history is not rebuilt.** Existing Agent trace-ledger data remains readable, but previously exported traces do not acquire the new nested spans. (#44673)  </details>  <a id="detailed-changes"></a>  ## Detailed changes  <details> <summary><strong>New capabilities · tracing, packages, and Skills</strong></summary>  **Tracing and provider integrations**  - Consolidate delivery through the shared provider interface for Langfuse, LangSmith, Opik, Weave, Arize, Phoenix, Aliyun, MLflow, Databricks, and Tencent. Retryable exports resend the same trace unit with deterministic identifiers. (#44673) - Include called Workflow Tools' persisted execution frames in external exports, under their invoking Tool spans. Independently nested workflow runs remain separate traces linked by parent identifiers. (#44673) - Keep trace credentials write-only. Blank or omitted secrets preserve stored values; changing a destination host, endpoint, or URL requires entering its secrets again. (#44673)   **Portable App packages**  - Package the workflows behind called Workflow Tools, including transitive dependencies, with a default maximum of **20** bundled tool workflows. Missing destination tools are recreated and published; existing tools are reused. Creating missing tools requires tool-management permission and sufficient app quota. Packages needing DSL-version confirmation are rejected in this flow, and apps created before an import failure are cleaned up. (#44592) - Always emit format v2 for App packages, including nested packages and packages without Workflow Tools. This does not change the separate roster-Agent package format. (#45353) - Workflow and API tool IDs no longer become spurious `langgenius/<uuid>` plugin dependencies during export. (#44592)  **Skills and OpenAPI**  - Export drafts or published Skill versions, import folders, and publish through account-authenticated workspace operations. An import with a Skill ID replaces that Skill's draft; the imported `SKILL.md` name must match. Optional `draft_hash` checks reject stale replacements, but use second-resolution timestamps and do not detect edits made within the same second. (#44941) - Skill import drops operating-system junk, rejects unsafe or duplicate paths, enforces file/size limits, reuses identical binary assets, and removes assets written by a failed import. Literal dashes inside Skill frontmatter are preserved. (#44941, #43602) - The operation catalog publishes each operation's effect, and CLI help displays it. Workflow/Chatflow stopping uses stored run ownership and state; unknown runs return `404`, and finished or otherwise non-stoppable runs return `409`. Message-app stopping keeps its task-ID semantics. (#44587, #45314) - Add the experimental `@dify/openapi-content-mapper` package for typed API calls and query helpers inferred from OpenAPI sources, with reusable schema/input/output types and opt-in contract-compatible type subpaths. This is development groundwork; these changes do not migrate production Console or Marketplace calls away from their existing clients. (#44627, #44915)  </details>  <details> <summary><strong>Improvements · editing, accessibility, performance, and operations</strong></summary>  - **Batch Agent file management:** multi-select and multi-file drop, parallel uploads, one atomic binding request, retry using cached upload IDs, and protection against stale Composer saves overwriting newly bound files. Existing single-file endpoints remain compatible. (#44947) - **More usable previews:** highlighted text, on-demand PDF rendering, stable open previews, and explicit truncated-text notices across Agent files, Skills, and working directories. (#45032, #45045) - **Direction and keyboard support:** synchronized locale direction, broader right-to-left layouts, date/time pickers integrated with forms and focus containment, visible labels retained in accessible names, and consistent read-only controls. (#44621, #43869, #44638, #44650, #44593, #44755, #44772, #44589, #45036, #45375) - **Safer editing dialogs:** deletion, rename, metadata, annotation, member-role, and plugin-setting dialogs preserve their sessions and drafts, keep pending actions open, and restore focus after completion. Repeated deletion submissions are blocked while a request is pending. (#44618, #44623, #44643, #44646, #44698, #44736, #44743, #44810, #44825, #44788, #44715, #44652, #44838, #44830) - **Clearer feedback:** field errors and persistent notices appear near the controls they concern, with shared alert presentation across apps, workflows, knowledge, billing, and Skills. Cloud billing uses localized prompts for retryable and action-specific errors. (#45151, #45264, #45266, #45267, #45268, #45192) - **Accurate Cloud quota displays:** distinguish unlimited counts from zero-capacity App Run and trigger quotas, and interpret reset dates as Unix seconds. Pricing display data no longer doubles as live usage limits. (#45117, #45115) - **Lower query overhead:** segment pages load child chunks and attachments in batches; new reference indexes support app storage cleanup. (#45050, #44982) - **Connection reuse:** Gunicorn's configurable keep-alive defaults to 10 seconds, reducing failures caused by reusing connections after the server's shorter idle window. (#44839) - **Docker configuration:** service and provider overrides now reach the relevant containers, database commands, health checks, and WebSocket settings. The generator creates only a missing root `.env`, leaving optional overrides under operator control. (#45359, #45376)  </details>  <details> <summary><strong>Security enhancements</strong></summary>  - **Workspace controls:** enabling or disabling provider models requires an owner or administrator. Email-availability checks require account admission. ([64fe95667b](https://github.com/langgenius/dify/commit/64fe95667b216dad6a44aa5ed4e45768f5672781), [76979a1e24](https://github.com/langgenius/dify/commit/76979a1e2421492ec34f852bc462ee4912643260)) - **Request-scoped cookies:** pooled SSRF clients retain response cookies only within the current outbound request and its redirects, preventing later requests from inheriting another request's cookies. (#44672) - **Dataset token scope:** resource access tokens can list only their explicitly bound active datasets; `include_all=true` cannot widen that scope. Legacy workspace Dataset API keys retain tenant-wide behavior without inheriting an injected owner's personal visibility filters. (#44840) - **App access settings:** importing DSL over an existing app preserves its Web App access mode, and converting an app inherits the source access mode. (#44700, #44857) - **Tracing secrets and egress:** configuration reads no longer return saved credentials, destination changes require secret re-entry, and provider traffic uses the shared outbound policy. (#44673) - **Dependency hardening:** update Authlib to 1.9.1, require LiteLLM 1.100.4 or later within its supported major version, move Agent Pydantic AI to 2.53.0 and Pillow to 13.0.0, update pypdf to 6.21.4, and refresh Werkzeug, GitPython, and other dependency fixes. The standalone Agent pins filelock 4.2.1; Docker build tooling updates uv to 0.13.4. These source updates do not claim to resolve every finding in external or Enterprise base images. (#44606, #44793, #44868, #44871, #44886, #45041, #45104, #45215, #45259, #45214, #45260)  </details>  <details> <summary><strong>Bug fixes · workflows, Agents, knowledge, APIs, and the web app</strong></summary>  **Workflows and execution**  - Restore structured-output fallback for models without native support and preserve JSON booleans instead of converting them to strings. (#44962) - Honor configured file and file-list defaults across Studio, published apps, and API execution. Explicit clearing stays distinct from omission, reset conversations restore defaults, and saving file defaults no longer sends browser Blob objects that cause `415` errors. (#44780, #45042) - Preserve numeric zero in required Studio inputs and previews, and preserve both zero and false in opening statements. Non-object file-reference payloads fall back to the original reference instead of failing unexpectedly. (#45005, #44833, #45088) - Admit streaming Workflow and Chatflow requests only once against app concurrency limits, so a limit of one accepts the single active request. Terminal Chatflow events now stop the execution watchdog and release its retained context. (#45038, #45029) - Preserve caller identity for scheduled Agent file access, use the UTC clock for the Start node's `sys.timestamp`, and order weekly schedule previews by date. Webhook query/form numbers retain integer precision and reject non-finite values. (#44796, #44847, #44718, #44706) - Refresh available workflow nodes when availability changes, retain generated variables when applying a generated prompt, escape variable-search highlighting, and preserve prompt-editor focus when variable metadata refreshes. (#44769, #45274, #44625, #44791) - Request clear-canvas confirmation only when clearing a previously nonempty saved draft, and wrap long workflow error messages. (#45028, #45106)  **Agents, tools, and Skills**  - Persist refreshed OAuth credentials and serialize concurrent refreshes across tools and datasources, protecting rotating tokens and concurrent reauthorization. Temporary refresh failures return a temporary-unavailability response instead of incorrectly asking users to repair valid credentials. (#32195) - Reject late non-terminal events after an Agent stream has completed or been cancelled. Deduplicate plugin JSON output by content rather than formatting, and retain trailing partial action/thought prefixes in ReAct output. (#45080, #45297, #43353) - Stop serializing the entire prompt history into every Agent LLM gateway response chunk. Preserve request content, tool calls, multimodal content, usage, and quota settlement. (#45137) - Handle non-recording tracing parents and isolate concurrent Celery tracing contexts. (#44701, #44803, #45049) - Allow current Qwen flash models in Agent v2 and refresh suggested model patterns. Omit empty optional Agent variable inputs, and accept legacy comma-separated values for multiple-select tool parameters. (#44873, #44748, #45058) - Restore creator-based Agent edit permissions. Skill and snippet searches treat wildcard characters literally, and imported integration bundles retain their plugin icons. (#45040, #44922, #44708, #45112)  **Knowledge and document processing**  - Remove index entries written by a document that was deleted during indexing. Partial QA-segment updates preserve an omitted answer and its contribution to counts. (#44364, #44997) - Include documents missing a metadata key in negative metadata filters, honor explicit `top_k=0`, normalize blank legacy retrieval settings on read, and use keyword retrieval for Economy datasets in retrieval previews. (#44753, #42980, #45020, #44895) - Restore Notion page listing and file import, keep failed page loads retryable, and surface Notion database HTTP failures. (#45053, #44893) - Read EPUB chapters in declared order without extracting archive members to disk; preserve already-decoded EML text; identify DOCX images by relationship type; support numeric CSV column labels; and keep headings inside Markdown code fences intact. (#44836, #44953, #44905, #43505, #43434) - Reuse extracted PDF image assets for repeated previews of the same source upload. Restore Service API file-document creation/update when tenant context is missing or the original dataset creator has left. (#44684, #44972) - Include users' own maintained datasets in their lists. (#44795)  **APIs, files, and SDKs**  - Let MCP Chatflow generation commit its conversation session without a closed-transaction error. API-tool imports ignore non-method Swagger path keys. Suggested-question parsing retains brackets inside quoted questions, and missing citation resources return an empty list. (#44636, #44992, #44916, #43036) - Normalize remote Content-Type values, preserve detected media types for uploads accepted through “Other File Types,” and apply media-specific limits during remote-upload preflight. (#44677, #41292, #44273) - Report download HTTP failures instead of passing error pages to the plugin installer. Distinguish Squid-generated denial responses from upstream authentication errors, preserve GitHub import paths containing `blob`, and reject incompatible multipart file shapes consistently. (#44333, #44841, #44280, #44903) - The Node.js SDK accepts long knowledge-document text, handles CR/LF/CRLF SSE framing and a leading BOM, and accepts an empty pagination cursor as the first page. (#44679, #44263, #44265) - The PHP SDK serializes empty app inputs as JSON objects, avoids buffering streaming Workflow and text-to-audio responses, and sends `last_id` for conversation pagination while preserving its public method signature. (#44675, #44271, #44659)  **Web app and workspace**  - Keep newly created, user-maintained apps visible in Studio and preserve primary navigation across Console routes. Calculate log-period boundaries from the current date. (#44929, #44633, #45360) - Use navigable links in document menus, open publisher destinations in new tabs, and advance integration-tour steps without reloading the page. (#44694, #44808, #44934, #44944) - Respect Marketplace availability across template and plugin entry points and render emoji-ID icons in recommended cards. (#45009, #45027, #45066) - Preserve annotation editor drafts and voice-input focus, and add the Simplified Chinese account-deletion success email template. (#44810, #44825, #45169, #43504)  </details>  <details> <summary><strong>Environment variables and integration changes</strong></summary>  | Setting | Change in 1.19.0 | | --- | --- | | `OPS_TRACE_QUEUE` | New; defaults to `ops_trace_v2`. Shared by trace delivery/retries, Agent trace work, payload cleanup, and sandbox metering. API, workers, and Beat must agree. | | `OPS_TRACE_EXPORT_MAX_ATTEMPTS` | New; defaults to `6` total attempts for retryable trace exports. | | `ENABLE_TRACE_PAYLOAD_SWEEP_TASK` | New; defaults to `true`, enabling daily cleanup of orphaned new-pipeline trace payloads. | | `APP_PACKAGE_MAX_WORKFLOW_TOOLS` | New; defaults to `20` bundled Workflow Tool workflows per App package. | | `GUNICORN_KEEP_ALIVE` | New entrypoint setting; defaults to `10` seconds. |  The old `OPS_TRACE_UNIFIED_ENABLED`, `OPS_TRACE_RETRYABLE_DISPATCH_MAX_RETRIES`, `OPS_TRACE_RETRYABLE_DISPATCH_DELAY_SECONDS`, and `OPS_TRACE_PARENT_CONTEXT_TTL_SECONDS` settings are removed. Existing Agent application-tracing opt-in settings remain supported. (#44673)  For Docker, use `envs/core-services/api-worker.defaults` as the reference for common API/worker/Beat settings and `api-worker.env` for new overrides. Existing `shared.env` remains compatible. Tracked defaults load automatically; root `.env` and explicit service overrides still take precedence. Review the [service-specific references](https://github.com/langgenius/dify/tree/laipz8200/release-1.19.0/docker/envs) rather than replacing local files with fresh templates. (#45359, #45376)  **Endpoint compatibility**  - Replace deprecated Console profile-update POST calls to `/account/name`, `/account/avatar`, `/account/interface-language`, `/account/interface-theme`, and `/account/timezone` with **`PATCH /console/api/account/profile`**. Avatar URL reads remain available. (#45015) - Custom clients of **`GET /console/api/apps/{app_id}/trace-config`** must consume `configured_secrets` instead of expecting saved secret values. (#44673) - For Workflow and Chatflow OpenAPI calls, use **`POST /openapi/v1/apps/{app_id}/runs/{run_id}:stop`** (`stop.run`) and **`GET /openapi/v1/apps/{app_id}/runs/{run_id}/events`** (`get.run.event`). Chat, Agent Chat, and Completion retain the task-ID stop route under `stop.task`. (#44587) - Cloud education verification returns an explicit **`status: eligible | not_eligible`** result; only eligible results contain a token. The corresponding internal billing service must support this response contract. (#45165)  </details>  **Full Changelog:** [1.18.0 to the 1.19.0](https://github.com/langgenius/dify/compare/1.18.0...1.19.0) 
```

### 2026-11-08T22:49:35Z [**xtrime-ru/TelegramApiServer**](https://github.com/xtrime-ru/TelegramApiServer)

```
## Features & Improvements  - Added native Rust crypto acceleration to MadelineProto via `madeline_crypto`, included in the Docker image. Heavy crypto operations run off the PHP thread. - Improved file download performance and connection reuse. - Added configurable request batching with size, timeout, and concurrency limits: `REQUESTS_BULK_SIZE`, `REQUESTS_BULK_INTERVAL`, and `REQUESTS_BULK_CONCURRENCY`. Disabled by default. - Cancel queued requests when HTTP clients disconnect. - Made file size and filename optional for downloads. - Updated MadelineProto, Telegram API schema, and dependencies. - Added the `zstd` PHP extension to the Docker image.  ## Performance  - **Up to 2× faster downloads:** 473 Mbit/s achived on 2 core VPS. - **~100× higher crypto throughput and 98.7% less CPU time per GiB** in the ARM64 benchmark versus upstream v8/OpenSSL: **1,514 vs 15.16 MiB/s**, using **0.85 vs 67.51 CPU-seconds/GiB**. - **Async crypto operations**. Decryption and encryption operations now run in separate thread in rust extension and dont block main thread and network IO. So other api requests can be processed during downloads/uploads.     ## Fixes  - Fixed CDN downloads and downloads with unknown file sizes. - Fixed video playback and HTTP error codes for media responses. - Fixed media constructor handling in nested request parameters. - Fixed MySQL startup on macOS. - Improved graceful shutdown and increased Docker log retention.  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/xtrime-ru/TelegramApiServer/compare/v2.7.2...v2.8.0 
```

```
## 升级指南  ### 镜像更新  - 更新 fastgpt-app（FastGPT 主服务）镜像 tag: v4.17.4 - 更新 fastgpt-pro（FastGPT 商业版）镜像 tag: v4.17.4  ## ⚡ 优化  1. **[列表体验]** 优化列表滚动分页监听，减少滚动过程中的组件重渲染，提升滚动流畅度。  ## 🐛 修复  1. **[对话体验]** 修复免登录分享对话中的全局变量、交互表单使用模型选择器时，未携带分享鉴权信息，导致模型列表或已选模型名称加载失败的问题。 2. **[对话体验]** 修复免登录分享对话顶部变量面板按普通对话规则展示变量的问题，外部变量不再向分享用户显示。 3. **[对话体验]** 修复免登录分享对话中删除消息、重试及编辑后发送失败的问题。 4. **[列表体验]** 修复部分列表在内容缩短后无法继续滚动加载、分页失败后仍自动重复请求的问题。 5. **[账号与团队]** 修复联系方式弹窗关闭按钮因正文滚动而偏移或被裁剪的问题。 6. **[账号与团队]** 修复登录和修改成员名时，使用中文输入法按回车确认候选词会误提交表单的问题；确认候选词后再次按回车可正常提交。 7. **[Agent]** 修复部分工作流工具在编辑器中配置正常，但运行时被错误过滤、无法调用的问题。 8. **[工具调用]** 修复工具参数 JSON Schema 格式不规范导致模型请求失败的问题。   **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/labring/FastGPT/compare/v4.17.3...v4.17.4 
```

### 2026-11-08T05:06:38Z [**QuantumNous/new-api**](https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api)

```
> [!IMPORTANT] > 增加了 gpt-image-2.6 的支持。它的新参数 complexity、svg、frame_count、frame_duration_ms 和 markup 会转发给上游，可以生成 SVG 和 GIF，编辑图片时可以带 markup 标注图层。 > > 修复了 Redis 集群和分片 Redis 上修改用户或令牌时报 `keys must in same slot` 的问题。多节点部署请把所有节点一起升级，新旧版本混跑时，一个节点上的用户和令牌变更不一定能及时同步到另一个版本的节点。 > > `SQLITE_PATH` 只写文件路径时，现在和默认配置一样开启 WAL 和写入等待。数据库旁边会多出 `-wal` 和 `-shm` 文件，备份时要一起复制。 > > 模型定价保存时会逐个模型列出当前价格和新价格，确认后才保存。删掉的价格在保存前可以撤销。 > > Added support for gpt-image-2.6. Its new parameters complexity, svg, frame_count, frame_duration_ms, and markup are forwarded to the upstream, so it can generate SVG and GIF images, and image edits can include a markup layer. > > Fixed the problem of `keys must in same slot` errors when editing users or tokens on Redis Cluster or sharded Redis. Upgrade every node of a multi-node deployment together. While old and new versions run side by side, a user or token change on one node may not reach nodes on the other version in time. > > A `SQLITE_PATH` that is only a file path now gets WAL and the busy timeout, the same as the default setting. `-wal` and `-shm` files appear next to the database. Copy them together with it when you back up. > > Saving Model Pricing now lists the current and new price of each model and saves only after you confirm. A deleted price can be undone until you save.  ## 中文  * **任务插件**：增加了 Vertex AI 渠道的 Gemini Omni 1.2 Flash，视频最长 15 秒。修复了 Gemini Omni 只把第一张输入图片发给 Vertex 的问题。Gemini Omni 生成失败时会返回失败原因。从插件市场安装或上传的插件也能用顶层的 `/v1` 地址调用了，比如 TypeSafe 的 `POST /v1/systemone`。 * **计费**：模型定价会列出价格需要处理的模型，选中模型能看到原因和处理方法。只统计启用的渠道实际提供的模型，内置价格只在已保存或有渠道提供时才列出。删掉的价格在保存前只是草稿，可以撤销。改过的行会显示新旧价格，保存时逐个模型列出当前价格和新价格，确认后才保存。在手机上，标签页单独占一行可以滑动，次要操作收进了菜单。 * **渠道**：增加了 gpt-image-2.6 的支持，complexity、svg、frame_count、frame_duration_ms 和 markup 参数会转发给上游，可以生成 SVG 和 GIF，编辑图片时可以带 markup 标注图层。DeepSeek 官方 Responses 接口的工具调用历史缺少 reasoning 时会自动补上，避免 DeepSeek 因此返回 400。高级自定义渠道的入口路径增加了 `/v1/tools/search` 和 `/v1/tools/fetch`。修复了 Vertex AI 服务账号每个请求都重新换取访问令牌的问题，令牌现在缓存到过期前 5 分钟。修复了多密钥渠道的密钥全部失败后，重新启用其中一个密钥，渠道仍然处于自动禁用的问题。修复了手动禁用的渠道在请求失败后变成自动禁用、又被自动测试重新启用的问题。渠道亲和性里的会话规则可以单独清空自己的缓存，刷新统计和清空缓存的说明也写清楚了。 * **其他**：修复了 Redis 集群和分片 Redis 上修改用户或令牌时报错的问题。修复了 `SQLITE_PATH` 只写文件路径时丢掉 WAL 和写入等待设置，并发写入报 "database is locked" 的问题。修复了用邀请码通过 OAuth 注册的新用户没有记录邀请人的问题。  ## English  * **Task plugins**: Added Gemini Omni 1.2 Flash on Vertex AI channels, with videos up to 15 seconds. Fixed the problem of Gemini Omni sending only the first input image to Vertex. A failed Gemini Omni generation now returns the reason. Plugins installed from the marketplace or uploaded can now be called at their top-level `/v1` paths too, such as TypeSafe's `POST /v1/systemone`. * **Billing**: Model Pricing lists the models whose prices need attention. Select a model to see the reason and what to change. Only models an enabled channel serves are counted, and built-in prices are listed only when saved or served by a channel. A deleted price stays a draft until you save, and you can undo it. Changed rows show the old and new prices. Every save lists the current and new price of each model and saves only after you confirm. On phones, the tabs have their own scrollable row and secondary actions are in a menu. * **Channels**: Added support for gpt-image-2.6. Its complexity, svg, frame_count, frame_duration_ms, and markup parameters are forwarded to the upstream, so it can generate SVG and GIF images, and image edits can include a markup layer. When tool call history sent to DeepSeek's official Responses API lacks reasoning, it is filled in, so DeepSeek does not reject the request with a 400. Advanced Custom channels can use the incoming paths `/v1/tools/search` and `/v1/tools/fetch`. Fixed the problem of Vertex AI service accounts requesting a new access token for every request. Tokens are now cached until 5 minutes before they expire. Fixed the problem of a multi-key channel staying auto-disabled when one key was enabled again after all keys failed. Fixed the problem of a manually disabled channel becoming auto-disabled after a failed request, which let automatic tests enable it again. Session rules in Channel Affinity can clear their own cache, and the refresh and clear actions are explained more clearly. * **Also**: Fixed the problem of errors when editing users or tokens on Redis Cluster or sharded Redis. Fixed the problem of a `SQLITE_PATH` with only a file path dropping the WAL and busy timeout settings, which made concurrent writes fail with "database is locked". Fixed the problem of new OAuth users who signed up with an invite code not recording the inviter.  ---  ## What's Changed * feat: forward image complexity and markup for gpt-image-2.6 by @feitianbubu in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/8208 * fix(cache): 修复用户和令牌缓存的 Redis 跨槽错误 by @wang48 in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/8218 * fix(plugins): serve uploaded plugins at top-level /v1 paths by @Calcium-Ion in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/8241 * feat(deepseek): 补全 Responses 工具历史缺失的 reasoning by @markcheney1 in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/8162 * feat(plugins): add gemini omni 1.2 flash and send every input image to vertex by @feitianbubu in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/8228 * feat(channels): 高级自定义渠道增加 DeepSeek 搜索与抓取路径选项 by @longtsing in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/8250 * fix(oauth): persist inviter when creating OAuth users by @Yorick-Ryu in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/8247  ## New Contributors * @wang48 made their first contribution in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/8218 * @markcheney1 made their first contribution in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/8162 * @longtsing made their first contribution in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/8250  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/compare/v1.0.0-rc.48...v1.0.0-rc.49 
```

### 2026-11-08T23:48:24Z [**open-webui/open-webui**](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui)

```
 ### Added  - 🦾 **Agents in automations.** The automation editor now lets you choose a terminal and working folder, then select a coding agent available there. Scheduled runs use that saved terminal and folder, and check that the terminal and agent are still available before starting. Agent automations send their results to a chat; channel destinations remain available for ordinary models. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/21fc016141c73a0e11b428c61bde20527bbc228a) - 📂 **Change terminal and folder during a chat.** You can now choose a different terminal or working folder after sending messages, including in temporary chats. Both controls remain disabled while a reply is being generated. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/a518ff1630b74ed6edd62538c5da4bb26cc6dd20) - 🇧🇷 **Brazilian Portuguese translations.** Added missing translations for terminal and folder controls, Git actions, spreadsheet editing, voice notes, tab shortcuts, notification sounds and tool settings. Updated the explanations for document retrieval, tool permissions and voice settings. [#33945](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33945)  ### Fixed  - 📂 **Files tabs follow terminal changes.** Switching a chat to another terminal now updates its open Files tabs to show that terminal's files, instead of leaving the previous terminal's folders on screen. With a terminal set to "Per chat", the Files tab also switches to the chat's own files after the first message. [#33954](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33954) - 🧭 **Agents in unsupported model pickers.** Connecting a terminal no longer adds its coding agents to Admin Models, base-model selectors, Arena candidates, task-model settings, group defaults, sub-agent settings, channel mentions or the Playground. Notes also stop choosing a terminal agent for title generation. Agents cannot be saved or imported as custom models or their base models, and their chat menu no longer offers Edit. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/21fc016141c73a0e11b428c61bde20527bbc228a), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/6d3123b5b4a90962bff44c69c7e0bae0c65557d5) - 🔌 **Agent list after switching terminals.** Changing terminals immediately removes the previous terminal's agents from the chat's model list while the new list loads. A model-list request from a chat you have left can no longer replace the current list. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/21fc016141c73a0e11b428c61bde20527bbc228a) - 📋 **Copy buttons over plain HTTP.** At a plain HTTP address on your local network, copying comment links, search links, shared-chat links, webhook URLs, chat IDs, Git branch names, commit hashes and spreadsheet cells now works. Copy Formatted Text preserves formatting, and copied text keeps its final line break. [#33939](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33939) - 📝 **Clipboard prompts and Auto-Copy Response over plain HTTP.** Prompts using the clipboard placeholder now insert into chats and channels even when the browser blocks clipboard access, with a message explaining that the clipboard could not be read. Auto-Copy Response stays off and shows a permission message instead of silently failing to turn on; turning it off no longer requires clipboard access. [#33939](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33939) - 🧠 **DeepSeek thinking mode with skills, knowledge and memories.** These chats no longer fail with a missing reasoning error when the connection's Provider is set to "llama.cpp". DeepSeek thinking mode still requires that setting; connections using "Default" are unchanged. [#33950](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33950) - 🏷️ **Labels on grouped tool calls.** With Tool Call Labels enabled, a group of tool calls keeps the labels that are present even if one call has none. The unlabeled call shows its tool name instead of making the whole group lose its labels. [#33932](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33932) - 💬 **Tool labels in later replies.** The model now sees the labels it wrote for earlier tool calls when continuing a reply or answering another message, removing a reason it could stop writing labels as the conversation continued. [#33953](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33953) - 🔎 **Knowledge searches containing punctuation.** Searches in knowledge files now find text such as "Art. 40" when the model marks the period as literal punctuation, instead of returning no matches. [#33936](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33936) - 🪟 **Side Chat with Temporary Chat disabled.** Leaving Enforce Temporary Chat switched on while disabling Temporary Chat no longer prevents users from sending and saving side chats or dragging an existing chat into the side panel. [#33930](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33930) - 🏠 **Home folder tab title.** A Files tab at the connected machine's home directory now says "Home" instead of showing the directory name, including when reopening saved tabs. Other folders keep their names. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/241c8c0e47e19b0adeb13d577ca538d2252b0ad4) - 🦙 **Missing Ollama in Docker images.** Building the image that includes Ollama now fails if Ollama was not installed, instead of producing an image that cannot start Ollama after its download failed. [#33944](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33944) - 📄 **Errors after leaving a chat.** Closing or leaving a chat while its tab layout is being saved no longer fills the server log with an error report for the dropped connection. [#33929](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33929) 
```

### 2026-11-10T09:15:53Z [**gitbutlerapp/gitbutler**](https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler)

```
## Changes  - Updating the workspace now updates linked worktrees too - Operations that update linked worktrees now preserve conflicting uncommitted changes with conflict markers  ## Fixes  - Repositories owned by another user now explain how to allow access with `safe.directory` - Fixes branch apply, unapply and update operations writing encrypted files or Git LFS pointers instead of usable file contents in git-crypt and Git LFS repositories - Fixes unnecessary conflicts and incorrect file contents when combining branches based on different target commits - Fixes updating the workspace leaving local branches behind after they were merged - Fixes failed checkouts leaving files partly written or losing uncommitted edits - Fixes project loading failing when another process deletes an untracked file while changes are being read  ## CLI  - `but commit`, `but amend`, `but discard` and `but diff` now accept Git-style paths, including relative and absolute paths, directories and `.` - `but undo` now refuses operations it cannot safely reverse on branches checked out in linked worktrees - `but` now gives clearer errors and recovery steps for repository, branch, worktree, push, merge, undo and forge authentication problems, and suggests alternatives to unsupported commands - `but push --dry-run` now reports branches that cannot be pushed, including branches containing conflicted commits, and exits non-zero if any are refused - `but reword` now reads commit messages or branch names from a file or stdin with `-F` - `but amend` now defaults to the latest commit when the target branch is unambiguous - `but amend` now refuses when there are no changes to amend - `but diff` now accepts several file targets - `but diff` and `but show` now support `--stat` for changed-file and line counts - `but pr new` now accepts messages with blank lines before the title - `but amend -t` and `but squash -t` now accept branches checked out in linked worktrees - `but worktree new`, `but worktree remove` and `but branch delete` now handle worktree directories deleted outside GitButler - `but commit` runs Git commit hooks again in the main checkout; `-n`/`--no-hooks` or `--no-verify` skips them (linked-worktree commits still skip hooks) - `but pull` now removes stale remote-tracking branches after updating - `but` now reports linked worktrees whose uncommitted changes gained conflict markers - Fixes `but uncommit` being blocked by unrelated conflicted commits - Fixes `but resolve conflicts <commit>` suggesting a resolution command for the wrong commit - Fixes `but diff` reporting clean files as missing - Fixes amending an empty branch rewriting the commit below it - Fixes `but resolve` overlooking conflicted commits in linked worktrees - Fixes `but` failing to open repositories beneath a previously registered project whose `.git` directory was removed - Fixes `but pull` leaving local branches behind after they were merged  ## Native app  Highlights from the native app, available under "Experimental" in settings:  - Adds split panes for pages and terminals, with drag-and-drop arrangement, keyboard navigation and saved page positions - The navigator can now appear as tabs across the top of the window, with drag-and-drop ordering for projects and worktrees - Adds a command editor for customizing keyboard shortcuts and finding commands by pressing their keys - Adds `but://open` links to projects, branches, commits, uncommitted changes, files and lines, tabs and settings - Files in the working tree can now be edited directly in the diff, including tracked files with no changes - Committing changes with dependencies now offers to stack the required branches instead of creating a partial commit - Adds an option to archive or delete clean worktrees after an update finds their branches merged  ## New Contributors * @bjoaquinc made their first contribution in https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler/pull/17018  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler/compare/release/0.23.2...release/0.23.3  ## Downloads  Download bins from https://gitbutler.com/ 
```

tunnels (DNSTT, MasterDNS, Soar), Psiphon, GooseRelay, XHTTP, mKCP, XDRIVE, FinalMask, Mirage, AmneziaWG, VLESS encryption, Mieru, Sudoku and more.  ☁️ WARP, rebuilt: WireGuard, AmneziaWG or MASQUE, an Auto mode that picks the fastest, a smart endpoint scanner, and noise that mimics DNS, QUIC and SIP.  🔗 Extra Security & Unblocker: WARP or Psiphon to secure your proxy or to unblock your proxy.  ⚡️ Big subscriptions, fast: keep only the fastest proxies, choose which ones run, and test upload and download speed per proxy.  🎨 New home screen and proxy list: live status for every proxy, signal-style pings, and the whole connection shown step by step.  🍏 New macOS VPN (system extension) and a Mac App Store build. 📺 Android TV: add a subscription by scanning a QR code with your phone. 🔄 Built-in updates on Android, Windows, Linux and macOS. 🔍 Know why: a proxy's details now say why it failed. 🔒 Safer: encrypted app-to-core channel, and crash reports without private data.  📖 Docs for every new feature (English & فارسی): https://hiddify.com/fa/core/ Full change logs: https://github.com/hiddify/hiddify-app/discussions/2487

### 2026-11-10T03:40:12Z [**team-spotube/spotube**](https://github.com/team-spotube/spotube)

```
## Spotube V6 is here 🎉🚀  ### 🔥Highlights  - Migration from Flutter to Compose Multiplatform, [learn why we did it](https://spotube.cc/blog/compose-multiplatform-migration/) - UI redesign, introducing [Millennium](https://github.com/team-spotube/millennium/) UI. 2000s look and feel with 2026's modernity - Jam sessions, play music with friends and family in sync. Runs on a new dedicated room server, with host-only controls and device bans to keep rooms safe - Full Android Auto integration. Browse, Library, Search all are there now, not just queue - iOS now plays audio through VLC, so every streaming format works, and keeps playing in the background - Migration from hetu_script to Kotlin for plugin development. Powered by [Zipline by Cashapp](https://github.com/cashapp/zipline/) - More extensibility, plugin support for Lyrics provider and Scrobbling providers - Redesigned plugin manager. Plugin login pages can only reach cookies and scripts of the domains they declare - Native Android APK, with reduced apk size, performance improvements and more native android integrations. - Relicensed from BSD-4-Clause to AGPL-3.0-or-later for a more Open Source friendly licensing. And for preventing distribution of closed source and maliciously modified Spotube.  ### 💫 Features  - Migration to Compose Multiplatform - Add ChipTab component and refactor SearchScreen to use it - Add AutocompleteTextField component and integrate it into SearchScreen - Enhance AutocompleteTextField with focus management and dropdown behavior - Enhance AppSidebar with improved button interactions and visual feedback - Implement endless playback recommendations in DeviceAudioPlayerQueue - Add GhostIconButton component and enhance button interactions in TrackList - Replace IconButton with GhostIconButton in AppExpandedPlayer and AppLargePlayer for improved visual consistency - Replace IconButton with GhostIconButton in PlayerQueueContent and PlayableCard for visual consistency - Enhance UI components with LocalBaseUITheme for consistent styling and improved interactions - Enhance button styles and interactions in Buttons and Theme components for improved UI consistency - Simplify highlight implementation in UI components for improved readability and maintainability - Add genre selection and tabs to HomeScreen for improved content navigation - Implement artist overview screen with loading, error handling, and pagination for albums, related artists, and featured playlists - Add genre and featured browsing capabilities to MediaBrowseHelper - Implement playlist creation and editing functionality with UI components - Add 'Add to Playlist' functionality with UI components and dialogs - Add Toggle component and update UI to use it in place of Switch - Implement settings section cards for updates, desktop, language, caching, and plugins - Update icons in settings sections to use Iconsax library for improved UI consistency - Replace FeatherIcons with Iconsax in PluginCard and PluginPermissionsDialog for improved UI consistency - Refactor PluginPermissionsDialog to use custom UI components for improved consistency and readability - Enhance UI with shared element transitions and improve state management in album and playlist screens - Integrate Uniffi for audio metadata reading and writing with Lofty library - Replace FeatherIcons with Iconsax in window controls and update sidebar with better design - Implement haze effect and UI enhancements in AppLargePlayer and AppShell - Enhance haze effect integration in AppShell and AppSidebar for improved UI experience - Add adaptive dialog bottom sheet component and enhance plugin installation UI - Enhance AdaptiveDialogBottomSheet with dismiss icon and improve plugin screen layout - Implement CollectionView component and refactor Album, Playlist, SavedTracks screens for improved UI consistency - Add current time management in AudioPlayer for improved playback control - Simplify playback control logic in AudioPlayer by removing redundant state checks - Introduce AudioPlayerInterface and refactor AudioPlayer implementations for better abstraction - Update skipToNext and skipToPrevious methods in AudioPlayer for improved media item management - Refactor LyricsViewModel to use AudioPlayerInterface for improved abstraction - Add Discord Rich Presence integration for enhanced media playback visibility - Implement System Tray integration for window management and media controls - Add hover effect to menu items in System Tray for improved user experience - Add SSH debug session setup on failure for improved troubleshooting - Implement blacklist functionality for tracks and artists with UI updates - **systemtray**: Self-manage tray lifecycle via Koin DI - **plugin_interfaces**: Update Maven publishing configuration and add GitHub Actions workflow - **plugin_discovery**: Implement GitHub plugin discovery and add logo support in UI - **plugin_discovery**: Implement GitHub plugin discovery and add logo support and information dialog in UI - **plugin_install**: Add plugin installation dialog and release fetching functionality - Update dependencies and configure compose hot reload settings - Improve NewPipe video rank matching - Integrate Nucleus framework for enhanced window management and webview support - Implement system tray functionality with Compose Native Tray integration - **mediacontrol**: Implement SystemMediaControlService for OS media integration - **devices**: Add device discovery and remote control features - **deeplinks**: Implement deep link handling for jam sessions - **remote-control**: Implement remote playback functionality and UI components - **remote-control**: Enhance queue management and player state synchronization - **remote-control**: Enhance remote playback functionality with new commands and UI integration - **webrtc**: Update ICE server configuration and enhance logging for peer connection events - **jam-session**: Add connection state management and update UI feedback for session status - **jam-session**: Enhance jam session functionality with improved playback synchronization and error handling - **remote-control**: Refactor service registration and cleanup logic for improved reliability - **jam-session**: Integrate MQTT broker configuration and enhance jam session settings - **jam-session**: Implement track addition to jam session and enhance UI feedback for jam state - **jam-session**: Enhance guest functionality with track addition and participant moderation options - **release**: Add support for Linux ARM64 builds and artifact uploads - **player-options**: Add PlayerOptionsViewModel and enhance player UI with options menu - **player-options**: Enhance options menu with localized strings and icons - **jam-settings**: Implement JamSettings screen and view model for broker configuration - Refactor track source management to support plugin-specific caching and retrieval - Implement shuffle play functionality for albums, playlists, and saved tracks - **navigation**: Refactor sidebar and bottombar navigation structure with new tab and group models - **search**: Enhance LazyRow with drag scrolling functionality - **autocomplete**: Simplify focus management and remove unused key event handling - **update**: Implement update checker with dialog for available updates - **update**: Refactor update checking mechanism and implement new dialog for app and plugin updates - **scrobble**: Implement ScrobbleManager to handle track scrobbling and integrate with audio player - **welcome**: Implement onboarding flow with multiple pages and donation options - **previews**: Add comprehensive previews for UI components including sliders, toggles, checkboxes, and text fields - **settings**: Add 'About' section and improve header visibility in settings sections - **surface**: Implement Frutiger-Aero themed surfaces with glass and acrylic styles - **ui**: Replace Scaffold with BaseScaffold for consistent surface theming across screens - **ui**: New custom surface integration on all components - **onboarding**: Enhance welcome screen with structured guide steps and improved layout - **player**: Add content descriptions for play, pause, and add to queue actions - **support**: Implement support reminder dialog and donation options - **player**: Refactor artist display and improve search field labels - **search**: Enhance search functionality with recent search suggestions and improved UI components - **sidebar**: Adjust spacing in sidebar and setting cards for improved layout - **jam**: Implement group jam functionality with UI components and state management - **library**: Enhance library UI with new tab titles, filtering options, and improved state management - **library**: Add Library screen list mode beside grid mode, Local Track, Download screen, Local Library folders dialog visual improvement - **ui**: Integrate LocalTextStyle for dynamic tinting in player controls and UI components - **navigation**: Add custom navigation transition specifications for push and pop actions - **i18n**: Replace hardcoded strings with localized resources across multiple components - **plugin**: Implement MetadataPluginGuard to manage plugin dependencies and user prompts - **ui**: Replace IndeterminateProgress with new loading components and enhance dialog structures - **ui**: Update MillenniumModalBottomSheet with compact padding and spacing adjustments - **plugin**: Add login prompt for metadata plugin access and implement authentication view model - **user-agents**: Implement dynamic user agent fetching and caching mechanism - **lyrics**: Implement SyncedLyricsList component and enhance LyricsViewModel for better state management - **server**: Implement port fallback mechanism for playback proxy server - **window**: Implement window state persistence and restoration - Enhance Android build configuration with stable/nightly flavors and update launcher icons - **graalvm**: Enable GraalVM native image support and update packaging configurations - **local-tracks**: Implement LocalFolder screen and view model for folder navigation - **navigation**: Implement fade-through and stack transition animations for navigation - **remote**: Redesign remote control and devices screens, fix connection issues - Download diagnostics, live connect status, remote settings on devices screen - Enhance loading states for various UI components and update dependencies - **packaging**: Flathub, AUR, WinGet and Chocolatey publishing - **plugin**: Implement automatic plugin updates and enhance JSON parsing - **jam**: Replace MQTT with a Durable Object room server and synced playback - **jam**: Harden the room server, add device bans and friendly names - **ios**: Play audio with VLCKit instead of AVPlayer - **ios**: Drop AVPlayer-era streaming format restrictions - Add iOS background playback support ([#3170](https://github.com/team-spotube/spotube/issues/3170)) - **update**: Compare app releases with full SemVer - **plugins**: Redesign the plugin manager - **plugins**: Limit WebView cookie and script access to declared domains - **plugins**: Download the public suffix list daily instead of bundling it - **plugins**: Enhance built-in plugin handling and add removal option for user-installed plugins  ### 🪲 Bug Fixes  - **newpipe**: Fallback to muxed streams if no audio stream is available - Dismiss search dropdown and keyboard on submission - Custom image helper null exception - **plugin**: Reload Zipline service on same-version replace - Convert buildCurrentMediaItemList to a suspend function for coroutine support - Enhance Discord RPC connection management with cooldown and error handling - Cached track audio not being invalidated causing alternative track source to not being played - Play next not working - Update pendingNextIndex when playing a track from the playlist - **webview**: Linux webview crashing the app - Add mavenLocal() to plugin and dependency resolution management - **plugin**: Handle service closure exception during authentication check - **sidebar**: Add spacer to AppSidebar for improved layout - **remote-control**: Update connection handling and improve logging for remote control events - Add window insets to top bar and bottom navigation bar, queue sheet - **stream-proxy**: Stream upstream audio instead of buffering the whole track - **newpipe**: Fall back through multiple searches when ISRC search misses - **audio-api**: Enhance fallback mechanism for text search results in YouTube - **audio-player**: Android shuffle wasnt working - **stream-proxy**: Improve logging for cache timeout and handle disconnection scenarios - **macos**: Add GraalVM runtime dependency and initialize GraalVm in main function - **android**: Create intermediate directories for Rust targets to prevent runtime errors - **android**: Update sharing functionality to use chooser intent with new task flag - **android**: Simplify service start logic for PlaybackService to avoid crashes on background playback - **android**: Update playback state management to ensure guests follow host's play state - **android**: App stuck on splash screen from main-thread string loading - **downloads**: Respect download format/quality and never save manifests or video - **ios**: Run Zipline on an 8 MiB stack thread - **server**: Don't crash when the proxy port is still in use - **ios**: Stream formats AVPlayer can play - **gradle**: Restore VFS watch setting and clean up properties - **build**: Restore version catalog lost in dev merge - **ios**: Make the login WebView identify as Mobile Safari - **android**: Use desugar_jdk_libs_nio so URLEncoder.encode(String, Charset) is backported on API < 33 - **flatpak**: Grant the Wayland socket so the webview doesn't render black - **jam**: Keep guests on the host's recording across song changes - **desktop**: Stop non-composable string lookups from querying the system theme - Stop memory blow-ups and crashes with large lists and local media - **ios**: Use MobileVLCKit from CocoaPods instead of a git Swift package - Crash at startup from a Koin cycle in DeviceAudioPlayerQueue - Update default Jam server URL to new endpoint - **linux**: Bundle libselinux in the AppImage - **artist**: Play the tapped top track instead of the first one - **welcome**: Keep step tabs and pages readable on narrow windows - **plugins**: Keep public suffix list updates off the startup and call paths  ## New Contributors  - @tomasalias made their first contribution in https://github.com/team-spotube/spotube/pull/2919 - @Akshat-104 made their first contribution in https://github.com/team-spotube/spotube/pull/3032 - @henrik242 made their first contribution in https://github.com/team-spotube/spotube/pull/3136 - @wuqqers made their first contribution in https://github.com/team-spotube/spotube/pull/3132 - @samuelegiacomin-stack made their first contribution in https://github.com/team-spotube/spotube/pull/3170  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/team-spotube/spotube/compare/v5.1.2...v6.0.0
```

```
### windows-macos-linux-android-ios - 💪 [OPT] Environment variables now support nested definitions. - 💪 [OPT] Support for GraphQL view. - 💪 [OPT] Support for DNS message parsing results view. - 💪 [OPT] gRPC message parsing now supports configuring service methods. - 💪 [OPT] Access control now supports configuring CIDR address rules. - 💪 [OPT] Loopback address detection is no longer limited to 127.0.0.1. - 💪 [OPT] Support for previewing images of type `image/vnd.microsoft.icon`. - 🐞 [FIX] A bug where localhost was forcibly resolved to 127.0.0.1. - 🐞 [FIX] A bug where loopback listening would not take effect after the application restarts. - 🐞 [FIX] A bug where the server address in the overview tab of the traffic details was not displayed correctly in IPv6 format. ### windows-macos-linux - 💪 [OPT] A new IP query tool has been added to the toolbox. - 💪 [OPT] The script editor now uses the rule name as the file name when exporting scripts. - 💪 [OPT] Configuration files now use timestamps in their names when exported. - 🐞 [FIX] A bug where the system proxy status has a delay in updating. ### windows - 💪 [OPT] When setting the system proxy, automatic detection settings and the use of configuration scripts will be disabled. ### linux - 🐞 [FIX] A bug where the installation status of manually installed CA certificates could not be detected.
```
