### 2026-11-06T06:37:25Z [**gkd-kit/gkd**](https://github.com/gkd-kit/gkd)

```
# 更新内容  - 重做特权服务，不强依赖 Shizuku，支持 Root、ADB 和手动启动，提供授权管理与断线恢复 - 新增「触摸优化」，支持通过特权服务执行点击和滑动，减少部分设备上的触摸卡顿 - 自动化模式新增「伴生无障碍」及按应用暂停功能，移除「局部无障碍」和「局部自动化」 - 重写选择器引擎，优化匹配调度与窗口识别，支持中断失效规则正在执行的滑动 - 新增匹配运行诊断，支持采集与分享诊断记录，完善崩溃记录管理和日志导出 - 新增持久化触发统计，支持按应用筛选触发记录及查看规则组最近触发时间 - 新增应用规则分类标签、显示方式切换与本地规则组拖动排序，完善全局规则应用范围设置和状态说明 - 调整全局订阅字段：`matchAnyApp`、`matchSystemApp`、`matchLauncher` 和 `apps[].enable` 仅在规则组层级生效；子规则 `apps` 改为按应用覆盖页面与版本限制，不再控制应用启用范围 - 重做快照浏览、设置与分享流程，支持分组浏览和批量操作，截图改用 WebP 压缩以减少存储占用 - 支持通过特权服务安装更新并自动重启，提供安装前备份入口 - 新增外部调用设置页与 HTTP 规则管理接口，支持快照命令及本地、内存订阅的规则读写 - 优化服务控制与保活引导，新增透明悬浮窗保活开关、通知栏快捷方式引导和事件悬浮窗紧凑模式 - 新增 Windows 预览版，仅供电脑体验界面 - 适配 Android 17 网络权限，修复部分 Android 16 系统的截图兼容问题 - 修复订阅备份数据不一致、追加规则组时 `scopeKeys` 引用错误及「最近使用」漏记等问题  ## 更新方式  - GKD - 设置 - 关于 - 检测更新 - 下列方式之一  <a href="https://gkd.li/guide/"><img src="https://e.gkd.li/f23b704d-d781-494b-9719-393f95683b89" alt="Download from GKD.LI" width="32%" /></a><a href="https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=li.songe.gkd"><img src="https://e.gkd.li/f63fabeb-0342-4961-a46d-cac61b0f8856" alt="Download from Google Play" width="32%" /></a><a href="https://github.com/gkd-kit/gkd/releases"><img src="https://e.gkd.li/c1ef2bb9-7472-46d5-9806-81b4c37e5b4d" alt="Download from GitHub releases" width="32%" /></a> 
```

### 2026-11-07T03:26:19Z [**gyroflow/gyroflow**](https://github.com/gyroflow/gyroflow)

```
### ✨ Improvements - **New cameras:** Canon, Z CAM, HOVERAir, XTRA and more, see below - **Optical stabilization** - New algorithm for stabilization without motion data that's actually good. It produces similar results to gyro-based stabilization on most videos with good tracking points and good lens profile. Enable in `Motion data -> Optical correction`. This fixes all O4 issues, vibrations, bad gyro, O4 Lite, DJI Neo shakes, Hero 7 on FPV, DJI with RockSteady, Sony with OSS etc. If you have a moving subject in frame, you can edit every tracked point and remove it from the solution. Valid lens profile is required. - **New "Optimal path" smoothing** - plans the camera path for the whole clip at once: it removes much more shake than "Default" at the same zoom, never lags behind pans, and keeps the frame within the zoom limit. Select it in the "Stabilization" panel and try on your footage - it has a completely different feel than Default. - **GoPro now uses official calibrations** - Lens profiles are more acurate and all new GoPro models are supported without changes to Gyroflow - **All [Sony issues](https://github.com/gyroflow/gyroflow/issues/849) have been fixed**, including Dynamic Active support - **Sony lens breathing compensation**, using the data recorded by the supported lenses - **RED gyro data is now accurately timed**, autosync is no longer needed - **Focal length stabilization** - smooths out fast zooms and zoom "pumping" by cropping. Works with every file that records the focal length (Sony, Canon, Blackmagic, RED, Nikon, Z CAM). Enable in the "Stabilization" panel (by @Milchgsicht-star) - **Manual zoom level for "Static zoom"** - every video is zoomed by exactly the amount you set, like in-camera stabilization, and smoothing is lowered only where the frame doesn't fit (by @anton-belkin) - **New horizon lock options:** "Lock pitch angle" (by @Ivan1248) and "Automatic lock", which lets the horizon lean into banked turns (by @mihatsu) - **Reworked lens math:** "Lens correction" below 100% is now exact and changes the image evenly, underwater refraction is exact across the whole frame, and Gyroflow now supports lenses wider than 180° - **Added "Output projection"** (stereographic, equidistant, equisolid, orthographic) to show the whole picture of very wide lenses like Insta360 X4/X5 or fisheye adapters - **Videos open faster**, and play unstabilized while the motion data loads (by @TimoLehnertz) - **Better support for variable frame rate and off-speed clips** - every frame is matched to the motion data at its own timestamp (phones, cameras that drop frames), and off-speed recordings are timed by the rate the camera captured them at - **AV1 export** - GPU only: NVIDIA RTX 40, AMD RX 7000, Intel Arc or newer (Windows, Linux), or Android phones with an AV1 encoder (#1140) - **"Preserve other tracks" now copies the camera metadata tracks and file metadata as-is**, so apps like DJI Mimo recognize the export and show their dashboard overlays (#1078). For Mimo, keep "Audio codec" at AAC - **New autosync method "Optical analysis", now the default** - new fast and acurate autosync method. The other methods are still in `Synchronization -> Advanced -> Offset method` - Added "Copy" audio codec (falls back to AAC when the container can't store it) - Added "Smooth" keyframe easing, which passes through keyframes without stopping or overshooting (#1119) - Blackmagic, RED, Nikon and Z CAM now follow the recorded focal length - Added "FL" timeline chart with the original and smoothed focal length - Added "Lens metadata delay" for lenses that report the focal length late, e.g. some Tamron zooms on Sony (`Lens profile -> Advanced`, measured with "Analyze") - Added "No distortion, focal length only" lens option for already undistorted videos, using the 35 mm equivalent focal length you enter - Added support for Gyroflow Protobuf, an open format for embedding motion data, lens and IBIS/OIS info in video files or .jsonl sidecars - Image sequences now automatically use a matching .gcsv file (by @baso53) - Calibrator: added "Distortion model" selection and the "Gyroflow" calibration method, which fits all frames at once, handles fisheye lenses over 180° and skips frames that don't match the rest - Calibrator: changing the model or method no longer analyzes the video again - Camera data export: added iris (f/T-stop), focus distance, and gyro/accelerometer data in "One sample per frame". Fields missing in the file are greyed out - Added BRAW and R3D support on Windows ARM - Improved autosync with the DIS optical flow method (by @NiYien) - Added "GMFlow (AI)" optical flow for autosync and "GMFlow (AI dense flow)" tracking for "Analyze image optically": an AI model that follows fast motion and weak detail, but is much slower. It needs a GPU with Vulkan (Metal on Apple), and its model (about 30 MB) is downloaded the first time it's used. Not available on Android and iOS (by @yasumorishima) - Autosync's "PyrLK" optical flow now runs on the GPU - Added hardware encoding on Windows on ARM through Media Foundation, for Snapdragon and NVIDIA GPUs (by @cryptofyre) - The preview no longer freezes while the motion data is integrated and smoothed again, which takes seconds for long, high-rate logs - Rendering checks the output before it starts: it never writes over the video it's rendered from - Joining the files of one recording (split by the camera, e.g. at 4 GB) is more reliable: files with different timescales or edit lists keep the audio in sync, damaged or unfinished files are refused, an existing output is replaced only after asking, and a failed merge leaves no partial file - The frame rate of CinemaDNG image sequences is read from the files - DJI: the ISO, shutter speed and white balance of each frame are now read and shown in the video information, plus the aperture and focal length on the Avata 360 (by @spirokai) - Blackmagic RAW: ISO, shutter, iris and white balance are now shown in the video information - More accurate timing of DJI motion data in clips with dropped frames - Cooke /i lens metadata (.yml): added support for LockitPlus recordings, and improved the timing of their motion data - Many robustness fixes for damaged or unusual files: reading their metadata no longer crashes or takes huge amounts of memory - GPU memory is now released after rendering - "Apply selected settings to all items in the render queue" is now instant (by @TimoLehnertz) - Files added to the render queue together are now sorted by name - Added Serbian language - Updated translations  ### 📷 New supported cameras **Full support:** - Canon C50, C80, C400, EOS R5 Mark II, EOS R6 Mark III and EOS R6 V (MP4/MXF), including per-frame focal length, so no lens profile is needed and zooms are followed. RF lenses only for now (depends on Canon). Turn the lens image stabilizer (IS) off: Gyroflow can't stabilize clips recorded with it, and warns about them - SIGMA fp with the fpSup firmware mod (CinemaDNG) - Z CAM Mark II: E2-M4, E2-S6, E2-F6, E2-F8, E2-M4G, E2-S6G, E2N - XTRA Edge and Edge Pro - HOVERAir cameras - GoPro .LRV proxies (by @ioaur) and Insta360 .lrv proxies (by @OMG648)  **Motion data only** - Gyroflow can't play these files. Open a playable copy as the video and load the original in `Motion data -> Open file`: - Canon Cinema RAW Light (.crm) - Z CAM ZRAW (.zraw) - MotionCam Pro (.mcraw), including its lens calibration - DJI Osmo 360 (by @Errorist79) and DJI Avata 360 - Insta360 X5 (by @NeYoNmoos) and Insta360 Antigravity A1  **Playback only** - no motion data in the file, use an external gyro or Optical correction: - Nikon N-RAW (.nev), decoded with the RED SDK (downloaded on first use)  **Lens metadata only** - focal length, focus distance and iris, no motion data in the file: - ARRI ALEXA 35, ALEXA Mini, Mini LF, LF and 65 (.mxf, ARRIRAW .ari/.arx)  ### ⌨️ New shortcuts <sub>(Ctrl is Command on macOS)</sub> - Shift+Page Up, Shift+Page Down - previous/next keyframe (by @czukowski) - Ctrl+click on the previous/next frame buttons - move by 10 frames (by @czukowski) - Shift+click on the previous/next frame buttons - previous/next keyframe (by @czukowski) - Tracked points editor: Delete - remove selected, Ctrl+Z - undo, Ctrl+A - select all in the frame, Esc - clear selection  ### ⚙️ Behavior changes - Minimum macOS version is now 10.15 - New keyframes use the "Smooth" easing by default. Projects with "Smooth" keyframes load without any keyframes in 1.6.x - Lens breathing compensation is enabled by default for Sony files with breathing data - VQF integration is now used for all clips (clips shorter than 10 s used Complementary) - Autosync on a file without motion data now runs the optical analysis - Zooming clips may be framed slightly differently, because the recorded focal length is now interpolated between frames - "Static zoom": the zoom limit now lowers the smoothing only around the moments that don't fit, not in the whole clip. Projects from older versions keep the old behavior (`Stabilization -> Advanced -> Zoom limit lowers the smoothing of the whole clip`) - "Preserve other tracks" no longer forces .mov and audio copy. Audio uses the selected "Audio codec", and the output stays .mp4 unless that is "Copy" - Autosync uses the new "Optical analysis" offset method by default, also in the CLI. The previous default, rs-sync, is in `Synchronization -> Advanced -> Offset method`  ### 🐛 Bug fixes - Fixed the horizon slowly rotating with horizon lock on some cameras, e.g. Sony a7 IV (#1233) - Fixed VQF drift, which could make the result shakier than the original (#1090) - Fixed "Gyro bias" being applied to the accelerometer too - Fixed corners cut off by a circle when lowering lens correction on some wide lenses, e.g. DJI Osmo Nano (#1185) - Fixed automatic zoom with lens correction below 100% - Fixed SuperView/HyperView changing shape with the zoom level when lens correction is below 100% - Fixed the "black borders in view" timeline indicator missing small borders - Fixed the preview being zoomed out and shaky outside the trim ranges (by @TimoLehnertz) - Fixed the previous file's motion data being applied to a new video opened while it was still loading (by @TimoLehnertz) - Fixed loading a project overwriting keyframed horizon lock values (by @czukowski) - Fixed editing a keyframe sometimes changing another one less than 2 ms away (by @Arthur031221) - Fixed "Zoom limit iterations" from a project or preset not showing in the UI - Fixed "Edge repeat", "Edge mirror" and "Margin with feather" on vertical videos - Fixed asymmetrical lens profiles being mirrored in the OpenGL preview and STMap export - Fixed rotation of DNG and other image sequences with EXIF orientation - Fixed lens metadata of vertical BRAW files - Fixed horizontal bands in Sony exports on some Macs (#1255) - Fixed a crash with Sony files with incomplete IBIS data - Fixed corrupted H.264/HEVC exports with VAAPI on Linux (by @TimoLehnertz) - Fixed VAAPI export failing when decoding on the CPU (by @TimoLehnertz) - Fixed "Apply settings to render queue" not applying the output size (by @TimoLehnertz) - Fixed audio sync when exporting multiple trim ranges into one file - Fixed timestamps of tracks kept with "Preserve other tracks" - Fixed GPU processing of videos larger than 8192 px (#1083, by @latenitefilms) - Fixed the preview flickering to black while scrubbing on some GPUs (#1188) - Fixed Android exports not being seekable in other apps (#1088) - Fixed a crash on exit on Linux (#1047, by @jp39) - Fixed crashes in dynamic zoom and automatic sync point selection - Fixed the render queue hanging on unreadable files - Fixed lens profile export on iOS (#1177) - Fixed file dialogs not remembering the last folder - Fixed opening RDC folders in the App Store version - Fixed the timeline tooltip covering keyframes (by @czukowski) - Fixed IMU orientation and lens center of Insta360 X4 (by @Ivan1248) - Fixed some Z CAM files not being recognized (by @bang-imvt) - Fixed finding metadata in files larger than 5 GB - Fixed empty focus distance export for some cameras - Fixed "Export all samples" pairing gyro and accelerometer data with wrong timestamps - Fixed exported audio ending a few milliseconds before or after the video, heard as a short dropout at the end of trimmed clips (#1273) - Fixed autosync with the "OpenCV (PyrLK)" optical flow method, which found no sync data - Fixed out-of-memory errors ("device lost") on integrated GPUs, which took about 3 times more RAM than needed (by @anton-belkin) - Fixed hardware decoding with NVIDIA GPUs on Windows on ARM (by @cryptofyre) - Fixed rendering clips above 255 fps with a timecode to .mov/.mp4, which failed at the end of the render - Fixed the creation time of trimmed exports of files that store it without a time zone, e.g. HOVERAir - Fixed .mkv and .webm files in the CLI and when added to the render queue directly - Fixed Gyroflow offering to delete the original files after a merge that failed - Fixed projects not remembering "Load all metadata" - Fixed the lens calibration of Insta360 files recorded with a cropped sensor window (wrong lens center and focal length) - Fixed a crash when switching between files quickly  ### ⚙️ Plugins - **Vertical footage now works correctly on a vertical timeline** (same aspect ratio) - Sony, lens breathing and lens math improvements apply in the plugins too, as do "Output projection" and Static zoom's "Zoom level" from projects and presets (by @anton-belkin) - Added "Save as default" button, which saves the current smoothness, zoom limit, lens correction, horizon lock and FOV for newly loaded clips (by @Mldphotohraphie) - Added "Disable lens stretch (for plugins)" to the preset dialog, for when the editor does the anamorphic desqueeze (by @globalprojectscorp) - Premiere Pro and After Effects: duplicated, pasted, split and preset-applied effects now each have their own stabilization, an effect copied to another clip loads that clip's project and keeps the adjustments, and the effect follows relinked or moved media, also on another computer - Added Shotcut support (by @jp39) - Fixed an upside-down image when locking the horizon on vertical clips (by @Mldphotohraphie) - Fixed Premiere Pro subclips using the wrong part of the motion data (by @Talla) - Fixed After Effects not updating when keyframes are moved, eased or removed in the timeline, or after an undo - Fixed a crash in the beta versions of the Adobe apps - Fixed Premiere Pro exports on computers with several NVIDIA GPUs alternating between stabilized and unstabilized frames. A GPU in TCC mode or without a Vulkan driver is now stabilized through the CPU memory - Fixed Shotcut losing sync when clips are trimmed or split, and crashing during export (by @jp39) - Fixed gyro filters (like the low-pass filter) not being applied in the frei0r plugin (by @jp39) - OpenFX: files without motion data now show "No gyro data in this file". DaVinci Resolve no longer shows the fps mismatch status, as its timing is exact at any frame rate (by @Mldphotohraphie) - Fixed memory usage growing with every rendered frame - Fixed corrupted frames in DaVinci Resolve with CUDA on NVIDIA - Fixed a GPU memory leak with Vulkan/CUDA on Linux - Fixed Metal processing in Adobe plugins in some cases - Fixed the "Video editor plugins" section missing on some macOS versions (by @NiYien)  ### 💻 CLI - Added `--optical-correction`, `--ignore-file-motion`, `--optical-per-frame` and `--optical-tracking` - `audio_codec` now accepts `Copy` - Image sequences (PNG, JPEG, EXR, DNG) can be rendered: pass their first frame or the frames' pattern (`A001_%06d.dng`). `--image-sequence-fps` sets their frame rate, CinemaDNG files state their own - `--optical-tracking` accepts `gmflow` - Added exit codes: 0 - success, 1 - render failure, 2 - invalid arguments (by @blancas-armando) - `--help` now lists all `-p` output parameter keys and values (by @blancas-armando) - Fixed `--watch` picking up files that are still being copied, and one bad file stopping the queue (#1199)  ### 📦 SDK Updates: - FFmpeg is now 9.0 - BRAW SDK is now 6.0.0 - R3D SDK is now 9.2.1  Installing from the platform store is recommended for most users. Downloading zip or dmg shouldn't be needed anymore.  [Microsoft Store](https://apps.microsoft.com/store/detail/gyroflow/9NZG7T0JCG9H) | [macOS/iOS App Store](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/gyroflow/id6447994244) | [Google Play](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=xyz.gyroflow)
```

### 2026-11-04T14:42:57Z [**tw93/Pake**](https://github.com/tw93/Pake)

```
<div align="center"> <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tw93/Pake/main/assets/logo.png" alt="Pake Logo" width="120" height="120" style="border-radius:50%" /> <h1 style="margin: 12px 0 6px;">Pake V3.18.0</h1> <p><em>Turn any webpage into a desktop app with one command.</em></p> </div>  ### Changelog  1. Downloads on Windows now show the native WebView2 download panel with progress, and same-site downloads go through it too. 2. Dragging files onto a page to upload them now always works. `--enable-drag-drop` used to block those drops, and on Windows it also broke drag and drop inside pages; it is now accepted but ignored, so existing build commands keep working. 3. Downloaded files keep their real names, so a link to `Annual Report.pdf` or `报告.pdf` is no longer saved as `Annual%20Report.pdf` or `%E6%8A%A5%E5%91%8A.pdf`. 4. The tray menu follows the system language, with Simplified and Traditional Chinese labels. 5. After a failed build on Windows, the next build starts normally instead of stopping on a leftover compilation cache lock. 6. Apps built without `--icon` get the new flat mother-and-baby icon, the same one Pake for Mac and Windows uses. 7. Dependencies are updated for published RustSec and npm security advisories. 8. The docs now live at [pake.run](https://pake.run), with a step-by-step getting started tutorial and pages for shortcuts, scripts and AI agents.  ### 更新日志  1. Windows 上下载文件会显示 WebView2 自带的下载面板和进度，同一网站内的下载也走这个面板。 2. 把文件拖到网页上上传现在都能正常用了，以前加了 `--enable-drag-drop` 反而会拦下这些文件，Windows 上网页里的拖拽也会失效，这个参数现在保留但不再生效，已有的打包命令照常能用。 3. 下载的文件保留原来的名字，`Annual Report.pdf` 或 `报告.pdf` 这样的链接不会再存成 `Annual%20Report.pdf`、`%E6%8A%A5%E5%91%8A.pdf`。 4. 托盘菜单跟随系统语言，中文系统上显示简体或繁体中文。 5. Windows 上构建失败后，下一次构建能正常开始，不会再被残留的编译缓存锁挡住。 6. 没有指定 `--icon` 时，应用默认用新的扁平母婴图标，和 Pake 桌面版用的是同一个。 7. 依赖已按公开的 RustSec 和 npm 安全公告更新。 8. 文档搬到了 [pake.run](https://pake.run)，新增一步步的入门教程，以及快捷键、脚本和 AI Agent 相关的页面。  Special thanks to @WavesMan, @NgoQuocViet2001, and @jakero for the pull requests behind this release. If Pake helps you, please consider giving it a star and recommending it to your friends.  > https://github.com/tw93/Pake 
```

### 2026-11-06T16:04:32Z [**nxtrace/NTrace-core**](https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-core)

```
## What's Changed  * 修复 iTerm2 选字时 MTR TUI 旧画面堆叠的问题：实时与回放统一按行更新变化内容，避免连续刷新写入滚动历史，并改善窗口缩放、残行清理和 Unicode 显示 by @tsosunchia in https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/257 * 修复 MTR 探测错误直接写入 stderr 导致交互画面错乱的问题：错误统一显示在 TUI 标题区，暂停时也能更新；收到成功响应或重置统计后清除提示 * 修复 Windows ARM64 默认 IPv6 探测报错：ICMP、TCP、UDP 在 Traffic Class 为零时跳过系统不支持的 socket 设置，解决 `set IPv6 Traffic Class 0: not implemented on windows/arm64` by @KexyBiscuit in https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/260 * 升级至 Go 1.27.3，正式发布、CI 和性能脚本统一使用 Go 默认 JSON 后端，移除 `GOEXPERIMENT=nojsonv2` 固定配置；继续使用 `encoding/json` v1 API * 改进 CI 专项验收调度：按改动路径选择检查，手动运行或无法读取差异时执行完整验收，保留基础测试、race 和跨平台构建 by @tsosunchia in https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-dev/pull/258 * 更新依赖：`go-runewidth v0.0.31`、MCP Go SDK `v1.8.0`、`quic-go v0.64.1`、`table v1.4.1`、`gjson v1.20.0`、`x/net v0.61.0` 等  额外的：   MTR 实时与回放画面按终端宽高显示，窗口较小时可放大查看更多跳；保存报告和会话录制仍保留完整统计。   Windows ARM64 的修复仅跳过零值 Traffic Class 设置，不代表支持非零 `--tos`；非零设置失败时仍会返回错误。   源码构建需要 Go 1.27.3+。对齐正式发布时使用默认 JSON 后端，无需再设置 `GOEXPERIMENT=nojsonv2`；macOS 最低支持版本仍为 13.0。    **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/nxtrace/NTrace-core/compare/v1.7.5...v1.7.6 
```

### 2026-11-09T01:48:50Z [**huginn/huginn**](https://github.com/huginn/huginn)

```
This security release fixes server environment disclosure through JqAgent and bypasses of the enforced outbound proxy.  Upgrade installations that enable JqAgent or rely on `OUTBOUND_PROXY` to restrict users' outbound requests.  ## Security fixes  - JqAgent now starts jq with an empty environment.  User filters can no longer read server environment variables through `env` or `$ENV`.  This affects installations with jq support enabled through `USE_JQ`.  [GHSA-3f56-748g-q34j](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/security/advisories/GHSA-3f56-748g-q34j) - Enforce `OUTBOUND_PROXY` for HTTParty, open-uri and directly built Net::HTTP connections, including JiraAgent and WeiboPublishAgent.  These paths previously bypassed the configured proxy, allowing users to reach destinations it was intended to block.  The fix also covers related paths in StubhubAgent and default Scenario imports.  [GHSA-98rv-h5p4-9rgr](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/security/advisories/GHSA-98rv-h5p4-9rgr)  Thanks to [@akash111-hue](https://github.com/akash111-hue) for reporting both issues.  ## Upgrade notes  No database migration is required for these fixes.  Update to `v2026.11.09` and restart Huginn's processes, or pull and recreate containers using the official release images.  If `USE_JQ` was enabled while untrusted users had accounts, treat secrets supplied through Huginn's environment as potentially exposed.  Rotate `APP_SECRET_TOKEN` and any affected database, SMTP, OAuth or other credentials.  Rotating `APP_SECRET_TOKEN` invalidates existing sessions.  JqAgent filters using `env` or `$ENV` now receive an empty object.  Pass intended inputs through the Agent's `variables` option instead.  Requests that previously bypassed `OUTBOUND_PROXY` may now fail under its destination policy.  Use an `http://` proxy URL for compatibility with open-uri clients; HTTPS destinations still use a CONNECT tunnel.  Normal routing is preserved when `OUTBOUND_PROXY` is unset.  See the [upgrade guide](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/blob/v2026.11.09/UPGRADING.md#jqagent-environment-isolation-and-outbound-proxy-enforcement) and [outbound request configuration](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/blob/v2026.11.09/doc/manual/outbound-requests.md).  This release also refreshes Ruby dependencies.  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/huginn/huginn/compare/v2026.11.05.2...v2026.11.09 
```

### 2026-11-04T17:19:02Z [**fatedier/frp**](https://github.com/fatedier/frp)

```
## Features  * frpc can verify server certificates using system CAs, optionally combined with CAs from `trustedCaFile`. * `frpc reload` now refreshes certificate files for unchanged `https2http` and `https2https` proxies without interrupting existing connections. Failed refreshes keep the previous certificate and report the error in proxy status and logs while reload still succeeds. * Added `metricsServer` to both frps and frpc, a separate listener for health checks, metrics and optional profiling that does not expose the dashboard or the admin API. It is disabled unless `metricsServer.port` is set, defaults to `127.0.0.1`, and has its own credentials and TLS. Setting the port serves `/metrics` on both: frps exposes runtime and business metrics, so `enablePrometheus` is not needed there, and frpc exposes Go runtime metrics only, without linking an extra metrics library.    The `/healthz`, `/metrics` and pprof endpoints on `webServer`, `webServer.pprofEnable` and `enablePrometheus` still work but are deprecated: configure `metricsServer` instead. They will continue to work until v0.80.0 is released, and may be removed in v0.80.0 or later.  ## Fixes  * Fixed proxy and visitor configuration changes being lost when reloading frpc during reconnection. * Updated yamux to fix a 100% CPU busy loop after a session shuts down and a session close that could hang when the underlying connection (for example WebSocket) blocks on a write, which could also delay reconnection. * frps now closes the multiplexed session of a replaced or failed control connection immediately, releasing its streams and work connections instead of keeping them until keepalive times out. * Fixed a data race when frpc updates log prefixes while other goroutines are logging, which could crash the process. * Fixed HTTPS and tcpmux route listeners leaving a pending connection open when the listener is removed during connection handoff; the connection is now closed instead of leaving the client to wait for a read timeout. 
```
